<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Gg</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

	box-sizing: border-box;

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;

body {

}

}

}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */

	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;

}

}

	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {

	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {

	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

}

}

}

@media only print {

}

@page {

.checkbox {
	display: inline-flex;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;

	background-size: 16px;

	margin-right: 5px;

.checkbox-on {
	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");
}

</style></head><body><article id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-9314-f4c932b1aa60" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Gg</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-bc6b-d31ea6ad4d72" class="">Full dictionary (minimal closed model) — State, Derived, Controls, Events, Invariants, Thresholds, Failure Modes</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8354-fc22f353c40e" class="">All variables normalized to . 
Higher = more of the named property unless noted.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-9a66-da49b97ea286"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-bfa4-eab02461e0b7" class="">A) Core State (15 variables)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-8caa-e8e71aace9f3" class="">1) SS — Somatic Safety</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-b9ee-e83654c27964" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> body-level sense of safety / settle capacity.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-a7fc-d8cd1a4296ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> sleep stability, HRV trend, appetite stability, startle rate (normalized), “can relax” self-report.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b16e-c527c77d2e11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update (conceptual):</strong> increases with CO, PR, TR; 
decreases with SH, OP, EC.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b037-d34dbf0ad83e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Failure mode:</strong> SS low enables AN spike and SH consolidation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-ba7c-d6d8e607894c" class="">2) TR — Trust (action-validated)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b77f-e6be420b16ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> expectation that the other’s actions match commitments under cost.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-aa98-f1f91e83ccd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> kept promises, consistency across contexts, disclosure accuracy, contradiction count.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-b990-e93307c6d4e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update rule:</strong> TR increases only via <strong>verified actions</strong> and transparency; 
words-only cannot raise TR.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-9358-d10fbb9042b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Failure mode:</strong> low TR raises AV/COLD and exit probability.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-900e-f935faa237f7" class="">3) PR — Protection of Dignity (new partner)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-adc9-d23edf042863" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> willingness to bear social cost to protect your position and dignity.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-80d5-d49fb61fcb9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> public defense, refusal to downgrade you, clear prioritization under conflict, cost paid.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-b86b-e0a61d8566f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> rises with PAY_COST + correct public designation; 
falls with mislabel and ex-shielding.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9bf1-e6569fe621f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Failure mode:</strong> PR deficit drives SH and SE (self-erasure).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9ca7-c2b16fb74483" class="">4) CL — Clarity (relationship structure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-a18e-c1ba377e9c2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> unambiguous definition of role/status/expectations.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-87a7-d7d285ce1746" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> explicit status statements + consistent behavior + aligned labels.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8540-d2916c6c9e8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> increases with explicit decisions + consistent naming; decreases with ambiguity patterns.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-89a5-f6c223f3134b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Note:</strong> CL is not “words”; it is <strong>structure</strong>.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-a2df-c238db420725" class="">5) OP — Opacity / Hiddenness</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-a907-f8c349c8cd9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> how much is kept in the dark; 
information asymmetry.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-97bc-c31e566a44b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> hidden contacts, undisclosed meetings, unexplained absences, “I can’t ask” rate.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-a61e-d213cf0d52dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> increases with hiding and label mismatch; decreases with proactive disclosure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-abda-f9a21477a771" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Failure mode:</strong> OP&gt;0.6 caps TR growth.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-baad-ff496b949565" class="">6) SH — Shame / Worth Collapse</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-b99a-c6880172406b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> internal collapse: “I’m not worthy / I’m minimized.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-b27b-e53c3f0a5b92" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> self-worth drop language, humiliation response, shutdown after downgrade.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-abe8-d5a3df168656" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> increases with PR/CL_public breach and OP; 
decreases with repair + protection.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-8994-c8a20ba107f0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Failure mode:</strong> SH high forces coldness and exit.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-aa83-fb9c9d2a929f" class="">7) AN — Alarm / Anxiety Activation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-86c7-f5b8329b2bed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> nervous system alarm activation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8e20-fa82386b84ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> rumination, urgency, reassurance seeking, hypervigilance.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8a2d-c1d6400db9ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> rises when SS low and OP high; decreases with CO and restored clarity/protection.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-8da8-f21669d2540b" class="">8) AV — Avoidance / Detachment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-9bab-f5ea20edb1b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> distancing response to preserve stability.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-af3d-f188dce9b1d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> withdrawal days, response latency, numbness, reduced attachment bids.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8136-dcc431cd05c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> rises when TR low and SH high; 
decreases when TR and PR stabilized.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-87be-fedf50e93592" class="">9) RJ — Repair Capacity (dyad repair reliability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-af41-e74626a36008" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> ability to repair ruptures with change.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-abe6-d98884a5b227" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> apology specificity, non-repeat, corrective action, time-to-repair.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-b29b-fa38f5f0d4e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> rises with repair-with-cost; 
falls with apology-only repetition.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-8091-cdb0d35d093d" class="">10) CO — Co-regulation Quality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-9a01-cd7f0ddec40f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> ability to settle together via attunement and presence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a321-f48c45ec27ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> presence when asked, attunement accuracy, settle time, touch/comfort adequacy (if relevant).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-8a1d-dc44528a18dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> can rise with behavior, but cannot substitute for PR/CL.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-baa8-d6eba1218ffc" class="">11) IM — Image Pressure (face optimization intensity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a200-c3612d299bb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> priority placed on reputation/face management.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b4de-d0cdb153b950" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> avoidance of public truth, narrative protection, fear of questions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-90e5-c21dd2d0fc1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Update:</strong> environment-driven; 
increases in high scrutiny contexts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-bd73-db4ccc515de6" class="">12) PD — Power Distance Pressure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-82cb-db29312fc136" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> hierarchy pressure suppressing truth and clarity.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-82d2-efc27b40003d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> “can’t say,” family authority dominance, fear of elders/social sanctions.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-80c9-f90dffad64c0" class="">13) TF — Social Trust Fabric</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-97a2-c03b25a8b4b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> baseline trust in environment/contracts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a98a-d2b8864af150" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> reliability of social agreements, scam exposure, enforcement consistency.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-bd64-eecae4e27073" class="">14) LI — Integrity Norm Strength</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-954c-ef8e1406b500" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> strength of accountability norms in that environment/actor.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-8b60-dfe5ff7aab96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> admission of fault, follow-through, 
consistent ethics under cost.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8387-d8f600feeade" class="">15) MS — Market Pressure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-ae88-e0fe9dca420e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Meaning:</strong> economic/competition stress driving concealment/extraction.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-977a-d1b1b6c338c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Signals:</strong> cost stress, income volatility, time scarcity, hustle intensity.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b2c6-c8465008d439"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-8bdf-eac7330ee0d7" class="">B) Controls (6 inputs)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-9c7a-f950a8a3f4f3" class="">u^PR — Protection action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-8a23-d36fa0fe6d90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> actions that visibly protect your dignity (public defense, priority choices, cost acceptance).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-93ef-c39063f72636" class="">u^CL — Clarity action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-82e2-e4f6c2cffd27" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> explicit structural decisions (status declaration, alignment of labels, 
boundaries).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-abaa-ef49fb58d435" class="">u^OP — Transparency action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-88c3-f11f9ab1ea0f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> disclose sensitive interactions proactively; remove hiddenness.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-ae08-d675f9d9da95" class="">u^RJ — Repair action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-be86-c5647889d19f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> apology + specific change + cost + non-repeat + timeline.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a874-d3f2c874a505" class="">u^CO — Co-regulation action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-ab61-fce2a2bd0c57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> attuned presence, physical proximity when requested, settling behaviors.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-808a-ed4ea1eb0db9" class="">u^TR — Trust-building action</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8c1b-f760ebfd5d44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Definition:</strong> repeated verified follow-through; tracking + consistency across contexts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a5ab-c5bf17847252" class=""><strong>Control constraints:</strong> each . 
<strong>Rate limit:</strong> avoid large step changes if confidence is low.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-be00-f91232b94d4c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8a85-fb774ae3a7da" class="">C) Derived Variables (computed; not part of core state)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a0ee-cb9e540d297e" class="">CL_public — Public clarity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-bfb0-f99a5b988e2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CL_{public}=\sigma(CL-\kappa IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9aea-d2caadecccf5" class="">LM — Label mismatch</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-8449-c4cac546888d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
LM=\sigma(|CL_{public}-CL_{private}|)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-b6f4-fd2d93f4ed26" class="">EX — Extraction pressure (derived)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b058-d98835a0626b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EX=\sigma(\alpha_1(1-PR)+\alpha_2 OP+\alpha_3 MS+\alpha_4 IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-95b6-e84a2da54b56" class="">COLD — Coldness lock</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b341-fde99e5b67d0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
COLD=\sigma(AV+SH+(1-TR))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-95a9-e2f80441ac92" class="">SE — Self-erasure tax</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-80b3-ced124b0d238" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
SE=\sigma((1-PR)+(1-CL_{public})+OP)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9c87-f56e1e3bd50c" class="">S — Stability scalar</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b790-e95259e4d6a7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S=SS+TR+PR+CL-OP-SH</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a6fe-c296c061c334" class="">Risk — Composite risk</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-9210-f6226412b801" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk=\sigma(OP+LM+EX+(1-PR)+(1-CL_{public}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a8a1-f83bbc877423"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8b39-f2ae2fd1313f" class="">D) Events (ingest layer)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-b73b-f810b3138cc5" class="">Each event has:<br/><code>{type, actor, context, publicness, cost, verifiability v, timestamp}</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-a914-cb15dcaa7427" class="">Event types (minimum set)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-bef0-faf883433069" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>NAME_PUBLIC</strong> — correct public designation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-ae24-dc8077386ba7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>MISLABEL</strong> — downgrade label (e.g., “colleague”)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9ccd-f2bdd0790214" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>DISCLOSE_SENSITIVE</strong> — proactive disclosure (ex contact, meetings, 
etc.)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-ba36-c225cb10075f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>HIDDEN_CONTACT</strong> — discovered/undisclosed sensitive interaction</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-852e-df69fde46d79" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>PAY_COST</strong> — pays social/family/reputation cost for you</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-9e04-df72f57050b5" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>GIFT_ONLY</strong> — care/gift without structural protection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9b52-d08f5bb3f16c" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>REPAIR_WITH_CHANGE</strong> — repair with specific change + evidence</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b4f9-f9095f922010" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>APOLOGY_ONLY</strong> — apology without change</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9dd7-fe86acde8bed" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>INTEGRATION</strong> — introduces to family/friends as partner (context-stamped)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-bbcd-fde3bd94f77d" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>DEFEND_EX_OVER_YOU</strong> — protects ex narrative at your expense</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-971d-f50095eb7d03" class="">Evidence weighting (verifiability v)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-8980-d3d12d35bc54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">words-only:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-860d-d209e2459883" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">private observable (messages + consistent actions):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-a334-fd3c0c38e6c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">public / third-party observable (intros, posts, witnesses):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-aa74-c6ace5653ccd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-85f0-e8d9892a6435" class="">E) Update rules (event → state deltas)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-977b-e037a9d62d74" class="">Deltas scale by verifiability . 
Example patterns:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a03c-e953b89cbc50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>NAME_PUBLIC (correct):</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-963d-d6c7ca94882f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>MISLABEL:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-9d4b-d17067a8b617" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DISCLOSE_SENSITIVE:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-832f-e6622fed1ed3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>HIDDEN_CONTACT:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-ad99-d10c4246af5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>PAY_COST:</strong> (large only if cost is real/observable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b791-fa3e1c6abad3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>GIFT_ONLY:</strong> (small); 
<strong>no PR/CL increase</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a476-c6a56599455c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>REPAIR_WITH_CHANGE:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a25c-cb13ee67522b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>APOLOGY_ONLY (repeated):</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-87cc-eef823eed135" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>INTEGRATION:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-89c5-d7c6f1bce8eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DEFEND_EX_OVER_YOU:</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a9d9-e7bc9839905f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-a777-f8ac2cdadc97" class="">F) Invariants (non-negotiable constraints)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a445-c36ef91bfb8a" class="">I1 — No-substitution</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b666-c84134f8d80c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CO cannot compensate for PR/CL deficits.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-82d2-c900d0dd3174" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If or : trust gain from CO is blocked.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a44a-ecb796a51fdb" class="">I2 — Words-do-not-raise-TR</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-ab98-dea683764d8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TR increases only from verified actions/events.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b37b-f0a975767a4d" class="">I3 — Opacity ceiling for trust</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-b0c6-e36f3588ecc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If , then .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-a1d1-d27e88645ec0" class="">I4 — Label mismatch cap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-815e-f5c09bad1d5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If , PR and “chosen” score capped until corrected publicly.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-b488-d96c7b712808" class="">I5 — Ex-contact transparency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-8863-fb5db2fad087" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ex-sensitive interactions must be disclosed proactively; 
hiding triggers hard penalty.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b3ca-ebce68ad62a1" class="">I6 — Cross-context consistency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-a3ef-c73754971730" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Chosen/priority” requires consistent naming and protection across contexts (public/family minimum).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a318-f271cb44deb4" class="">I7 — Self-erasure red line</h3></div><div trigger).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b057-c7bfafdfc69c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-9e33-dbeda78e1de9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bounded zone:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b3d0-f7b6b582a62e" class="">Safety</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-be9d-fb05278ea112" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Acute alarm: and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-8c56-d18f461a4c9f" 
lass="">Risk</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-9cc2-d98243900687" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High risk:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-950e-e82a5f8395f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Recovery gate: sustained 3 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-8487-e926c657f0ed" class="">Dignity floors (sensitive profile)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-b98c-dc6a30c30bd0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-ac08-fb68e3ad9344" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-b728-d811db7e0be2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-9545-ea8b10cf9c25"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-ac1f-e6f189fcaafb" class="">H) Failure Modes (repeatable patterns)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-9d42-d19f52f419a0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Dignity failure mode:</strong> + ⇒ ⇒ ⇒ exit</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-91da-c9cc1555d16a" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Face/hiddenness attractor:</strong> ⇒ ⇒ ⇒ ⇒</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-b937-c94c951e77ee" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Repair failure:</strong> apology-only repetition ⇒ , 
persists</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a5d6-f921de8f5572" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Limbo trap:</strong> stuck in bounded band with flat PR/CL ⇒ cumulative SE tax</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-84c8-f775368826bd" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>False compliance:</strong> logged claims without telemetry/evidence change ⇒ TR collapses fast</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-8db0-c61b5c972520"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-92f7-d5a1eefe66d7" class="">I) Terminal classifier (Valid / Bounded / Invalid)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b95e-fa5ad2b7f55a" class="">Valid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-a476-e43c16569203" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">, , , , sustained (≥2 windows) and no false compliance.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-b675-d3493e5cb309" class="">Bounded</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-928a-fa57be19b45e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mixed signals or insufficient observability; no worsening trend; 
constraints partially met.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-9261-f471ddad1a4c" class="">Invalid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9b02-fcee780ff6fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repeated OP/LM + dignity breach + verification failures (or sustained SE&gt;0.7).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-9f6a-e755629b02ae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-866b-fa5d32482637" class="">If you want the dictionary extended to include <strong>every derived variable and every event subtype</strong> (e.g., 50+ event types and per-context state variants), specify whether you want:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-92a2-cd66aa239317" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>“relationship runtime” scope only, or</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-800c-e33089a52e8b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>full Vietnam macro-environment decoder scope (HC/PD/IM expanded).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-bdc3-d1caee9e5fa7" class="">A. 
Loop template coverage gaps</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-acb5-c7e581748679" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Negative feedback (balancing) archetypes</strong> beyond simple damping</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-8953-e2a968b99fa2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Goal-seeking control (PID variants)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a8d1-ca72e89d8930" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Overshoot with integral windup</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-824b-eaee5e7d6901" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Balancing with delayed sensing (oscillation class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a647-d20874dd4c62" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Hysteresis / path-dependence templates</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a9db-c767741441bd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Different thresholds for enter vs exit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-ae5c-ec3806c62de0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lock-in that persists after driver removed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-86b5-efedab805487" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regime “stickiness” (state-dependent parameters)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-90bc-e2807981133a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Regime-switching / state-dependent dynamics</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-8a31-d515659f3858" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Markov switching (low-vol/high-vol regimes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-8c57-f12d6c923326" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Piecewise parameter sets by stage (S1–S4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-828a-f64dcd0cb09b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Triggered policy response (if-then dynamics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-917e-d2c4827a7f51" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Multi-timescale templates</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-81af-edfdeff757cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fast loop riding on slow loop (microstructure vs macro)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-810e-dd396baa115e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Slow accumulation + fast release (fragility buildup → crash)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-937e-ca60796c0d40" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Structural breaks and re-baselining</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-816a-c1f87f1fdd54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reset events (bankruptcy, war, 
reform)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-b499-c6131241298e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Re-initialization rules after collapse</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9531-ffc5b98e4352" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Permanent parameter shifts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-868f-d5c2679dc2ce" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Network / graph-based diffusion loops</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-90d1-f3c77d10c4f9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Contagion on graphs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-aee7-d508122e0cde" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cascades via degree distribution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-aaa2-eb13afd3dac9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Percolation thresholds</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-a5ee-d9e58b0f09a9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Queueing / congestion loops</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a471-cb57b6981ba5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bottleneck accumulation (ports, support tickets,

id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a34d-c987a7195dd8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deterministic run_id + artifact h
ashes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8593-c4c8b767f0a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">replay support</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-b611-ef9187fbb0af" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Termination classification for loops</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a516-e202341554b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Valid (equations executable + evidence sufficient)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8f9e-f377605cf157" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bounded (missing data or unmodeled format)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-81cc-d71514abc2a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Invalid (contradictory definitions or unstable units)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-a495-f225a729a085"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bd7e-d370c52ffe7a" class="">F. 
Safety and compliance boundaries (especially for markets)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8880-ee6108ade2df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Explicit non-execution boundary</strong> for trading</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-b832-d04377686099" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">analysis, scenario, risk, constraints allowed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b280-f0ee140d2247" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no direct “buy/sell now”, 
no personalized allocation commands</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-89f4-ef8d9eb2ae63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“bounded compliance” policy encoded in kernel gates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b381-e1734c3e04aa" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Market engine components that are safe</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-9579-e8a4df0c6bef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">regime detection</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a426-c959476ac6fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">volatility and liquidity diagnostics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-a1ed-ca3a712d331d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stress tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9eb0-f5b59a9a9bef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">scenario generation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-932d-e2f0ce493781" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">execution-agnostic signals (not trade instructions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-af6f-c2792567db5b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-b0b7-eb0359a77962" class="">G. 
UI/UX + productization gaps</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8982-f05060d93a0f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Canonical outputs</strong> (one format)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-8320-da4d28916176" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">loop cards (stage, drivers, constraints, triggers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a73d-fac0f9f571ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">heatmaps (clusters, stage distribution)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9356-e9d51d12e9c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">timeline of stage transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-bdff-f910a022518c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">top fragility contributors</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-ab77-c0b19a8a6b97" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Interaction design</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-942c-caa9b9a0ade2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">search by loop_id / alias</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-b429-d1c38ccb94bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">filter by stage, domain, 
confidence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a6db-cfbc21a8c6af" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">drill-down evidence view (audit trail)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-aeb4-e1f1f031584c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Portal content build</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-bcf3-e764841ae68b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">static pages from JSON artifacts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-aee3-c605a8c6ce75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">search_index.json build rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-9642-c72cbdc189c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">link integrity checker</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-9b1e-dd3094a6cb06" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Product packaging</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a043-e3f47814e81e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Loop Library” as a product artifact</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-9ac5-f8fe567bf3d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Stage Scanner” as a product artifact</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8132-cda6ac9d533d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Scenario Engine” as a product artifact</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b14c-eaab5714a09c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-b05a-d9e37e9c78aa" class="">H. 
What’s missing specifically from your request thread</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9851-eee4b303319c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Equations for “all loops”</strong> in a consistent template-per-loop artifact</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-88ea-e566d2023c51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">right now: templates exist, but not instantiated per loop ID</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-861c-f5b5282412da" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Mapping “all loops by stage”</strong> using the rubric</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-9ed8-f269927e4e17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">not yet produced for the 250 list</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-a97c-f4d412cbf090" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>“Scan for true C7” operationalization</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-af92-ebbff8fe1675" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">you need a deterministic rule-set for “C-layer attribution” (C1–C7) with evidence gates, not just interpretation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b654-d2fa7ff33410" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cycle-stage (birth→decay) mapping for C1–C7 loops</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-9c03-c2db956cc347" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stage assignment should be computed per loop with g/F/X/H/R/S/Δ, 
then aggregated by C-layer</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-927f-fc9f27b4a267"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-a964-e49a34c8307c" class="">If you want the next deliverable to close the biggest gap: I can output a <strong>single canonical JSON schema + deterministic scoring/spec</strong> that turns every loop into a machine object (<code>loop.json</code>) and makes “stage mapping + evidence links + dedupe + consolidation” executable inside AMOS.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-90d3-e9545be4e0ce" class="">2) 25 Master Equations (Civilization as a controllable dynamical system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8778-ee4575a75aac" class="">1) Core stability inequality</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-a2d0-f57b6b763113" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Stability(t) \;\Longleftrightarrow\; D_{eff}(t)+Buf_{eff}(t)+Align_{eff}(t)\;&gt;\;G_{eff}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-ab3c-cf6863252258" class="">2) Effective gain / damping / buffer / alignment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-8f68-fe2fcdab5f22" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{eff}=\sum_d w_d\,M_{d,G},\quad D_{eff}=\sum_d w_d\,M_{d,D}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-947a-e232ed70cc95" class="">Buf_{eff}=\sum_d w_d,M_{d,Buf},\quad Align_{eff}=\sum_d w_d,M_{d,Align}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-b3b8-cfefd4ca1584" class="">3) Cascade risk (connectivity-squared law)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-ae1f-f97165ba4a26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk_{cascade}\propto \kappa^2\cdot\frac{G_{eff}}{D_{eff}+Buf_{eff}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-9131-ef06ffae4284" class="">\kappa=\sum_{i\neq j}\lVert W_{i\to j}\rVert<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-ab33-c2c1f369ca61" class="">4) Shock sensitivity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8014-e2aeb39b49b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Stability}{\partial Shock}\;\propto\;\frac{1}{Red_{eff}\cdot Buf_{eff}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b4cb-c8b7a768b9ee" class="">5) Maintenance debt accumulation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-bacf-c96816090f2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_d(t+1)=H_d(t)+\alpha_d\,Load_d(t)-\beta_d\,Repair_d(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-82c5-d56a18130200" class="">6) Debt attacks damping/buffer first</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b384-c94e025ec79d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{eff}(t+1)=D_{eff}(t)-\lambda_D\sum_d H_d(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b6b7-c0296682ab99" class="">Buf_{eff}(t+1)=Buf_{eff}(t)-\lambda_B\sum_d H_d(t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-aaf5-ef9260192509" class="">7) Expansion illusion condition (surface growth, core rot)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-82bf-ee33e133eadc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Output_{surface}\uparrow \;\wedge\; \sum_d H_d\uparrow \;\Rightarrow\; Fragility\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bef5-c212e91f65ce" class="">8) Goodhart collapse</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9b80-c622e91c5347" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Proxy \to Target \Rightarrow Signal_{reality}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-bafe-db58a80292b2" class="">Truth_{bandwidth} ;=; \frac{Signal}{Signal+Noise}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-9855-c054789afee3" class="">9) Information gain creates social gain</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-8b10-c9236e983b03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Inf:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Cul:G\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-bd81-dd3b11b94ad3" class="">10) Social gain erodes rule damping</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b180-d776c27c8d7f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cul:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Law:D\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-bdf2-c04a2e96a4b2" class="">11) Selective enforcement (false stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-9698-ebbb68aeabce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Law:D_{surface}\uparrow \;\wedge\; Law:Align\downarrow \Rightarrow Trust\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b542-d0e18a5e2431" class="">12) Trust as transaction friction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9ada-e88eb45c407b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Cost_{transaction}\propto \frac{1}{Trust}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-91ed-cb5977e1da8f" class="">13) Alignment drives real legitimacy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-82fa-ddd795c4c52b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Legitimacy_{real}\propto Align_{eff}\cdot Trans_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-bfc0-df51b82c5371" class="">14) Symbolic legitimacy substitution (late C6)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-bc61-f3d12337b248" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Legitimacy_{real}\downarrow \Rightarrow Legitimacy_{symbolic}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8629-fc52b07ffddc" class="">15) Demographic capacity constraint</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-828b-cdfd6f1d77d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
BioCapacity = Health \times Fertility \times CognitiveFunction</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8848-e1fe6b3dc36a" class="">BioCapacity\downarrow \Rightarrow LongRunGrowth\downarrow<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-9b8c-efd1754651b9" class="">16) Competence density vs system complexity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-b61c-cb3a97bf5d20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
If\;\;Competence\_density &lt; Complexity \Rightarrow Failure\_rate\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-88bd-c6181110622c" class="">17) Credential inflation (competence divergence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-be2a-c24f66744d25" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Credential\_rate\uparrow \;\wedge\; Skill\_rate\not\uparrow \Rightarrow Competence\_density\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-81b7-d8bcdc3e94fa" class="">18) Energy redundancy as macro buffer</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-95d3-c4be04b14414" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Buf_{eff}\approx f(En:Red,Fin:Buf)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-9d30-ff3431eb90a9" class="">19) Finance leverage amplifies fragility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-83f7-f0d54d212613" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Fin:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Buf_{Fin}\downarrow \Rightarrow Risk_{cascade}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-ad58-ceb769b2e36f" class="">20) Recovery slope (fatigue detection)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-93db-e00d8ead8621" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d\,RecoveryTime}{d\,ShockCount}&gt;0 \Rightarrow Late\;C6</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-866a-dd59a870b3e4" class="">21) Latency mismatch (governance failure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-9020-e1f55777d98f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Latency_{acknowledge} \gg Latency_{failure} \Rightarrow Drift\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-b29e-fede6af2bf3c" class="">22) Elite exit is internal belief collapse</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-b80f-d9e8cffeb9ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CapitalFlight_{elite}\uparrow \Rightarrow Align\downarrow \Rightarrow Trust\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-8099-ce2e179c9079" class="">23) AI as gain amplifier (default)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-a5c4-e3e87ad5d653" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AI\uparrow \Rightarrow Inf:G\uparrow,\;\;Noise\uparrow,\;\;VerificationCost\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-96e7-c9311cfb0466" class="">24) AI reduces human time-horizon unless counter-damped</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-8eab-f119ecefe868" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AI\uparrow \Rightarrow Hor_{eff}\downarrow \;\;(\text{unless } Law/Fin enforce long-horizon incentives)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-871e-c7cd9b4c30ea" class="">25) True C7 entry condition (multi-axis phase lock)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-84e4-cd24fb53516a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta Trans_{Law}&gt;0 \;\wedge\; \Delta Hor_{Fin}&gt;0 \;\wedge\; \Delta Red_{En}&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-9ec8-c5b99f75f5c4" class="">\Rightarrow; system;can;rotate;from;C6\to C7<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-b564-cb39beac465e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9dda-c06ce9dbddaa" class="">4) Unified Field Model: Micro (Cell) ↔ Macro (Civilization) “Resonance” Architecture</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a85d-c65c7ef9c323" class="">A) Three-layer state vector</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b47e-ec43bf883b78" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b0bb-d86faf5bc534" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cell/Body layer</strong>: (metabolism, sleep, inflammation, autonomic tone)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8b4e-f0411cfc7fb3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Mind/Group layer</strong>: (attention, trust, aggression, coherence)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-990d-cc25540aab46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>System layer</strong>: (energy, finance, law, info, buffers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-84d6-d154673d567c" class="">Coupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-a2a9-e1e38e6dbffd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\begin{bmatrix}
x_\mu\\x_\psi\\x_M
\end{bmatrix}_{t+1}
=
\begin{bmatrix}
x_\mu\\x_\psi\\x_M
\end{bmatrix}_{t}
+
\begin{bmatrix}
F_\mu(x_\mu,x_\psi,x_M)\\
F_\psi(x_\mu,x_\psi,x_M)\\
F_M(x_\mu,x_\psi,x_M)
\end{bmatrix}
+
\begin{bmatrix}
s_\mu\\s_\psi\\s_M
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-8b4b-e20acc9d0e0c" class="">B) Bottom-up: biology drives politics through bandwidth</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-a5ef-ca3b25fce256" class="">If sleep and metabolic stability fall:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-bddd-e60351dd9e78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sleep\downarrow \Rightarrow ExecutiveFunction\downarrow \Rightarrow Impulse\_control\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-bdfe-cc9abebff974" class="">Aggregate effect:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-8849-c67f85ad7a68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
MeanExecutiveFunction\downarrow \Rightarrow Cul:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Inf:G\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8e97-c3a02f5be364" class="">This is a <strong>biological gain amplifier</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9c5f-d5ae9ca8dfed" class="">C) Top-down: institutions drive biology via environment</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-a447-fb29949254d2" class="">Institutions shape:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-acb0-ddb6fd847361" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise baseline</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-9493-d3c4a38c0f87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">commute stress</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-bd13-de4cb509326c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">housing insecurity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-9a7c-c5cd500b6394" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">food quality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b9ba-cea4cd333c72" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">healthcare access</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-990c-d773e63f1538" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-bec5-e535327e1188" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Law:Align\downarrow \Rightarrow InequalityStress\uparrow \Rightarrow Inflammation\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-b593-fdefb13ecb6d" class="">D) The resonance condition (phase alignment)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-84f1-fc239c096585" class="">Define coherence at each layer:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9438-dd3d485489e1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_\mu,\;C_\psi,\;C_M</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b6c7-e33071474903" class="">Resonance failure occurs when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-ac8a-f5d70da69240" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|Phase(C_\mu)-Phase(C_\psi)| + |Phase(C_\psi)-Phase(C_M)| &gt; \Theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-bb80-e89304214e31" class="">Interpretation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-be2a-ebd844b42946" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bodies destabilize</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-aaa1-ecbf64f7ab11" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">groups polarize</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9600-d558833e8545" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">institutions lose control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8aa1-ddd7e175a2a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cascades become likely</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ad51-eed7d8763bc5" class="">E) The damping spine across scales</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-97b9-dcc6c5baf73d" class="">True stabilization requires damping at all three layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a1f6-db6f853a24c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{total}=D_\mu + D_\psi + D_M</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a091-cf2e51cef790" class="">If society tries to stabilize only at macro level (policing/controls) while biology and psyche remain unstable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b425-e99002ddd675" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_M\uparrow,\;D_\mu\downarrow,\;D_\psi\downarrow \Rightarrow False\;Stability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-baca-c01c6399fd4e" class="">F) AI in the unified field</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-b903-c0203636f6f4" class="">AI primarily changes the <strong>information layer</strong> first:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b5a0-d570782e1f02" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
AI\uparrow \Rightarrow Inf:G\uparrow \Rightarrow Noise\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a447-d60343dbea68" class="">Noise increases cognitive load:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-bcad-fe9ba0c93c13" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Noise\uparrow \Rightarrow Sleep\downarrow \Rightarrow C_\mu\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-8f6f-dec022160c56" class="">Which then feeds polarization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-8350-e02f1a595ebc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_\mu\downarrow \Rightarrow C_\psi\downarrow \Rightarrow Cul:G\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-a472-d65753c89678" class="">This is a <strong>closed positive feedback loop</strong> unless macro damping increases via verification, friction, and accountability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b560-f06ef7b438e8" class="">G) What C7 means in unified-field terms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-9a2a-e7e4ae560585" class="">C7 is not “political stability.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-a6d0-c842b13945ae" class="">C7 is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bb6f-e4d7fbb517f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_\mu\uparrow \wedge C_\psi\uparrow \wedge C_M\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-96cf-e501137c459a" class="">D_{total}\uparrow,;;Buf_{total}\uparrow,;;Noise\downarrow<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a93e-c36477787b64" class="">C7 fails if any one layer stays in chronic high-gain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-8b94-cdacb35d8c28"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-baff-fca8b000bc9f" class="">If you want the next extension (still equation-first):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-892a-c904e2d6e582" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Build a <strong>phase diagram</strong> (regions where the system converges to A1–A12 attractors)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-89cd-ee31c573ccc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or give a <strong>control policy set</strong>: minimal levers that shift the system from late C6 to C7 with lowest cost</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-808b-f4e8c5c0f87a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or formalize “verification friction” as a new damping variable in the AI era</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-9201-c77a31dc5a36" class="">Exhaustive missing set (beyond the collapsed AMOS-Core)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a27a-d7d1507734c0" class="">This is the full gap inventory required to make AMOS operational as a coding/programming-grade deterministic system (local engine + verification layer). Grouped by irreducible subsystems. 
If any item is absent, you will eventually get undefined behavior, non-reproducibility, 
or unverifiable outputs.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-99e3-ed7459af98a3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a643-e0e5b14bf0fa" class="">1) Formal semantics layer</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-9f5d-fef8c20ecf18" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Object semantics</strong>: formal meaning of each object type (Claim/Invariant/Rule/Loop/Matrix/Test/Metric/Event/Artifact/Source).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-96e0-e1be841a8351" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Rule semantics</strong>: whether rules are<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-9a64-f9ea9281762b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">constraints ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-8c99-c7af8fa35f08" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">functions ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-a454-e01c36a2526b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">partial functions ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8952-d6c1cf902714" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rewrite rules ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-ba8a-e42dc127ed98" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">or programs (state transitions).</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b9a9-cb8bb27e75bd" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Inference semantics</strong>: what inference operators exist and are allowed:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-bd25-f046aab5bcc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deduction, 
typed inference, model-bounded inference, empirical aggregation.</li></ul></div></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a41a-e71787e4aafe" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Evaluation order semantics</strong>: total order / partial order for rule evaluation:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-b228-d0a13c41684e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   r_i \prec r_j \Rightarrow eval(r_i)\ \text{before}\ eval(r_j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-8f8f-e17a7cf3d31e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Truth-status semantics</strong>: allowed statuses (e.g., Valid/Bounded/Invalid/Unresolved) with formal criteria.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-92a5-ff1e94fa4705" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Rewrite termination semantics</strong> (if any rewrite exists): normal forms + termination proof strategy.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-964b-c60fb7d9e5b0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Side-effects semantics</strong>: allowed/forbidden side effects and how they are logged.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-9aa7-ce8dc9a6401c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-a250-dcad3118c65c" class="">2) Scope and boundary control</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b285-c3926aa4755a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Scope taxonomy</strong>: exact scope types (document, module, release, actor, environment, time-slice).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-94e3-ff04d4d7d9df" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Scope assignment rule</strong> for every object:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-bb92-d66ecddfaf9f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   \forall x:\ scope(x)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-ad10-ecb64d81a7a9" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Scope-limited contradiction rule</strong>: contradiction detection must specify whether it’s within-scope only or global.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-bdde-c20e66fcea55" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Scope-limited closure rules</strong>: inference closure must be explicitly scoped and terminating.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-9128-f0d4099d639f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-9930-ea6f8e9628a3" class="">3) Assumption registry (UCIA completeness)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-8921-ef127baba867" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Assumption extraction function</strong> for each claim:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a4c9-dfe8b84f963f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
