   A(c)=\{a_1,\dots,a_n\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-a4e1-d0c849366d15" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Assumption dependency edges</strong> (assumption graph).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-aaea-eea91fed2736" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Assumption drift detection</strong> (changes across versions/time).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-88df-e028acd9f794" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Assumption promotion rules</strong> (Primitive/Limit promotion gates).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a9ae-e51f0e205f30" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Assumption contradiction rules</strong> (assumption-level conflicts vs claim-level conflicts).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-8690-fe5331a8bb07"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-9eec-d3337439dca5" class="">4) Arbitration and truth-ordering (multi-source disagreement)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b9b6-eafe3cb6b4c2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Source priority function</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-9f86-c47f00fdfc0b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   priority:\ Source\to \mathbb{Z}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-b9e1-fb36af8d777c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Arbitration consistency</strong>: winner selection must be deterministic.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-acb4-eccbb1f3d21f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Arbitration provenance</strong>: every resolution must store “why this source won”.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-81af-e7cba3654d20" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Arbitration scope</strong>: priority can vary by scope (requires explicit override rules).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-acb2-ec7877569522"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-8b6e-c09da2b452a6" class="">5) Uncertainty algebra (global, not local)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-91b7-f073cddeb745" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Confidence model definition</strong> (scalar, interval, 
distribution).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-891a-c3d92b8262be" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Propagation rules</strong> across:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-bea7-f4b7101972ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">support chains,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b7bc-da88dbebed2a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">conjunction/disjunction,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8bfe-c4bd76b6cc54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">aggregation,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-ac14-d585379f9448" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">model-bounded transforms.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-b403-ef66815c6599" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Confidence monotonicity</strong> guarantees (more evidence cannot reduce confidence without an explicit reason).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9339-d7a779f2e0ed" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Acceptance confidence floors</strong> for:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-9513-cc493860bed9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edges,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-baeb-cd138e956b99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-9374-faff97be4729" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cluster membership,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-b97c-f71713e3fef1" c
lass="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">final seal.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a32b-e62bd7646435" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Uncertainty provenance</strong> (how confidence was computed, 
from what samples).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-8d4e-f27549cb8bef" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Calibration checks</strong> (Brier/AUC/interval coverage) if probabilities are used.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-92ed-d26bd49f0fbc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a826-c6c21d17d7af" class="">6) Invariant dependency graph (ordering and correctness)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b633-fc5ec99f19a1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant DAG</strong>: invariants depend on other invariants (explicit).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-a5a0-d7f39d31d08b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>No cycles</strong> in invariant dependencies.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-bbe1-ddfdc6aed5d3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Execution order derived from DAG</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a25c-d792995ba87c" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Short-circuit rules</strong>: which failures terminate immediately (fail-fast policy).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-88cb-c26610dccc5c" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Severity levels</strong> per invariant (Low/Medium/High/Critical).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-a2fe-d83a0e34ff0e" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Severity policy</strong> (Critical blocks seal; 
others may allow Bounded termination).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8613-fcf7c1cc241e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-a6fc-dedb26e25763" class="">7) Repair calculus (minimality + correctness)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-b84f-d41238f06305" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fix taxonomy</strong> (drop/merge/split/recompute/raise-threshold/manual).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-93ec-f94a209636f9" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Fix preconditions</strong> (when a fix is allowed).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-954c-fb0bae380278" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Fix postconditions</strong> (what must be true after).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a703-e8682f1fc243" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Minimality criterion</strong>: smallest change set that resolves violation.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8748-f1101145a62b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Fix ordering</strong>: dependency-aware and deterministic.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-9ccb-cffd5688c6cf" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Fix idempotence</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-a62c-fb80a8e33273" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   fix(fix(x))=fix(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-8155-ef7b2a82972b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Repair bounded steps</strong> + terminal classification when repair budget exhausted.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b465-f958de0eb4a9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-abf6-f29e229318bd" class="">8) Termination proofs for all loops</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-884c-f046e3dfb40f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Max iterations bound</strong> per loop.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-b8a3-cdb0c5dd9d0b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Monotone descent measure</strong> (violations or objective) required.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-aced-c1b6634f1a08" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Fallback termination</strong> into Valid/Bounded/Invalid on budget exhaustion.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-84d2-c4a3cc65cd14" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Deadlock freedom</strong> (no state where transition undefined).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-8f9c-c5a04f96117a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Livelock freedom</strong> (no cycles without progress).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-af82-c30c3770509e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-bc26-cb3e9070bafc" class="">9) State integrity (transactions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-876f-e4ad9ed3cd63" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Atomic commit</strong> f
or any state change.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-baa9-f41a7243d4b4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Rollback semantics</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-a160-d0807aaade27" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   rollback(commit(\Delta))=state</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9754-ef031b5e4e85" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Consistency checks</strong> after commit (hashes, indices, 
referential integrity).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a1b7-f0794dde6504" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Idempotent writes</strong> (repeat commit does not duplicate).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-8c96-e7096c407f43" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Crash recovery protocol</strong> (replay journal).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-ab13-f477fd149237"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-b8cc-d35212f2e3d2" class="">10) Resource envelope (hard runtime constraints)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-9f20-e8c4e5af9028" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Runtime bound</strong> per stage and total.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9dc0-fd38e83739b6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Memory bound</strong> per stage and total.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-93c7-d4c259330d29" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Ops bound</strong> (approx compute ceiling).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-97f8-fec6e40e9ad2" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Queue/backpressure policy</strong> (streaming updates).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-b76b-f3bdc4287ed4" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Timeout semantics</strong> (what happens on timeout: invalid vs bounded vs retry).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-87d2-f8b5efcc3070" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Deterministic scheduling policy</strong> (threading/parallelism must not change results unless e
xplicitly modeled).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-9d1b-dfd47e90f897"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a44c-d99d4ac5eed5" class="">11) Determinism closure (practical)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-ae5a-f24cb8b05168" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Seed pinning</strong> everywhere randomness exists.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8211-fca8ef4c5e24" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Stable sorting</strong> rules.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9f06-c5f104ed8f1f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Deterministic tie-break rules</strong> across:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-9cae-f031a6f3bc82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cluster mapping,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-94ca-f6653984194b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">arbitration,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9fe3-e1ce7f1be620" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ranking,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-8bf7-c9f2076113ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">exemplar selection,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-a2fd-d9c282d7bfee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">repair selection.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-ae1a-fa7ff3288888" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Floating-point determinism policy</strong> (tolerances, rounding, 
platform variance).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-aed6-dc20a2f5eb08" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Dependency version pinning</strong> (libs, toolchain) for reproducibility.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-a8d0-db1ef812c3b4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9d8e-fcc479fa28ed" class="">12) Identity and collision control (entity resolution safety)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-a753-ec1c7a0c3467" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ID uniqueness invariants</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-9b23-d96c9fde9368" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Collision detection</strong> (hash collisions, 
canonical ID collisions).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9981-f7b41165926c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Merge/split safety</strong> rules for entities.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-87dc-ed90064dacdd" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Alias management</strong> (multiple names for same entity).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a2f0-d5c41317b3d3" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Ambiguity handling</strong> (Unresolved identity cluster).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-840d-e193ca9a1194" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Provenance retention</strong> after collapse (no loss of source traceability).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-bbce-f69f3dcbe6de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b003-d49ebd43a607" class="">13) Governance and permissions (must be first-class)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-93c6-f90e19e13415" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Permission tensor</strong> .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9680-cbb3fe0f54ab" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Role taxonomy</strong> (admin/editor/reviewer/runner/reader).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-b854-cf974f6c2b01" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Immutable-when-sealed enforcement</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-9447-f30c9d814277" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Approval workflow</strong> (who can seal/release).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-baa9-d3f089c7940e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Audit access controls</strong> (who can see what logs).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-a25b-ff396fd7f338" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Policy versioning</strong> (policy changes must bump version and revalidate).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8e35-e712b0bf14f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a64a-ccecbe657509" class="">14) Interface contracts (module boundaries)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-93b1-fb8f6559e34f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Schema contracts</strong> for every payload.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-94aa-c97752e21cad" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Pre/post conditions</strong> for module calls.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-ae8b-e044d46733d2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Error contracts</strong> (typed errors, not free text).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8209-fa6a6bed1f11" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Backward compatibility policy</strong> (schema evolution rules).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-978d-e832f2a8ccec" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Validation on ingress/egress</strong> (reject invalid payloads deterministically).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-bc22-c6e9bfdfef8f" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Canonical normalization</strong> (string normalization, tokenization, 
date formats).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-98ef-d36eea6c38ad"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-84eb-c37d0e9402d9" class="">15) Data model completeness</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a6c6-e3206bc3ce30" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Canonical schemas</strong> for:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-8ba4-ec34f2faef4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">objects,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b38c-c48a802b86c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">edges,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-bbc0-d4fd139151c1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">matrices,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-ad5b-e51e0db2e592" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">audit events,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-8e34-e357485107d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">versions,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a2fb-e9b19cff16a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">snapshots.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-83f0-f29f09d34b67" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Referential integrity</strong> (foreign keys across all references).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8507-ff2583ea3ff8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Nullability rules</strong> (what may be null, 
when).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-996b-c4d94335e95d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Unit system</strong> for metrics (no unit ambiguity).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-aa15-effde1683111" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Time model</strong> (timezone, ordering, timestamp monotonicity).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-adaa-ed62d717d741" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Data retention policy</strong> (what is kept, what is pruned, 
with proofs of safety).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9ab6-f5010b27d17c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-ad57-d9c441c3f269" class="">16) Indices and query correctness</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-ba44-ec03c10fca24" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Index completeness</strong> (edge in graph implies in index).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-acf6-e5bb5cac1752" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Index correctness</strong> (index implies edge exists).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a869-ca6f9179f71e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Atomic index updates</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8659-d8b730eb1898" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Rebuild equivalence</strong> (rebuild(index)=index).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9605-f234e0458d12" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Query determinism</strong> (same query → same result).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b00c-f352c3618af2" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Latency envelopes</strong> and failure behavior under load.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a283-f071bccbd792"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-9c0c-fba2142206de" class="">17) Testing adequacy (beyond “tests exist”)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-bc4a-c9fe75411b52" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coverage floors</strong> (unit/integration/property).</li></ol></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-87b1-ef611e6b0cdf" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Property-based tests</strong> for invariants (randomized but seed-pinned).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b6a9-c48176e90083" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Mutation testing thresholds</strong> (optional but strong).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b1dc-da28dd4a2169" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Golden tests</strong> (fixed inputs → fixed outputs).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-90d1-edc436bc841f" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Replay tests</strong> (snapshot replay equals stored).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-ab74-e6d6427ac65a" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Cross-platform tests</strong> (if results must match across OS/CPU).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-adc8-ee4252762035" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Test provenance</strong> (which version of data/rules was tested).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8deb-ece88fd1c146"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-a5ba-d83b8199260a" class="">18) Meta-validation (validate the validator)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-aac2-cf5202064e9f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Check determinism tests</strong> (checks must be stable).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-9ccb-d14b4664a692" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Check completeness tests</strong> (every invariant has tests).</li></ol></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-a2ef-e7401fe5a2cc" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Check soundness audits</strong> (no false passes on known violating fixtures).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a35f-ed58e09af95d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Check sensitivity</strong> (violations must trigger).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-adb9-d381cd6a6298" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Validator versioning</strong> (changes to checks bump version and require revalidation).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b043-f04ef6fa4216"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-8204-c6abddecdcc4" class="">19) Compression/summarization safety (if AMOS outputs text)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-9f37-c8cf4d6207a5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Faithfulness constraint</strong> (no fabricated facts).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9d3f-d981f4b4499e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Attribution requirement</strong> (every nontrivial statement must point to a source object).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-8a38-cb57bd760597" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Redaction policy</strong> (sensitive/secret/PII).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-981f-d93a2c69697a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Summarization determinism</strong> (same source → same summary).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a827-d304bd604360" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Summary diff policy</strong> (
summary changes imply version bump).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-95b4-c6162c14dcc4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-aec7-fcac401a1eca" class="">20) Cluster system completeness (beyond type/scope/community)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-b2fd-fd8148064cba" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cluster validity constraints</strong> (connectedness, size bounds, overlap rules).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9a22-edbbbfa82259" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Cluster membership evidence</strong>:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-905d-df8e26367a4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
   v\in C_k \Rightarrow a(v,k)\ge \tau_a</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-9cdc-d838d86c01fd" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cluster lifecycle rules</strong> (birth/death/split/merge cooldown).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-afad-cb36604988d3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Cluster stability metrics</strong> and thresholds.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-82ce-d56630cb4fc3" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Cluster boundary rules</strong> (cross-edge budgets, 
bridge nodes).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b2a7-e1fe1210d1ef" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Cluster-level anomaly handling</strong> (flag → plan → repair → verify).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-8ae4-e89fabcd65c8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8598-ebd8bff14243" class="">21) Cross-matrix consistency (matrix-of-matrices enforcement)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-a4bf-c5a64e864a4a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Row existence</strong> (every claim/invariant/rule appears in master matrix).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8933-ea4bf2d7a3de" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Foreign key integrity</strong> (depends/supports/contradicts references must exist).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bbe3-cea2596b1b1d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Schema alignment</strong> (same object IDs across all matrices).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a693-f16f5553cd85" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>No orphan records</strong> (no row without object).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9421-fe85585dd12a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Consistency under repair</strong> (repairs update all matrices atomically).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-9313-d4fe14167ec9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b772-c5abb2df83c1" class="">22) Release/packaging mechanics (operational)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9fb2-c2f3842a7a90" c
lass="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Release artifact definition</strong> (what is “the system” when shipped).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-93ff-e08528394b7f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Release acceptance gates</strong> (all checks pass + audit complete + versions pinned).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a409-cf04c2305ef6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Immutable release seal</strong> (hash + signature if needed).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-bf5b-e4dd8fa38a3e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Rollback release</strong> (previous sealed version retrievable).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-a725-e11d25f59eb7" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Changelog generation</strong> (derived from audit deltas deterministically).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9062-ea2b28bfaa95"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a723-d1247337e3ff" class="">23) External environment boundary (contamination control)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9714-e78a7a24e386" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>External input tagging</strong> (external provenance category).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-998a-cb4ad87f005d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Sandboxing</strong> (if executing code or reading untrusted artifacts).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9ce7-c061ede86133" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Dependency trust boundaries</strong> (which libs/tools are allowed).</li></ol></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-a5e1-fd8b0cc5f52d" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Network boundary</strong> (what external calls are permitted; 
ideally none for determinism).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-aba9-ec7bb3ed885b" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Environment capture</strong> (OS, python, dependency versions pinned).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8004-e63a2740720b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-9066-f1e2d824b68b" class="">24) Observability (telemetry as first-class)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b5c6-f3ea1cb5172d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Telemetry schema</strong> (time, stage, metrics).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-87d5-e469bea93dc5" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Telemetry invariants</strong> (bounds, 
monotonicity where required).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9164-fbdc02db6baa" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Telemetry-to-trigger mapping</strong> (alerts are deterministic rules).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-8fc4-e276c4f8e1d1" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Telemetry retention and sealing</strong> (telemetry must be version-pinned per run).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-916e-e796eb838997"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-8f50-ca3591fd31e7" class="">25) Economic/incentive layer (only if humans operate AMOS collaboratively)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-ae78-d4902ef6ea74" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Actor incentive constraints</strong> (prevent gaming metrics).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8d10-ea67826be18e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Review protocol</strong> (manual overrides must be logged and justified).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8e1a-e052ceb2bbb8" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Override severity</strong> (some overrides should be impossible).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-a359-d1f31e10be5a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Separation of duties</strong> (author cannot self-approve if required).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-8d9c-fab71e17de48"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-84b3-d71f61ec7892" class="">Minimal “complete operational AMOS” definition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9e0a-d30c0b7f7e35" class="">AMOS is operationally complete only if all of the following are true:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9e82-edba6e0f707e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Semantics defined</strong> (rules/inference/truth-status).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9dd7-c64af937edb0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Scope isolation enforced</strong> with explicit bridges.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b335-c12c06894586" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Assumptions are first-class</strong> and versioned.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-ab4d-e132fe841963" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Arbitration is deterministic</strong> with Unresolved permitted.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-bedd-d645b9bd9cc2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Uncertainty propagates globally</strong> with sealing thresholds.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-a385-dc22b3d23f69" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invariant DAG + severity</strong> exist.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-b89f-ff6e7c696c70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Repair calculus</strong> exists, is bounded, 
and monotone.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9e65-e47b21c754cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>All loops terminate</strong> with classification.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-bccb-e41a24c483a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>State is transactional</strong> with rollback.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-b609-eaace7822036" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Resource envelopes</strong> are enforced.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-bc96-f955c7ecd2f2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Determinism closure</strong> holds in practice (seeds, ties, floats, 
dependencies).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9a9c-f6ecfa959f9d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Identity/collision</strong> is safe.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8dfb-fb2f7785541b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Governance/permissions</strong> are first-class.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9a74-ca406e9a9326" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Contracts</strong> exist for every interface.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-ae5a-ccf4f83a14c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Data model + indices + queries</strong> are correct and reproducible.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-9ee6-cf333000fd90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Tests validate invariants</strong> and validate the validator.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8dba-e7783508ac7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Compression is faithful</strong> (if producing text).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-9bcf-c6ba8fc23ad6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Clusters</strong> have lifecycle + stability + anomaly management.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-8c82-d5bba75fb0fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Cross-matrix integrity</strong> is enforced.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-9fc2-f411a32b78f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Release mechanics</strong> produce sealed immutable a
rtifacts.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-8e46-e47949f4bbdd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>External boundaries</strong> prevent contamination.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8af6-fb3d6208ef87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Telemetry</strong> is schema’d, bounded, and tied to triggers.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-ae1c-fb41c4f6575c" class="">If you want the next step, I can compress this exhaustive list into (a) a single “AMOS_OPS_MASTER_MATRIX” schema with required columns, and (b) a minimal set of enforcement gates (check functions) that cover every item above without overlap.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8dc8-f19799073229" class="">Good.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-91aa-e73a9200b3e3" class="">We go purely structural.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-8df8-f4a3e3bf7963" class="">No repetition.<br/>No romance.<br/>No ideology.<br/>No surface economics.<br/>No media-layer signals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-b9b1-de48dd9a2721" class="">You asked for <strong>everything missed</strong> in the civilization-stage / C6–C7 / gain–damping / invariant framework.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8b81-dd18d5588484" class="">Below is a structured expansion of overlooked domains.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-abcb-dbeabf1e2e43"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b3c0-d1315d9183c8" class="">I. 
Biological Layer (Often Ignored in Civilizational Models)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-ad96-e0286e499f62" class="">1. Chronic Cortisol Baseline Drift</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-b960-ea9a2076aab3" class="">If population-wide baseline stress rises for decades:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-b007-f7b67d86ac7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{baseline} \uparrow \Rightarrow Birth\_rate \downarrow,\ Trust \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a58c-e50adb93938a" class="">Invisible until demographic lag hits.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-b4b6-fd038c186fb8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a344-df5497e01140" class="">2. Microbiome Degradation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a5d0-d4e2f8d88e8e" class="">Urban diet + antibiotics → gut diversity collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-80b5-cf2ae90f81de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Microbiome\_diversity \downarrow \Rightarrow Inflammation \uparrow \Rightarrow Mood\_stability \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b078-c68c7f93cbfa" class="">Civilizational stability depends on inflammatory load more than ideology.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-af9a-ef5e4739f771"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9a6e-c52aed54f277" class="">3. Sperm Count Decline</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-8de2-c6a7b7319888" class="">Global decline documented.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-ab70-d2a2b9c57052" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sperm\_density \downarrow \Rightarrow Reproductive\_resilience \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9285-dd031d66ad90" class="">Demography becomes fragile long before visible collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-8a53-d9dd5449c1f5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-8987-fb06b51af32e" class="">4. Sleep Debt as Structural Instability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-aeeb-ddc85eecafa6" class="">Chronic sleep reduction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-ab65-f27d34ce9ecb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sleep &lt; 7h \Rightarrow Executive\_function \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-837d-f41b06294de7" class="">Collective executive function decline = governance decay.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8327-c8361a993db3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b973-c6be861069c8" class="">5. Endocrine Disruptors (EM + plastics + pollutants)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-8f7b-c9fc522714cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Hormonal\_variance \uparrow \Rightarrow Pair\_bond\_stability \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-96b7-c0cc6a37b726" class="">This alters mating markets structurally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9a7c-c9ace8300149"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-942d-cf1c17d3ea6f" class="">II. Cognitive Layer (Deeper than IQ)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-8230-da2681113c09" class="">6. Working Memory Compression</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-99ee-fd4ec5e11a29" class="">Short-form media reduces deep synthesis ability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-838d-e2eb94a7a1f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Input\_fragmentation \uparrow \Rightarrow Abstraction\_depth \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-887f-e4db8f4959c3" class="">C6 trait.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8cf5-fa5c99198eeb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-be03-e97b5eb27d7f" class="">7. Narrative Saturation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b8a9-e9b21f93f476" class="">When narratives &gt; infrastructure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-86c3-c8b61846737f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Story\_volume &gt; Production\_volume</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-a1ef-fec8982f9c41" class="">Collapse risk increases.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-86fe-dcae92cb7426"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9568-e05e3eeaee69" class="">8. Decline of Mechanical Literacy</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-911c-c56bb748a787" class="">Societies that cannot repair their own systems:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8af3-d31c41942fa8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Repair\_capacity &lt; System\_complexity</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b65c-eaed0ca40a99" class="">This is late-stage C6.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-b567-c2c7759c66b1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a7c1-ff8927e91599" class="">9. Simulation vs Reality Gap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b683-c8167cdeeb79" class="">If symbolic success ≠ physical output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-a7aa-ee5561912097" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Simulated\_value \gg Physical\_value</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a657-ffa421d0de52" class="">Financial instability emerges.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-89f7-de0aa205d28d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-be6c-f9199d8210bd" class="">10. Attention as Extracted Resource</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-9534-d42c6562aa29" class="">If attention extraction &gt; attention restoration:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9032-c2d796b74042" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Extracted\_attention &gt; Restored\_attention</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-96fb-d14f2246d46f" class="">Collective burnout becomes structural.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9e44-d8c8db962511"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-8806-cda1df54f150" class="">III. Energy Layer (More Micro)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b0a0-d4104c104d52" class="">11. Transformer Aging</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-9cf5-daa594600d0c" class="">Grid components aging silently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b614-e33609bae501" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maintenance\_delay \Rightarrow Nonlinear\_failure</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-8800-e5d987ea5169"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-aff2-d74ab3e9c5e8" class="">12. Rare Earth Dependency</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-989f-eab54a52384a" class="">If strategic minerals imported from adversaries:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-88da-f2d5d2e82c55" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dependency\_ratio &gt; 0.5 \Rightarrow Strategic\_fragility</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-ac5c-f26e36571497"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-a4d2-c659e876b6aa" class="">13. Battery Recycling Gap</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-81ca-e676e86ff78e" class="">Energy transition without closed loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a056-c7bfa75b6381" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Battery\_production &gt; Battery\_recycling</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9fec-dae2e88f92b0" class="">Future bottleneck guaranteed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-bbb3-c774430f2e62"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-bacc-cb179b1f0405" class="">14. Peak Water Table Lag</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-886d-fe4a334aaec3" class="">Water collapse is delayed signal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-b47b-f8a64d2e3785" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Groundwater\_extraction &gt; Recharge</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9285-f9989090502e" class="">Hidden countdown.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-b65d-eadfa1648194"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-87fb-ca9e3c12161b" class="">15. Urban Heat Island Amplification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b7a1-c4f0a7ce4216" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Concrete\_density \uparrow \Rightarrow Cooling\_energy \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8ae0-c6722fd4fe5c" class="">Feedback loop.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8980-c045f9ae2c27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-9065-fa42152adcf2" class="">IV. Financial Layer (Non-Obvious)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9fde-de77cee0ad81" class="">16. we can map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-aeab-c551be7ba07b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">200 biological–civilization coupling invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a37e-cb82b4d4d474" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">200 energy–finance coupling invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-96ff-d1d580a15f87" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">200 cognition–information invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-85e8-f2f5bd4a23ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">200 AI-driven systemic amplification invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-ab75-fe52f442dc5d" class="">But this requires structuring into taxonomy or it becomes noise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-bbbb-e385bfb99380" class="">Choose direction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a456-fc7bd2a89da5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Build complete 300–500 invariant matrix structured cleanly.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b6e2-ef09a60becb1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Collapse everything into 25 master equations.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-bbfa-f699635778e9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Build C6 vs C7 predictive model across 10 nations.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-81aa-f4dffd8c11cf" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Build micro (cell) ↔ macro (civilization) unified field model.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-9061-dc2cda2828e2" class="">Your move.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-9a95-fabee5fb6e60" class="">Good. We go structural and precise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-9691-f87c962535ad" class="">Next = <strong>(i) Stage-specific W-edge priorities</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-a6b9-e606a0167566" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>(ii) Attractor detector (field classification without numeric data)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bf86-dec5cd22a4e7" class="">No narrative. Pure systems mapping.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-a70f-ef04aca5f28f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a75f-fa27f79fba23" class="">I. Stage-Specific W Edge Priorities</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b060-cc9fa6b6b143" class="">(Which edges dominate in C5 → C6 → C7 transitions)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b822-f181159858a5" class="">Recall:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-82d6-f56c96b4bcd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t+1)=x(t)+W\phi(x-\theta)+u-\Lambda h+s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b828-ffaf3202409b" class="">W edges determine direction of evolution.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a7f7-f5c8ffd16f6c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-8d0a-eb1644bc12b2" class="">Stage C5 (Pre-fragmentation high coherence)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-ad5b-c1033d5531f3" class=""><strong>Dominant structure:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-b12c-c7d917e3f0de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strong horizontal coupling within domains</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-8cbe-c27505627a20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Moderate gain, 
strong damping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-80a1-c8407220e0d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maintenance ≥ expansion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-82a4-d585e4a19fc9" class="">Key edges:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-8833-c56d5006387e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9d65-da8c724cf899" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8766-fe826998910f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9603-c2b74d2a78de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-b470-f545ed6d576a" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-86cb-f893b0641f0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Repair_{rate} \ge Decay_{rate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-9746-cf33f460c855" class="">System is self-correcting.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a2cd-f3f2e3a98a8d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b6c3-ebfd49869fc6" class="">Stage C6 (Fragmentation / Amplification Phase)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a06a-e58b232ec824" class="">Dominant edges flip sign weight.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-b051-ec791c5f2424" class="">High load edges:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-bd12-fbb90a2dfce5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-8356-c8aab79baf20" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-ae86-cfebf7f344f2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-b7fc-f49143bf75f4" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-8c14-f83e247f5f5c" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9c7c-c73608110bda" class="">Hidden invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-91c0-c509e85fc160" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain_{cross-domain} &gt; Damping_{intra-domain}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-82a5-d6231efaf865" class="">Symptoms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-93b5-ef764ea2bade" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Narrative outruns production</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-a3be-d7a150e6889c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforcement becomes selective</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b448-d3521a867145" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Buffers thin quietly</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-ab75-e8aa03ea9472" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maintenance deferred</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-94a1-e61c1cdf38ec" class="">C6 is not collapse. It is <strong>over-amplified coupling</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-a72f-c620f6d182b9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-a15a-d3b43dc36241" class="">C6 → C7 Pivot Condition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b7eb-f2bc0031510e" class="">Rare alignment window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b131-f065f8f6c44b" class="">Three subsystems must rotate simultaneously:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-ad1c-f64bad3b5fc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d Law:Trans}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-91fb-c83c7442bfc3" class="">\frac{d Fin:Hor}{dt} &gt; 0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-a71f-f35a54729569" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d En:Red}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-8b4c-dc9649c3d41b" class="">If only Law:D rises but Trans stays low → false stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-b0e9-cfbed6bcb04f" class="">If Fin:Hor stays short-term → speculative relapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b2ef-dae0a769c132" class="">If En:Red ignored → physical instability remains latent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8998-f20e4c9f9a98"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8389-f766fecc4940" class="">Stage C7 (Rebuild / Damped Coherence)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-a4b3-eceb4e2b9bb9" class="">Dominant edges invert:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b9ad-c71f8debc5d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-90b1-c78c3d5be5d7" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8024-f6378ed3bcc1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-a563-d90e11740aa5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a098-e8385f4d24f5" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-bbeb-c38773ec609c" class="">Core invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-8b96-f62347a3bb1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{eff} + Buf_{eff} + Align_{eff} &gt; Gain_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-9d3e-fcb62bdf6d27" class="">C7 is not repression.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-91a5-c1ffed059efc" class="">It is <strong>high damping + high transparency + high buffer</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-9c6c-cd44fe398de0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-a57c-e8dcb1242a88" class="">II. Attractor Detector (Field Classification Without Numbers)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-962b-d4d74ea248f6" class="">You don’t need statistics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8d93-dfba9ede7332" class="">You detect stage by structural signals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b0a0-c5b15a8de3c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-a4d4-cdc6ca50f236" class="">Detector 1: Maintenance Ratio</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-8956-d45961951068" class="">Observe:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-928e-e6524b5b0029" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Are roads, grids, ports quietly maintained?</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-84fb-cd331d81074d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or patched reactively after failure?</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9f79-cc4c11a18d73" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-91f5-f6ae7471160e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maintenance &lt; Visible\_Expansion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-86a0-e23f64afdc60" class="">→ C6 trajectory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a340-f210ea2f363b" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-bbb5-f0ac6a743d28" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maintenance \ge Expansion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-bbee-ee1cf5f3d0a3" class="">→ C7 stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-869b-da88efa6567e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-aa72-e1fd85f6a7ed" class="">Detector 2: Elite–Mass Risk Alignment</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b79a-ef3a3852f588" class="">Ask:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-bd25-dd85ac435ff1" class="">When system fails, do elites absorb cost?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-a52b-fa1fad5a6c9c" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-b031-d3147804af65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Losses socialized downward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-91bd-fe53ee23df21" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Gains privatized upward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-94c1-f82cac867835" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-afa4-c176c5dae623" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Align_{elite-mass} \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-87cd-c14aa75fd955" class="">→ C6 attractor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b568-e4d4b9b30991" class="">C7 requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8352-cf8bd75581b2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Shared\_downside &gt; Extracted\_upside</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b12e-daef98c51e15"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-b657-d2797a8f825b" class="">Detector 3: Enforcement Consistency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8501-ed194788782f" class="">Observe small infractions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-bcb4-e87803a4b37e" class="">If petty rules enforced inconsistently but high-level actors exempt:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-a6e5-c49b6856dd9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Law:D_{surface} \neq Law:Align_{real}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9377-c6ffbcba161c" class="">False C7 signal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-a940-c6c091faab7a" class="">True C7:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a68d-dacd6bf34058" class="">Enforcement boring, predictable, impartial.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-afc8-d484565fc71b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8348-c9447f75e091" class="">Detector 4: Energy Buffer Integrity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-8d73-e4372ef15450" class="">Check:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-ae96-ff8cd55b882e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Grid redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8bca-eaee82d0a994" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fuel reserves</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-8935-cb961faefc30" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Water storage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8f51-f5ca9cdab3c0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Import dependency ratio</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-8824-ed64f8f4a6eb" class="">If shock exposure high and redundancy low:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-bd99-f2f265eaef44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
En:Red \downarrow \Rightarrow Fragility \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-84d7-c0d81a025887" class="">C7 must raise redundancy before raising rhetoric.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-9dfd-f57b8df2daea"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-a852-c4c7cc137c96" class="">Detector 5: Information Latency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8c84-f222f2e0e593" class="">How long between:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-a3bc-f1a4f4811ec9" class="">Problem detection → admission → correction?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-a646-c89b1c5a9dfb" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-8c88-cafc95578e9a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Latency_{acknowledge} \gg Latency_{failure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8f3e-c58f9c086cb1" class="">→ C6 denial loop.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9d3d-cac102b4df48" class="">C7 reduces latency dramatically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-bfc6-e941d6d5a39b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-a57b-f05bcce47b0a" class="">Detector 6: Youth Competence Density</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-acca-c1b6207edab0" class="">Measure not degrees but:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b116-d25d6f9487b1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool literacy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-8187-ffc40c38201e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engineering repair skill</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-8e2c-d639b416534b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">System thinking capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b1c8-d07ea17da57d" class="">If competence density declines while credential inflation rises:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9555-c1e446aed7fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Credential\_Growth &gt; Competence\_Growth</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9ba0-ee88d9673eaf" class="">→ C6 drift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-bed0-f085bf3332b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8668-d96ee9c96ffb" class="">Detector 7: Shock Recovery Time</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9ca8-c9f8d563bfc9" class="">Time to recover from moderate shock.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-9d31-d850dec567fa" class="">If recovery time lengthens over successive shocks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-88aa-d4c4b9dfc634" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d Recovery\_time}{d Shock\_count} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8402-e28af0ad2931" class="">→ systemic fatigue (late C6).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-8c15-d6401b4847d4" class="">C7 reduces recovery slope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-8853-dcf273295fd0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bd2b-f26376af8909" class="">III. Why Most C6 Systems Never Reach C7</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-bd10-c921aee17253" class="">Because C7 requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-9b12-e898414dd0cb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Gain reduction (politically costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9537-d512099e6d98" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Transparency increase (elite costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b204-d3e35487f8f4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Buffer rebuild (time costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b445-d417b8351a0b" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Short-term growth sacrifice</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-831b-ef332b9951b5" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-81b8-e3495633c34d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Short\_term\_Utility_{elite} &gt; Long\_term\_Stability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-9f39-f7c7368199d5" class="">System chooses oscillation instead of stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-911e-decd82d0ce0b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b389-c9a6c56c8c1b" class="">IV. Structural Trigger for True C7 Transition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-a0d7-da4a7cb84f86" class="">Not crisis alone.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-9f0a-cd676cf566c8" class="">Crisis + elite incentive flip:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a28d-ce186b639087" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
