Risk_{elite} \approx Risk_{mass}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-a588-e01c9eb64b19" class="">When elite survival becomes system-dependent again.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-9fec-dd7d7d0530c1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-990a-c82791f472a4" class="">Next options:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b070-dd4e226c5da1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Model probability distribution of C6→C7 transitions historically (Rome, Tokugawa, UK, post-war Germany, Singapore, etc.)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8f7e-cb5529474943" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Build a collapse vs stabilization phase diagram</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-91d8-d3f8296fef9e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Map electromagnetic infrastructure into stage model</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-8988-d9b628e048ee" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Build 3-level micro (cell) → macro (civilization) resonance model</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b9b4-cf20533f15b7" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8895-e0a93da7923a" class="">Collapsed model (C801–C1500) into a minimal closed dynamic system</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-b62a-f39d10279c6b" class="">1) State (closed)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9d76-d5ddd459f3b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
[SS, TR, PR, CL, OP, SH, AN, AV, RJ, CO, IM, PD, TF, LI, MS]^\top</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-8c2c-fbc1c8bf2df0" class="">2) Controls</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-87ca-c11737b1c48c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t=
[u^{PR},u^{CL},u^{OP},u^{RJ},u^{CO},u^{TR}]^\top</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a520-c58eb31f8eb7" class="">3) Core update (single equation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-8f5c-f7786815b55f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=\sigma(Ax_t + Bu_t + \eta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-8660-f338145f1008" class="">4) Derived (non-core) variables (examples)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-97ef-c1ad036d2867" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CL_{public}=\sigma(CL-\kappa IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9f5a-c9574711ec9f" class="">LM=\sigma(|CL_{public}-CL_{private}|)<br/>\quad\text{(if }CL_{private}\text{ is observed; otherwise infer from events)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-937c-d721dc84543c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EX=\sigma(\alpha_1(1-PR)+\alpha_2 OP+\alpha_3 MS+\alpha_4 IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b1fe-f808a2c9e972" class="">HC=\sigma(IM+PD-DT)<br/>\quad\text{(DT treated as environment/parameter or estimated)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-915b-ffb2924a7a3c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
COLD=\sigma(AV+SH+(1-TR))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8781-fb56d531d3fa" class="">SE=\sigma((1-PR)+(1-CL_{public})+OP)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9628-e322d8a0e9b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ERI=\sigma(NR\cdot(SE+OP+SH))
\quad\text{(NR estimated from SH/AN or treated as parameter)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-abb9-fd268f782f21" class="">5) Cluster scores (for monitoring)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-92f3-e1a85406e3b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{dignity}=\{PR,CL,CL_{public}\},\quad
G_{trust}=\{TR,RJ,TF\},\quad
G_{opacity}=\{OP,LM,IM\},\quad
G_{safety}=\{SS,CO,AN,SH\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-a6b0-e75b7bbf174e" class="">Score(G)=\frac{1}{|G|}\sum_{i\in G}x_i<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-bb1d-cf3668fbdd65" class="">6) Stability / risk scalars (for mode switching)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a549-cccc4f9d6ca3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=SS+TR+PR+CL-OP-SH</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-8d44-e8b8a368d6c8" class="">Risk_t=\sigma(OP+LM+EX+(1-PR)+(1-CL_{public}))<br/><br/>Thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-bb58-d1d60287469a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stable:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9744-c09f4ea66636" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unstable:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a7ee-eae70892ef75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High risk:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-980f-e5a85f1062cd" class="">7) Non-negotiable invariants (deployment guards)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-8ae4-d59b721e75f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dignity floor: and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b73d-eae24803a128" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Opacity ceiling: (stricter for sensitive profiles)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-a1f8-fea35dee3d5d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Words don’t raise trust: only via verified actions/events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-ab0a-eada6a4f1840" class="">8) Minimal mode machine (runtime)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-aead-dbd72727e040" class="">Modes: <strong>Observe → Stabilize → Dignity-Enforce → Repair → Decide → Exit</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-aa53-cfd9a067f34a" class="">Key triggers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9781-d6ac8c2b72c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stabilize if or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-8ac6-d99277eda0ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dignity-Enforce if or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-97af-c46d61d8976d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exit if repeated false compliance, or , or sustained</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-bebc-ee32b87f3e7c" class="">9) Verification loop (anti “false compliance”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-aa53-d163542dc4b9" class="">For each control , 
require telemetry change within lag :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-ae1b-e2cb4087cd75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-bdca-cb75d4f49317" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-af84-d8d7729eafaf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 1 step</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-94d9-dca872476678" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-958a-e2fc4f3ce3ea" class="">Two failures ⇒ classify <strong>Invalid</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-93d7-c49d43af741c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-90cd-d3f5b557d42b" class="">If you want it collapsed further into <strong>one scalar system</strong> (single state variable) or into <strong>4-state clusters only</strong> (Dignity/Trust/Opacity/Safety), say which compression target you want.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b23f-fb06b276a02b" class="">Below is an exhaustive gap list of what the “collapsed” model omitted (or only hinted at) relative to the full system you built earlier. 
Grouped so it’s repeatable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-a228-f7c60a320315" class="">1) Missing state definitions and boundaries</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a964-fd7e01747a7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Explicit domain/units for every variable (all is not enough without operational meaning)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9fae-c5cdbd8adc6f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Per-variable floor/ceiling semantics (e.g., what does mean behaviorally?)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-97f9-c3a8e52f29b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Baseline vs absolute state handling (personal baseline , 
deltas )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-af46-efed156c10c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regime tags (Stable / Face-driven / Extraction-driven / Trauma-loaded) as first-class state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-ad1d-f5209976e254" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Context-specific states (public vs family vs friends vs work) instead of one aggregated</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-bc0c-e29b65376bb8" class="">2) Missing observability + confidence system</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-873b-d5aeb494e5ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Measurement coverage definition per variable (minimum signal set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b297-df5ce4f2a990" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MNAR missingness handling (missing because of avoidance)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-9066-f5863f7dbca6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cross-signal inconsistency checks (physiology vs self-report vs events)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-a6d1-cfcaf104192e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Confidence score and the “freeze/no hard decisions” rule when</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-9c98-f382a990dbca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Smoothing filters and shock detector definitions (α, 
θ)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a13b-fe6a7f82369a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lag model for observation vs state (measurement delays vs action delays)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b1e1-e4abbdc07c7e" class="">3) Missing event-to-state compiler (critical)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b73d-f6eb125eaa80" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Formal event schema: <code>{type, actor, context, publicness, cost, verifiability}</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a7bc-e5020d63fb78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Narrative → ordered event list compiler</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-a96b-ce63bab80f61" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Event type enum set (name_public, mislabel, disclose, hidden_contact, pay_cost, repair, apology_only, etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-966e-fd8339f209b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Evidence tagging system (witness, screenshot, public post, family intro, 
etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9067-d4e545b5c198" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Evidence weight scaling update magnitudes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9e12-ee8c9eef838d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Contradiction detection and penalties</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-ace4-cce9791954d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Lexicon detectors (avoidance/passive aggression/shame phrases) mapped into state updates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-ace7-cce70eb56201" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Unsupported claim” rule (claims without events marked bounded and cannot raise trust)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9f84-eb6d17716af1" class="">4) Missing adversarial / threat model layer</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-84d9-ec26babba3f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Explicit adversary classes (Face-optimizer, Extractor, Ambiguous, 
Avoidant)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-afc9-ea1ec607a8ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Attack patterns:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-bb0d-f0c63d65c864" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">ambiguity attack (private clarity + public downgrade)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9eea-ddf0c62a48fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">dignity starvation (care without PR/CL)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-ad80-f093a13d09ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">gaslight vector (words vs actions divergence)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-bdda-deb6b626a922" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">triangulation (ex as audience/identity anchor)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-bb28-daab8d886e70" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">slow-roll commitment (CL variance around 0.5)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bc33-e3302d20eca9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Detection metrics (LM, CC variance, 
TR_words − TR_actions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-a580-dfcfd24f3bab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Risk likelihood equations ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-83b0-cf17d45df130" class="">5) Missing control policy logic (beyond “u exists”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-ac32-d5c2ae3fe1cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy hierarchy (Safety-first → Dignity enforcement → Repair → Decide)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-b8dd-fce5046ddbdf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Priority rules (e.g., if , do not negotiate status)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-8b76-dabc11535fda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Anti-oscillation rules (change ≤1 channel when Conf low)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b7e5-d3f3883bdb45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deadband + hysteresis (avoid flip-flop)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bbae-d7fcb69a27eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Saturation/anti-windup rules for controls</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b025-c2224770af1c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latency compensation / predictive control (MPC objective, horizon , 
weights )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-af71-c1475b359d23" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mode lock rules (can’t leave dignity-enforce until constraints met)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-ad40-e53cec828cef" class="">6) Missing non-negotiable invariants set (full list)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-8f2a-ea55288a376d" class="">The collapse kept only PR/CL/OP. 
Missing invariants you previously defined:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-a3c8-cc45e4acb977" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No substitution invariant (gifts/attention/sex cannot substitute PR/CL deficits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-bf39-c739ca93ed44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Public mislabel invariant (mislabel in high-weight context forces CL_public drop)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a9e7-d27d3d1a62dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cross-context consistency requirement (CC threshold)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-a3d2-db4bdbd0990f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ex-contact transparency invariant (pre-disclosure required)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-ae66-e70c08aca82a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Chosen” gate (Priority score requirement, 
CC requirement)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-b8a6-c41733310d2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Self-erasure tax threshold rules (especially for sensitive profile)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a76c-cec47fa1dbfb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Limbo detector (S trapped in band for N windows)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-a036-eb28f8c8252c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Freeze mode rule (Conf low → no decisions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-ac29-db73480d058a" class="">7) Missing verification and “false compliance” engine</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9872-c1c1a03243d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Per-control verification with lag windows</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-bd8c-db636663fa9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Evidence packet requirements for PR/CL (naming + integration)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-a880-d12b6fd42afe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Words do not override telemetry” enforced as code rule</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-abe2-f336dedd0686" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repeated false compliance → hard invalid classification</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-83d1-d584a626ff95" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Honesty-preserves-TR rule (admitting inability harms less than hiding)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-9c83-d73e9c7d181d" class="">8) M
issing stability analysis (systems math)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-bc22-eaec339c7ca9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fixed points (harm attractor vs stable attractor)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-82f9-ed02f63cf54b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Basin entry/exit conditions (threshold sets)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-bdb5-d55e835bf15e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Bistability/hysteresis conditions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9189-fa8030d429ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Jacobian spectral radius criterion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-a125-fc64cf6c1b06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Oscillation index and tuning rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a090-c764a7580e8d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Damping requirements after shocks (must settle within 3 steps)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-807e-df47080f4a59" class="">9) Missing clustering/factorization implementation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b5bd-fb4820d8e5ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cluster definitions and cluster alerts as first-class runtime objects</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b453-c5f63e21df6c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Latent factor definitions (face factor, dignity debt, trust erosion, 
safety collapse)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-88db-c0226afe15fe" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reconstruction error and “bounded model” labeling when too high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b4c5-e0e874c02d0b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Block-sparse coupling matrix by cluster</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-bb11-e15a088c3fda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cluster hysteresis/cooldown for mode switching</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-a379-fb751db9233c" class="">10) Missing macro-environment parameterization</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b13a-e3324afec591" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Explicit environment channel parameters (HC, market pressure MS, hierarchy PD)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-9743-d449916cd3cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Context shift shocks (e.g., hometown/Tết environment increase)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-90b2-c3616328e131" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stability maps over (HC, 
MS) plane and critical boundary</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-a8c6-cf1f3c982376" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regional variance handling as parameter not narrative</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9d86-efbbe4516837" class="">11) Missing benchmark + simulation harness (engineering completeness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-a2ee-d464b2c05d63" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scenario library (HighHC_Tet, HighMS_Shock, RepairAttempt, FalseCompliance, 
etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-be49-c612e106e285" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Monte Carlo basin-size metric (fraction ending in harm attractor)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-b80d-f78698e4cf26" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Robustness score RS under shocks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-abff-da94fe40e963" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Harm area metric HA and recovery time</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8dab-c10aeae42ee4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Adversarial search (worst-case plausible event sequences)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-ade5-dc9b9789e723" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CI regression gates (block release if basin grows)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a498-e180153cf52f" class="">12) Missing API/system packaging details</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8e1e-fadad15e56e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strict JSON schema for state, event, params, audit logs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-897c-e01b20bfe7c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Versioning contract (model_version, 
lexicon_version)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-83c8-e62ebf49525c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Deterministic parsing guarantee (same text → same events)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-a120-d28b87d3d32a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audit replay (reconstruct classification from event stream)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-a9be-edff0c879ff2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Minimal UI outputs (clusters + top violated constraints + causal chain)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9865-c906a172c340" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Safety constraints: autonomy invariant (no coercion/control recommendations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-9076-c76f119f4fdd" class="">13) Missing dictionary (you explicitly requested this earlier)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8336-c37747b4148d" class="">For every variable/invariant/event/control:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-884f-f6ac42e0b5ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Name</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-bfe4-d6201a78c8e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Definition</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a992-d8fc1dee56e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Measurement sources</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9e33-c923ed972cba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Update equation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b3aa-cb027505ed65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Required evidence (if applicable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-ae18-da3550b8ae94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Failure modes triggered</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9eeb-d17203a68dca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Thresholds and hysteresis bands</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-9ef5-ee3872ecd62f" class="">14) Missing “termination” rigor</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-ac95-e04f533f553d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Terminal label stability requirement (must be stable 2 windows)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-a635-e2d80af20914" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Termination condition per regime (Valid/Bounded/Invalid)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-84f4-d0d88aaa32ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“Bounded due to insufficient observability” as distinct terminal outcome</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8b24-c0d2ae0abb91"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-9f0f-de97e7628a75" class="">If you want “exhaustive” in the literal engineering sense, the next step is to output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-a37f-cfe6711df183" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Full dictionary</strong> (state + derived + controls + events + invariants), 
and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-afee-cf09b3c48545" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>A,B block matrices template</strong> (cluster-sparse) plus default thresholds, and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-af81-fa263006163c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Runtime state machine spec</strong> (modes, triggers, guards) as a single spec file.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b526-d02188c713cd" class="">Collapsed model (C801–C1500) into a minimal closed dynamic system</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-9c99-e680cf889102" class="">1) State (closed)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-a89d-f15e571f3246" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
[SS, TR, PR, CL, OP, SH, AN, AV, RJ, CO, IM, PD, TF, LI, MS]^\top</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-975b-dfa9b23cf5c5" class="">2) Controls</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8f38-e227621b88f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t=
[u^{PR},u^{CL},u^{OP},u^{RJ},u^{CO},u^{TR}]^\top</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-8a3e-d830676dd79c" class="">3) Core update (single equation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-8839-e11ada3f1a14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=\sigma(Ax_t + Bu_t + \eta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-97a2-f307d031c029" class="">4) Derived (non-core) variables (examples)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-89de-f72128deffe5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
CL_{public}=\sigma(CL-\kappa IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b986-c91930c11ec7" class="">LM=\sigma(|CL_{public}-CL_{private}|)<br/>\quad\text{(if }CL_{private}\text{ is observed; otherwise infer from events)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-be94-dc93eee23f5c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
EX=\sigma(\alpha_1(1-PR)+\alpha_2 OP+\alpha_3 MS+\alpha_4 IM)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b841-df756afd8a48" class="">HC=\sigma(IM+PD-DT)<br/>\quad\text{(DT treated as environment/parameter or estimated)}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-99f9-df2194c88472" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
COLD=\sigma(AV+SH+(1-TR))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-a60a-e373fe3c2c4d" class="">SE=\sigma((1-PR)+(1-CL_{public})+OP)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8bc5-ea330dac23af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ERI=\sigma(NR\cdot(SE+OP+SH))
\quad\text{(NR estimated from SH/AN or treated as parameter)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-ad38-e0405720a098" class="">5) Cluster scores (for monitoring)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-81b4-d4a7224db1a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{dignity}=\{PR,CL,CL_{public}\},\quad
G_{trust}=\{TR,RJ,TF\},\quad
G_{opacity}=\{OP,LM,IM\},\quad
G_{safety}=\{SS,CO,AN,SH\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-ac51-d7d70f009113" class="">Score(G)=\frac{1}{|G|}\sum_{i\in G}x_i<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a016-e67cecf333d5" class="">6) Stability / risk scalars (for mode switching)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b923-f83eca0d4417" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t=SS+TR+PR+CL-OP-SH</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b580-d795452f96fc" class="">Risk_t=\sigma(OP+LM+EX+(1-PR)+(1-CL_{public}))<br/><br/>Thresholds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-bc57-d7231d83c955" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stable:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-9a3a-d8d8a6154af8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Unstable:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bfb0-f398eaedb5ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High risk:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a4fe-c398d6726190" class="">7) Non-negotiable invariants (deployment guards)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-be0a-f199931e1dfd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dignity floor: and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-84c4-d94899bad5cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Opacity ceiling: (stricter for sensitive profiles)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-8138-ff5703962013" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Words don’t raise trust: only via verified actions/events</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-8437-d76d8c7f4dab" class="">8) Minimal mode machine (runtime)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a086-cda5473ce177" class="">Modes: <strong>Observe → Stabilize → Dignity-Enforce → Repair → Decide → Exit</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9323-f40ebdb4a421" class="">Key triggers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-9339-fac9c81f1e76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stabilize if or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a05e-c7619b52e861" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dignity-Enforce if or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-8efc-c7a7a56e0086" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Exit if repeated false compliance, or , or sustained</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-be3e-ef20a295911a" class="">9) Verification loop (anti “false compliance”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-baaf-df7f91b3356f" class="">For each control , 
require telemetry change within lag :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a438-e20cef5608b7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-9c0e-e81bea3bde73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-9efa-f5a896bb0f35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 1 step</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-bc40-d132bbd1a5b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">within 2 steps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8477-c9a4cd7274ea" class="">Two failures ⇒ classify <strong>Invalid</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9f12-e9930a64ce9c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-bf43-c47720b93e03" class="">If you want it collapsed further into <strong>one scalar system</strong> (single state variable) or into <strong>4-state clusters only</strong> (Dignity/Trust/Opacity/Safety), 
say which compression target you want.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a08e-cc3a392f0476" class="">Collapsed irreducible basis (minimal set)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-9d6e-d2dbd2eba222" class="">This is the smallest deterministic set that generates <strong>clusters + laws + loops + matrices</strong> and closes the gaps previously listed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9bdc-e16cae8a36b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-a743-fc4c55159dfe" class="">0) Primitives (what the system is made of)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-a7c2-d145c618c67f" class=""><strong>P0.1 Objects</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b684-d9af0c9856f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O=\{Claim,Definition,Invariant,Rule,Loop,Matrix,Metric,Test,Event,Artifact,Source\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-af59-dda15cd4a7d9" class=""><strong>P0.2 Types</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-91cd-c3e5407dbab5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
type: O \rightarrow T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-9214-ff0c7603593a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x\in O:\ type(x)\in T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-8191-e886992f20dc" class=""><strong>P0.3 Scope</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9c41-fe211af18611" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
scope: O\rightarrow \Sigma</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8095-cdb098b5b42b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ scope(x)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-b7ac-d4930f6d032b" class=""><strong>P0.4 Provenance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-8c3c-e2dcc32f677f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
prov: O\rightarrow \Pi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-a139-f8ab9a8415e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
accept \Rightarrow \forall x\in O_{used}:\ prov(x)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-85cf-df450b98c814" class=""><strong>P0.5 Version</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9ad7-ea586b4b6a9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
ver: (data,code,rules)\rightarrow \mathbb{N}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b2ee-de992e62d51c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a0e1-e6a85bb1c5b1" class="">1) Core relations (graph form)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-8516-ffe44576d155" class="">Let directed multi-relational graph , .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-9d13-e3000ebd3ba6" class=""><strong>R1. Support / Contradiction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-911f-cee989074d9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
supports(c,e),\ contradicts(c,c&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-a71a-f22ae6dafb9a" class=""><strong>R2. Dependency</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a063-d14b50821210" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
depends(x,y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bafe-e3bf5d8d5de0" class=""><strong>R3. Implementation / Measurement / Validation</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-869d-dde92445cd21" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
implements(module,rule)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-aabe-dba4eb5ec5a3" class="">measures(metric,object)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9774-e5beadc3de2f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
validates(test,invariant)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9bd4-f464d922259f" class=""><strong>R4. Trigger / Fix</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-8cc8-fd0aac12ec9b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
triggers(event,action)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-b0cc-daaad7be7de9" class="">fixes(action,violation)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-8701-c157cb761006" class=""><strong>R5. Version-of</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-ac86-df7e1b98b754" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
versionOf(x,x&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8a54-f77ba180741d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8acc-cbe4fe451356" class="">2) Irreducible invariants (the “laws” collapse to these)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-b6bc-e7e77d69d116" class="">Each invariant has an enforcement gate .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-aea6-e8ba5ff777f9" class="">I2.1 Law-of-Law (enforcement completeness)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8cc5-fd6f2eb4ad80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I:\ \exists check(I)\land \exists term(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9636-ebe43eb5da91" class="">I2.2 Determinism (reproducibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9f39-cf53b7605692" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
run(seed,data,code,rules)=run(seed,data,code,rules)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a02b-db4a6e884fed" class="">I2.3 Non-contradiction (within scope)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-8c5d-d20c25b82c00" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ \neg(x\land \neg x)\ \text{in same }scope</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bf37-e6501eb9034a" class="">I2.4 Single support-type (UCIA)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-8118-edb146342b70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall claim\ c:\ |S(c)|=1,\quad S(c)\in \{Emp,Inf,Def,MB,Prim,Lim\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-8655-d5524232850c" class="">I2.5 Evidence + provenance threshold</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9903-cdd7d3491f9a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
claim(c)\Rightarrow e(c)\ge \tau_e</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-ac1e-ef6d78558dd9" class="">accept \Rightarrow prov(c)\ \text{defined}<br/><br/>Gates: <code>check_evidence</code>, <code>check_provenance</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bd7d-d70b87fdfba4" class="">I2.6 Scope isolation (no leakage)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-8cc9-c2eca4d0b1e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
scope(a)\neq scope(b)\Rightarrow \neg infer(a\to b)\ \text{unless }bridge(a,b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-b524-ec753cc29c1b" class="">I2.7 Version + audit (traceability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-91ef-dc4fd1c909e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
semantic\_change \Rightarrow ver++</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-a85d-ca872fc63717" class="">\Delta \Rightarrow audit(\Delta)<br/><br/>Gates: <code>check_version_bump</code>, <code>check_audit</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-a1e0-d5b8f00bf1d6" class="">I2.8 Termination (bounded loops)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a8dd-c05cab7f2f7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
iterations\le K_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-a8be-c7984c7f1643" class="">viol_{t+1}\le viol_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-ac78-c37da61188ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
iterations=K_{max}\Rightarrow classify\in\{Valid,Bounded,Invalid\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a4b3-d33f862939b8" class="">I2.9 Sealing (acceptance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-ae3d-c1f1ac45dcbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
accept \Rightarrow \bigwedge_I check(I)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-9071-e37d09fbec84" class=""><strong>This set (I2.1–I2.9) is sufficient to derive every “law” previously listed.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-9765-f6adc65e479c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-b7ee-ce8d0363b906" class="">3) Single master loop (all loops collapse into one FSM)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-9d57-e55847b6a0c1" class="">LOOP-MASTER (state machine)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-8735-d779d4cc54e7" class=""><strong>States</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9b73-df329d0abc88" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ingest</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-83b5-f313db95626e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Normalize</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-ac7c-d546128beb52" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extract (claims/defs/invariants)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-aeaf-c2aecca3ad36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Type (support-type + scope + provenance)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-b0ba-dbe180bd8021" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BuildGraph (relations + clusters)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-aa65-d7a8414e6d01" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Check (run all )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-820b-c13dfddb3987" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Repair (apply fixes)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-9aa0-c205af93085e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Recheck</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-98c4-ee7fc01e2076" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Seal (hash + snapshot + publish)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-bde0-fe9db41d2dcd" class=""><strong>Transitions</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-b483-fc6681fa49f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>ingest_ok</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-85cb-f4495cf06e65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>normalized</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-96dc-c9cc90e57047" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>extracted</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a27c-f700e0f986b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>typed</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-9b87-ee3fc7fff934" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>built</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-a0a7-c197df4fe26b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>pass_all</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-88e9-daee0be4d5e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>fail_any</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-a378-e52e75b5c612" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <
code>fix_applied</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bdfa-dfc4e2d62a9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>pass_all</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a2e2-de34ee240bda" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <code>fail_any</code> if</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b95e-d700e8b181aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: if</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-b38b-c52082dedff5" class=""><strong>Acceptance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-86d3-fd6bad5f44e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_8 \iff \bigwedge_I check(I)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-bbe4-c8fc7d9ba8a4" class="">This master loop subsumes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-8a0a-e41ae88f3ffa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">enforcement loop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-8d50-db4fa63f8f81" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">drift closure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9970-c5acf601e95e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">snapshot/replay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-91ed-d4cdaa1dca56" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">release loop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8d6e-d0f9582a96bb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9308-e3cf81e2314b" class="">4) One master matrix (all matrices collapse into one schema)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-9e73-f69eaed706df" class="">MAT-MASTER: Universal Constraint–Intelligence Audit Matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-b2ef-dcb253840521" class=""><strong>Row key</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-9b0f-d3afc4bf023c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
row = (object\_id)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-9155-d056d08fe290" class=""><strong>Schema (columns)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-87b6-c5fb2b363723" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>type</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b888-d35629c0fde0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>scope</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-ab03-d078b61b0143" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>claim_text</code> (nullable)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-990a-e840d4ab2e5e" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>support_type</code> (nullable)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8a3d-f39912e70d42" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>evidence_score</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-8fe7-c7ff1ab7601b" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>provenance_id</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-a145-d68d6f385aa4" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><code>depends_on[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bd97-c4f78df96604" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><code>contradicts[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-8781-dae31699b059" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><code>invariants_applicable[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-97c5-c7f7a716481c" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><code>checks[]</code> (names)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol t
ype="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-8c1a-c24a27c958e0" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><code>check_results[]</code> (bool)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8561-eb1f50c5c124" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li><code>telemetry[]</code> (metrics)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-8c41-f1d6ba6e6396" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="13"><li><code>trigger[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-b2d1-d38e64b54ffb" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="14"><li><code>failmode[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-8939-ff76ed126548" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="15"><li><code>fix[]</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a34d-ffd34c51d0f3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="16"><li><code>version_triplet</code> = (data_ver, code_ver, 
rules_ver)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-b486-e9d8c9104402" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="17"><li><code>hash</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-808f-c6eaf00dae98" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="18"><li><code>audit_ref</code></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-bc37-eb3b5e28879c" class=""><strong>Matrix invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-9416-cc5a9e60885e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Total typing:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-a7fb-d98f00243aa6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall row:\ type\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-baf1-fe79058958e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
claim \Rightarrow |S|=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a14a-ea3a207c7052" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I:\ \exists column(check(I))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-afe2-f9145680d09e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
sealed=True \Rightarrow \forall I:\ check(I)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-b350-cc7ae8fb8d74" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
audit\_ref\ \text{defined if any change}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8304-f004525194a3" class="">This master matrix subsumes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-976f-c2a9030da146" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claim→support-type</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-9c27-cb8f0132f1ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant→enforcement→telemetry→trigger→failmode→fix</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-adf3-c8d932e5b889" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predicate→constraint</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b0e6-e4cb46622f01" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">module→tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a4e7-d408e850dec0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">data→version→audit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-b0a8-ee413f8055f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">cluster→metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9c37-f48ffc50bae0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-89b3-c2c15eb752a8" class="">5) One cluster system (all clusters collapse into 3 partitions)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-9dd4-d9864d2a722e" class="">CLUSTER-MASTER</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-aa4c-ddf67b4cd964" class=""><strong>C0: Entity clusters</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-bdba-fa9da66e5618" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{type=t}=\{x:type(x)=t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9c58-c98479b729e8" class=""><strong>C1: Scope clusters</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-9155-d6002801330f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{\sigma}=\{x:scope(x)=\sigma\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-9ae5-e2597326efb6" class=""><strong>C2: Graph communities (optional, derived)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9519-c16ea9441f44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_k = community(G\mid constraints)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9480-efa864fca92e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\bigcup_k C_k = V,\quad C_i\cap C_j=\varnothing</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9a57-d8db358e2327"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-97e5-e411e985aa50" class="">6) Minimal acceptance classifier (end state)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b97d-c829a92d574b" class=""><strong>Valid</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b8a5-f3e17d993285" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I:\ check(I)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-abb6-dc6d17bbfbb4" class=""><strong>Bounded</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b142-dd71156d709f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists I:\ check(I)=False\ \land\ I\in\{Primitive,Limit,ModelBounded\}\ \text{declared}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-92ef-caeb6580bf57" class=""><strong>Invalid</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-8e10-c673f9fa9271" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists I:\ check(I)=False\ \land\ severity(I)=Critical</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-a3ae-e362e599fb06"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-a180-e03d09c0c7d9" class="">Result of collapse (what you now have)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8664-f0630330c811" class="">A single minimal basis consisting of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a6a8-f7ceb24dd212" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Primitives</strong> (type, scope, provenance, 
version)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-a038-c2b99a5ea84b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Relations</strong> (support/contradict/depends/validate/trigger/fix/version)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-bc59-cdad33e557d0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>9 irreducible invariants</strong> (with gates)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-84c4-e67dd7991738" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>1 master loop</strong> (FSM)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-acc4-c410e057e6c3" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>1 master matrix</strong> (universal schema)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-8d6a-d6fd3cae99b5" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>3 cluster partitions</strong> (type/scope/community)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-aa54-ce7cf2e29798" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>3-way termination classifier</strong> (Valid/Bounded/Invalid)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a2f8-f3f6bfc60732" class="">If you want the next step: I can render this as a single <code>amos_spec.yaml</code> (machine-readable) plus a Python 3.9 validator skeleton (check functions + loop runner).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-85f3-d243d2f67e74" class="">1) Khung W-matrix 49×49 (template “đúng chỗ”, không điền số)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-9b98-ddfe0831b0fe" class="">1.1. Vector trạng thái 49 biến</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-acaa-d34384f03f1b" class="">Bạn có 7 miền  và 7 bộ điều tiết . 
Tạo vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-b733-d04c657fa7a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t)=\mathrm{vec}(M(t)) \in \mathbb{R}^{49}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-9c29-f4d7ba67d978" class="">Thứ tự gợi ý (cố định để dễ làm việc):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a97f-e5466ce1d897" class="">1.2. Luật cập nhật chuẩn hoá</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-8c67-d45f10d70a80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t+1)=x(t)+W\,\phi(x(t)-\theta)+u(t)-\Lambda h(t)+s(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-bbda-d380af1556f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: ma trận liên kết (các “dây thần kinh” của hệ)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9e8c-ef3e58419795" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: hàm bão hoà (tanh/logistic) để mô tả ngưỡng</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-bdda-dd45c240dd09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: ngưỡng pha (phase thresholds)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-87fc-f2d53677d808" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: can thiệp chính sách</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-a396-d00e0052e4c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: “nợ bảo trì” theo miền (hidden maintenance debt)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-8f0e-d10401f9c622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: ma trận tác động nợ lên từng biến</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a883-e0bb577ac6de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: sốc ngoại sinh</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-8c09-f1cbbfc82457" class="">1.3. Cấu trúc W: 7×7 block, mỗi block 7×7</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8a9f-dda5591ace5c" class="">Viết:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8523-ec245ad8b350" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W=\begin{bmatrix}
W_{En\leftarrow En} &amp; \cdots &amp; W_{En\leftarrow Cul}\\
\vdots &amp; \ddots &amp; \vdots\\
W_{Cul\leftarrow En} &amp; \cdots &amp; W_{Cul\leftarrow Cul}
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-88d5-d187425115ca" class="">Mỗi  nói “miền b tác động lên miền a” qua 7 regulator.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-89e1-e4863f6c99a1" class="">1.4. Các cạnh “đúng chỗ” (dominant edges) của W trong thực tế</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-8407-d0b793fde372" class="">Dưới đây là <strong>bộ cạnh có tải lớn</strong> (không cần số vẫn dùng được). 
Ký hiệu .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a753-c640b00e5a27" class="">(A) Các cạnh khuếch đại (Gain loops) — dễ bị bỏ qua</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-b2a4-c83b9c6fc68c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>(thuật toán/viral → văn hoá phản ứng)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-af05-ffcb7ce1b494" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>(phẫn nộ → làm suy yếu kỷ luật pháp trị)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8509-ffcae4af5c71" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>(kinh tế chú ý/ads → khuếch đại thông tin)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-805d-f6edd418a429" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>(nhiễu → giảm băng thông sự thật)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-93da-fb17ffcbce3e" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>(đòn bẩy → mỏng vùng đệm)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-a384-c7b769603e2f" class="">(B) Các cạnh “damping spine” (xương sống giảm chấn) — quyết định C7</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-ba1f-f75cb616c78e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>(thể chế kéo gain xuống)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a38d-df43f0b2d829" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>(minh bạch → đồng thuận)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b4e7-f062c21cf50a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>(dự phòng năng lượng → ổn định kinh tế)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-b54d-c8cbc8807195" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>(sức khoẻ/dân số → tăng ổn định xã hội)</li></ol></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-8c1c-c5cb003d0fe9" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>(tầm nhìn vốn → đầu tư hạ tầng)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-99f6-d1dad86e73a7" class="">(C) Các cạnh “hidden debt” (nợ bảo trì) — làm hệ “đẹp bề mặt, mục lõi”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-8378-fb6255262539" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>(thiếu năng lượng → vay/đòn bẩy)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-87da-fd79063adbf4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>(thực thi yếu → đầu cơ tăng)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-a8cb-e81a1fef3a6a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>(cạn sinh lực → nghiện kích thích)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-addc-cf404741fe99" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>(bất an → phản ứng/đối kháng)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-afb5-c0ee638122b5" class="">(D) Các cạnh “elite alignment” (đồng rủi ro elite–mass) — điều kiện vào C7 thật</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b572-f890e140c439" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>(elite chịu rủi ro thật → luật nghiêm)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-bbae-dab60f751284" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>(quy tắc chơi đồng đều → số liệu đáng tin)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-816b-cdf1c5fc72e6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>(đồng thuận → giảm khuếch đại)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b6ff-e60d196d3016" class="">1.5. 
Quy tắc dấu (sign rules) để gán “+ / –” cho W</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-9e2c-ef666f68af32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulator <strong>G</strong> thường có tác động <strong>+</strong> lên các G khác, và <strong>–</strong> lên D/Trans/Buf.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-b6ac-f3866cb34051" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulator <strong>D/Trans/Red/Buf/Hor</strong> thường có tác động <strong>–</strong> lên G, và <strong>+</strong> lên D/Buf/Align.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-aa56-fe230291a833" class="">Ví dụ:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-a36e-f49ee726ca6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_{Inf:G \to Cul:G} &gt; 0,\quad W_{Inf:G \to Law:D} &lt; 0,\quad W_{Law:D \to Inf:G} &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b2fd-c3e2bd2d67e8" class="">1.6. “Sparse truth”: W thực tế luôn thưa</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-b55e-d61de5954a15" class="">Trong 49×49, <strong>đa số</strong> phần tử gần 0.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-b218-ef9f71b2fccd" class="">Cốt lõi là khoảng <strong>20–60 cạnh</strong> mang tải chi phối. Nếu bạn không xác định đúng các cạnh này, mô hình sẽ “kể chuyện” thay vì mô tả hệ.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-aaab-cf85742406ae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-9dc8-c9dca12ce7e2" class="">2) 12 attractors điển hình (điểm hút/chu kỳ hút) của hệ C6→C7</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-ac03-cf066755d966" class="">Gọi một attractor là mẫu động lực học ổn định của :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ac88-d35a1ed3b99d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">hội tụ về một vùng (fixed-point / basin)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-8996-d0b5fd6e2e93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">hoặc dao động quanh một chu kỳ (limit cycle)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-ac6c-cfcbfdf4526c" class="">A1. 
C6 “High-Gain Oscillation” (dao động khuếch đại cao)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8ece-f898c3d8a19d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dấu: cao; giảm dần</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9f7d-cfff2aaa4750" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Nhận diện: khủng hoảng theo chu kỳ ngắn, không giải quyết gốc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9523-dfa6475a60a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{eff} \uparrow,\quad D_{eff} \downarrow,\quad \text{cycle length} \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-bb26-d7b150c17c16" class="">A2.
