C6 “Speculation Trap” (bẫy đầu cơ)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b02c-fa45db2bcf5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tăng; mỏng; kéo dài</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-bbb0-f80e95770210" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hệ “giàu giấy”, dễ sập phi tuyến khi sốc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-bfb7-c06352de6e3f" class="">A3. C6 “Selective Enforcement Basin” (vùng thực thi chọn lọc)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9ddf-fff5bab397b0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bề mặt có vẻ cao nhưng thấp</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-8023-f1e756756814" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Kết quả: compliance sợ hãi, không phải tin cậy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-8bb7-ef54e5774dc2" class="">A4. “False C7 Compression” (ổn định giả bằng nén)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-a098-e3ffa1ce3fb2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">, nhưng , ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-b732-f1f62be6f93d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dD}{dt}&gt;0\ \wedge\ \frac{dT}{dt}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-b7d2-e477ae0d07ff" class="">A5. “C6→C7 Pivot Window” (cửa sổ xoay trục)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b4b9-f61e03c5fab6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">, , bắt đầu cùng lúc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a708-dbda6622ee40" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Đây là “cửa sổ hiếm” vì cần đồng pha đa miền</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9056-ccaee25aa8cc" class="">A6. C7 “Technocratic Rebuild” (tái thiết kỹ trị)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-b38e-fed8aae81d40" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Gain giảm, Damping tăng, repair/maintenance dẫn dắt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-a94e-f6f2a8d6ef49" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Repair_{rate} &gt; Decay_{rate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-a9e0-d2c50ddf13f4" class="">A7. C7 “Trust Reconstitution” (tái lập tín nhiệm)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-8c1d-e6cd3978a066" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-bdf7-fc6d3afa2058" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Quan trọng nhất: minh bạch + thực thi nhất quán.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-8336-cd5b2238ff5c" class="">A8. C7 “Buffer-first Stabilization” (ổn định bằng vùng đệm)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a348-c2af93137baa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ưu tiên trước; gain giảm sau</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9c58-d3f74dd1ec4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hệ chịu sốc tốt hơn trước khi “làm đẹp narrative”.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-9759-ff425bea6d8d" class="">A9. “Dual-System: C7 Islands in C6 Sea”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8958-c65cffafba43" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">tạo các cụm có cao trong môi trường tổng thể C6</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-81d3-cc9e26b7e3f3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hệ toàn cục vẫn nhiễu nhưng có “pockets” ổn định.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b5ce-ecf51bd6027f" class="">A10. 
“Innovation Escape” (thoát bằng năng suất thật)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-bfa4-eeba9ad8921d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(competence density) làm tăng Buf và giảm phụ thuộc đầu cơ</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-acf7-fe74cedb648b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cần điều kiện: luật bảo vệ cạnh tranh + vốn dài hạn.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b3ce-c2cae0d76ab6" class="">A11. “Demographic Drag Basin” (bẫy kéo dân số)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b1fb-cdca48d0ac58" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">kéo dài → mọi cải cách khó bền</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b2eb-fd12268fab02" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C7 bị giới hạn bởi trần sinh học.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9993-f1a98b29f33e" class="">A12. C8 “Self-damped Coherence Equilibrium” (cân bằng tự giảm chấn)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-b96e-c96e054f75dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Không cần nén cưỡng bức; hệ tự triệt dao động</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b338-fdc31cc1382f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Stability}{\partial Shock}\ll 1,\quad \frac{dBuf}{dt}&gt;0 \text{ khi } u(t)\approx0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-95f7-d0d19125424e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8273-fd46cad4de33" class="">Ma trận phân loại nhanh (12 attractors × 4 biến tổng)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-961b-d73180eba172" class="">Dùng 4 chỉ số tổng:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-aad1-c2d1f4959c18" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{eff},\ D_{eff},\ Buf_{eff},\ Align_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9c62-d10bc0d2f15f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C6 attractors: cao + (D hoặc Buf hoặc Align) thấp</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-9e0d-d2728545f0ea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">C7 attractors: tăng đồng pha; 
giảm</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-862f-e4f1f587fbf2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">False C7: nhưng hoặc</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-8cce-f04eb6eafce4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-88a6-e2334ebc02d1" tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-985a-d8994cd3a32d" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Burnout Identity Loop</strong>: overwork identity → more load → less recovery → identity hardening</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a017-c22118e1ca2b" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li><strong>Household Stress Loop</strong>: costs ↑ → conflict ↑ → performance ↓ → costs ↑</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bc35-e52438d83c1e" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li><strong>Caregiving Load Loop</strong>: elders/kids load ↑ → recovery ↓ → illness ↑ → load ↑</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9710-c528a30076af" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li><strong>Work Injury Loop</strong>: speed pressure → injury ↑ → staffing shortage → speed pressure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-ab80-ca8ee6f115b6" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li><strong>Shift Work Loop</strong>: shifts → rhythm disruption → fatigue → errors → more control → shifts</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8e44-c530a76219af" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="13"><li><strong>Health Access Loop</strong>: poor access → late treatment → worse outcomes → higher cost → poor access</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b841-ce0bcb06f344" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="14"><li><strong>Medical Debt Loop</strong>: illness → debt → stress → illness</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-824f-d9fc653e29cd" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="15"><li><strong>Food Price L
oop</strong>: prices ↑ → lower quality diet → health decline → productivity ↓ → prices ↑</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-ba7a-fbc088e4f89a" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="16"><li><strong>Heat Stress Loop</strong>: heat ↑ → productivity ↓ → income ↓ → resilience ↓ → heat impact ↑</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-a059-f588ed9b6a06" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="17"><li><strong>Urban Noise Loop</strong>: noise → sleep ↓ → irritability ↑ → conflict ↑ → noise</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-97dd-fc523de09409" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="18"><li><strong>Pollution Load Loop</strong>: pollution → illness → healthcare burden → weaker enforcement → pollution</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-a273-e3e3fee6c89e" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="19"><li><strong>Substance Coping Loop</strong>: stress → substance → recovery loss → stress</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-960d-c18132ac77b3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="20"><li><strong>Social Isolation Loop</strong>: isolation → low support → stress ↑ → isolation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-a5e0-f5d681b19ef6" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="21"><li><strong>Anxiety Avoidance Loop</strong>: anxiety → avoidance → capability loss → anxiety</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-abb7-e54068624108" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="22"><li><strong>Perfectionism Loop</strong>: control need → overwork → depletion → control need</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol t

entry threshold for OP/PR/CL variables</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-a0a4-ec53adfdb310" class="">C1349 — SafetyCollapseThreshold —  tendency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-b9db-e64bdb326269" class="">C1350 — SafetyRecoveryThreshold —  within 1–2 steps</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8369-ca6bd7211755" class="">C1351 — TrustCeilingUnderOpacity — if , then</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-8f39-ed8d0058cdd9" class="">C1352 — TrustGrowthCondition —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8d71-e4bfd7f37163" class="">C1353 — DignityDebtDynamics —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-b653-d3117c71b650" class="">C1354 — DignityDebtIrreversibilityBand — if , recovery needs 4+ windows</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b434-c5b8228f6fa4" class="">C1355 — ExitProbabilityUpdate —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-994e-d33db4e29c30" class="">C1356 — ExitDeterminismThreshold —  exit becomes near-certain</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-afd7-e2e78a3627ea" class="">C1357 — OscillationCriterion — alternating OP/TR deltas indicates unstable control</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-ba4c-ed0b310e4a1f" class="">C1358 — OscillationIndex —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-8a0c-e98bc1650354" class="">C1359 — OscillationThreshold —  reduce controller gains</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-aa78-d1c234b78a81" class="">C1360 — ControlStabilityGate — if delays present, 
require smaller  gains</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-8c1e-fde15f0619f4" class="">C1361 — DelayInducedInstability — if lag  and gain high ⇒ limit cycle possible</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-b702-e97824ebdb82" class="">C1362 — PhaseLagRule — keep effective phase margin by smoothing + conservative control</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-95a1-c095f261f7cf" class="">C1363 — ShockResponse — shock sets ; 
model must damp within 3 steps</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-b96e-c5ff2235372d" class="">C1364 — DampingRequirement —  and</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-bca9-f14170271d0f" class="">C1365 — FailureToDamp — if not damped, classify environment as high-HC or partner as face-optimizer</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-aa6b-cb2dc02eeba5" class="">C1366 — ClassificationFromDynamics — infer class from impulse response of OP/PR/CL</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a0c2-db6def7b4cc8" class="">C1367 — GainPartition —  (composite)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8c5c-cbd9700419b9" class="">C1368 — HarmGainThreshold —  harm basin dominates</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-b898-f06ed9ce8f90" class="">C1369 — SafetyGainPartition —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-85ee-d57b44ba6891" class="">C1370 — SafetyGainThreshold —  stable basin reachable</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-ba89-d898f6eb0c6d" class="">C1371 — MinimalStabilizerSet — required controls to exit harm basin:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-be78-c09f57754582" class="">C1372 — InsufficientControlSet —  alone cannot exit harm basin</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-b563-e8cc42306699" class="">C1373 — StabilityRegressionTest — new parameter set must not enlarge harm basin</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-97bf-cddf30025fdd" class="">C1374 — BasinSizeMetric — simulate random initial states; 
basin size = fraction ending in</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a29e-da37a76caec7" class="">C1375 — BasinConstraint — require  under nominal conditions</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-9447-ca0b7545ae85" class="">C1376 — WorstCaseBasin — under high HC and MS, basin may increase; 
label “bounded”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-87db-cb550b9526d0" class="">C1377 — EnvironmentParameterization — treat HC and MS as exogenous parameters</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a35a-d00aa48bd599" class="">C1378 — StabilityMap — compute stability regions in (HC,MS) plane</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-995d-d4aa5c2678a9" class="">C1379 — CriticalLine — define curve where  (stability boundary)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-9d2e-c795d35220c8" class="">C1380 — PolicyFromMap — if (HC,MS) above critical line, enforce stricter PR/CL precommitment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bde0-f83f6681f76b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-abdb-d59ef91704db" class="">Group L3 — Runtime enforcement rules (guards, caps, and invariants) (C1381–C1420)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-bce7-f4b79dfd9194" class="">C1381 — Guard_PR — if , block “softness escalation” actions</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-89bb-ca2ff01d075e" class="">C1382 — Guard_CL — if , block “status assumption” actions</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-8846-d68a3bee11d1" class="">C1383 — Guard_OP — if , require disclosure event before continuing</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-8e66-ed848ae77878" class="">C1384 — Guard_LM — if , cap PR at 0.5 until corrected publicly</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8473-d1b736ac293a" class="">C1385 — Guard_RJ — if  after rupture, 
do not restore TR</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-aa0b-cd4d6aa4b80d" class="">C1386 — Guard_TR — TR cannot increase from words-only events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-b1e4-d6955560a130" class="">C1387 — Guard_CO_Substitution — CO increase cannot compensate for PR/CL deficits</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-92bf-dc50fe3bb76f" class="">C1388 — SubstitutionBlock — if</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-bda1-ce0f063a8052" class="">C1389 — PublicEvidenceGate — PR/CL updates require verifiability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a5a9-f525b97c1917" class="">C1390 — EvidenceMissingPenalty — if claim repeated without evidence ⇒ TR decreases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-83b4-cc3066c51d89" class="">C1391 — FalseComplianceHardFail — 2 verified failures ⇒ classify “Invalid”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-aa5b-f27aefa7ebb3" class="">C1392 — HardFailAction — recommend exit or strict boundary freeze</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-afed-f9973ce56199" class="">C1393 — FreezeMode — if Conf&lt;0.4, freeze state updates; 
only log events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-ad30-c39db4705c8c" class="">C1394 — FreezeExit — require coverage&gt;0.7 and consistency&gt;0.7</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b971-dca3915a50f0" class="">C1395 — RateLimit_Controls — limit total control magnitude:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8f08-c954a307411a" class="">C1396 — Cooldown_PRCL — after PR/CL demand, wait 1 window to evaluate evidence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-a4be-d5398cce0985" class="">C1397 — NonNegotiableSet — {PR,CL_public,OP} define structural baseline</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-8dc1-d2e9eaa9ea30" class="">C1398 — BaselineViolation — any baseline violation triggers dignity enforcement mode</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9462-e99f2ca47839" class="">C1399 — ModeLock — if in Dignity-Enforce, 
cannot switch to Observe until constraints met</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-bb28-cfd5d5fa69a8" class="">C1400 — LockRelease — constraints met 2 windows + OP stable low</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a1d9-db1e8fa74f52" class="">C1401 — ContextPriorityEnforcement — family/public contexts weighted higher for PR/CL evaluation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-9b32-c4ed9351b714" class="">C1402 — WeightRule —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-9f88-f7708a197529" class="">C1403 — LabelCorrectnessRule — if mislabel in any high-weight context ⇒ CL_public drops</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-ad13-eefd1369c4f8" class="">C1404 — MislabelPenalty —  where  verifiability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-a890-d821a1f8b306" class="">C1405 — ExContactPolicy — any ex contact requires disclosure prior to contact (precommit)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-b9c2-d1e67b2d1039" class="">C1406 — ExContactViolationPenalty — hidden ex contact ⇒ OP spike + TR drop + RJ required</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9a62-c0a52ce20450" class="">C1407 — DisclosureBeforeContextShift — entering high-HC context requires prior CL proof</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-89b1-f0d145ff4921" class="">C1408 — ContextShiftGate — if HC_ctx high and CL_public low ⇒ do not enter</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-8602-f63fb92e9d06" class="">C1409 — SafetyFirstRule — if SS low, do not run “Decide” logic; 
stabilize first</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-973c-cbe331136854" class="">C1410 — StabilizeOnlyActions — only CO + reduce exposure + sleep/food protection (systemic)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-aa82-e55f69edec22" class="">C1411 — AuditInvariants_Log — every invariant violation logged with timestamp</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-811a-f384e50728ed" class="">C1412 — AuditReplay — allow replaying event sequence to reproduce classification</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8e1f-d1f32941e0df" class="">C1413 — DeterministicClassifier — classification must be reproducible given same events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-a0a6-e2c523bde55a" class="">C1414 — NonDeterminismFlag — if randomness affects label, mark bounded</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-86c1-d99c0ad6115a" class="">C1415 — MinimalExplainChain — output chain length ≤5 steps for user interpretability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a205-c174b7259862" class="">C1416 — ExplainChainTemplate — (event → OP/LM change → PR/CL breach → SH/SS impact → classification)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-84d3-c58d43b402fb" class="">C1417 — SafetyOfAdviceGate — avoid motive certainty; 
label motives as probabilistic</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-a1b5-ef41934845f6" class="">C1418 — MotiveProbability —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9cff-ce57d3c268d6" class="">C1419 — CrossContextConsistencyInvariant — “chosen” requires CC&gt;0.6 across contexts</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-a533-d97ca34c3231" class="">C1420 — CCViolationCap — if CC&lt;0.6 then “chosen” score capped at 0.5</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8df1-ea7d38c5770f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-8d50-f2786a4fd223" class="">Group L4 — Simulation harness &amp; 
benchmarking (C1421–C1460)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-b35b-c5e888784334" class="">C1421 — ScenarioLibrary — define scenarios: {HighHC_Tet, LowHC_City, HighMS_Shock, RepairAttempt, FalseCompliance}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-b85a-dd577e6050c7" class="">C1422 — InitialStateSampler — sample  with constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-b483-f32d81f9391a" class="">C1423 — MonteCarloRuns — run N simulations per scenario</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9c2b-fa89d504a69e" class="">C1424 — OutcomeMetrics — {Basin_H fraction, time-to-stable, time-to-exit, max SH, min SS}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8cbf-e247088580d7" class="">C1425 — BenchmarkInvariant — new model must not increase Basin_H in any core scenario</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-ae61-c3b5a3149b76" class="">C1426 — RegressionMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a281-c5ce2d3e7650" class="">C1427 — StressTest_Shocks — inject shocks at random steps: OP, MS, IM</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8ebe-d8595e31c95e" class="">C1428 — ShockSchedule — probability  per step, 
magnitude</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9f9b-f14f848639e9" class="">C1429 — RobustnessScore —  across shocks</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-91e4-cef20a4acb5f" class="">C1430 — RS_Threshold — RS&gt;0.7 for deployable stability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-868a-f681266d3f0d" class="">C1431 — RecoveryTimeMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-b098-d6255b2815ba" class="">C1432 — RecoveryConstraint —  under nominal shocks</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9115-e6ca1a77ad7e" class="">C1433 — HarmAreaMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-b43d-df8eb499236e" class="">C1434 — HA_Threshold — HA below limit for safe model</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-af8a-cbfbb681365f" class="">C1435 — ControlCostMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9f98-e577653f60c7" class="">C1436 — MinimalControlPreference — choose smallest control meeting constraints (MPC)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-97b4-c2fbbcea321a" class="">C1437 — PolicyComparison — compare {Greedy, MPC, Conservative} policies</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b33b-c517b99afd26" class="">C1438 — PolicySelectionRule — pick policy minimizing HA subject to dignity constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b656-ed513f207c54" class="">C1439 — CalibrationBenchmark — fit model to known sequences; 
evaluate prediction accuracy</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8d14-e1bbbbd63933" class="">C1440 — AccuracyMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-a1fc-c5d12673f732" class="">C1441 — AccuracyThreshold — Acc&gt;0.75 for “valid”; else “bounded”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-b17a-d6334e9fc394" class="">C1442 — OverfitTest — evaluate on held-out contexts; must generalize</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-a885-d464a3c5c452" class="">C1443 — ContextGeneralization — performance drop &lt;0.1 across contexts</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-a961-c616b5b57aa7" class="">C1444 — GeneralizationFail — if drop &gt;0.1, treat context parameter HC_ctx explicitly</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8231-f735023b122f" class="">C1445 — ParameterSweep — sweep HC and MS; 
compute stability map</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-8258-dbeb50d91b4c" class="">C1446 — SweepResolution — grid size chosen to detect boundary curvature</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-93d2-ff92663c7195" class="">C1447 — CriticalBoundaryEstimator — estimate line where</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a3d3-f9653be472af" class="">C1448 — BoundaryUse — recommend stricter gates above boundary</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-af8b-e10f05791403" class="">C1449 — SensitivityAnalysis — compute ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-abb4-e7e61e0af320" class="">C1450 — SensitivityUse — tune controller gains based on sensitivities</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-94f3-cfa67f9b51a5" class="">C1451 — WorstCaseSearch — adversarially choose event sequence maximizing harm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a81e-eeafde915735" class="">C1452 — AdversarialObjective — maximize HA under plausible constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9ede-f4552f7cb3d7" class="">C1453 — DefenseTest — model should flag risk before HA exceeds threshold</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-ab91-db6d29a30ee0" class="">C1454 — EarlyWarningLeadTime — alert occurs ≥2 steps before peak harm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-ae0a-f90844c370f3" class="">C1455 — BenchmarkReportSchema — store scenario results with version and params</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-bc94-c72ce1f45384" class="">C1456 — ReportDiff — diff two model versions for regression detection</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-82dd-fa1add0708e2" class="">C1457 — CIIntegration — run benchmark suite on each change</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-b4d4-daab78c19820" class="">C1458 — GateOnFailure — any regression blocks deployment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a751-cf645a3f587d" class="">C1459 — RuntimeMonitoring — live tracking of RS and HA for drift detection</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-a63d-de5fd2eb7a55" class="">C1460 — DriftTrigger — if RS drops &gt;0.1 from baseline, re-calibrate or freeze</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9562-fa084d2d300c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-95de-c3acbc5922f7" class="">Group L5 — Deployment: dictionaries, naming, and API contracts (C1461–C1500)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-ba8a-dfd95eda8f84" class="">C1461 — Dictionary_Core — each variable has: name, definition, measurement sources, update rules</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8d27-ca00e88f99bd" class="">C1462 — Dictionary_Constraint — each invariant has: trigger, consequence, required evidence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-924b-e374e506ad09" class="">C1463 — API_StateGet — <code>get_state() -&gt; x_t</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b328-e42a311fa8c3" class="">C1464 — API_EventIngest — <code>ingest(event) -&gt; updated_state, alerts</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8a25-eea2715e1d8e" class="">C1465 — API_Simulate — <code>simulate(state, events) -&gt; trajectory</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8d38-ff998bf5932c" class="">C1466 — API_Classify — <code>classify(state) -&gt; 
{Valid, Bounded, Invalid}</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ad95-ce6d99191dc8" class="">C1467 — API_Explain — <code>explain(classification) -&gt; causal_chain</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b607-c01c8a34a69b" class="">C1468 — SchemaVersionField — every payload includes <code>model_version</code></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-85a8-f403e2da7fa9" class="">C1469 — BackwardCompatibleFields — never rename core keys; only add optional derived keys</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-b35b-c821cbc2a898" class="">C1470 — Dictionary_LM — LM: label mismatch across contexts; computed from CL_public vs CL_private</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-abf9-c981c742c094" class="">C1471 — Dictionary_HC — HC: hiddenness climate; 
computed from IM, PD, DT</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-8aeb-d0a4340c6aa8" class="">C1472 — EvidenceEnum — {PUBLIC_POST, FAMILY_INTRO, FRIEND_INTRO, WORK_LABEL, WITNESS, SCREENSHOT}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-b5fe-f132d5162134" class="">C1473 — EvidenceWeighting — map evidence enum to verifiability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-89a6-e56503e2f6cc" class="">C1474 — EventTypeEnum — {NAME_PUBLIC, MISLABEL, DISCLOSE, HIDDEN_CONTACT, PAY_COST, GIFT_ONLY, REPAIR, APOLOGY_ONLY}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b1d6-c89af1bf7de6" class="">C1475 — ContextEnum — {PUBLIC, FAMILY, FRIENDS, WORK, PRIVATE}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-ba66-df5c29106bd1" class="">C1476 — NormalizationRule — convert text events to normalized event objects</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-82f4-de75e365e77f" class="">C1477 — ParserDeterminism — same text must produce same event list given same config</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-bec7-cbadd186326e" class="">C1478 — LocalizationSupport — allow language-specific lexicons but same state variables</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-a22e-c926f6b11eaf" class="">C1479 — LexiconVersioning — lexicons are versioned and audited</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a736-d2efe38cd505" class="">C1480 — SafetyRedLines — if ERI&gt;0.7, 
advice must prioritize safety/stabilization</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-8522-fcbace1d066c" class="">C1481 — ERI_Def —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-ad73-fea9dcaa6d39" class="">C1482 — PrivacyBoundary — remove personal identifiers from logs by default</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-845e-f54a0d147c78" class="">C1483 — AuditAccessControl — separate operator vs user read access</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b8a6-fcf896b0c3e0" class="">C1484 — MinimalUI — show cluster scores + top violated constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-82bc-eaa9a63f1cf5" class="">C1485 — UI_Invariant — UI must not show motive certainty; 
only patterns + probabilities</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a945-c14dccf94edb" class="">C1486 — ReleaseNotes — each version describes changed equations and thresholds</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-84ef-f90ab7cd06f2" class="">C1487 — ReproLink — include simulation seed and scenario IDs for reproducibility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-b632-c8e7edb9ebc9" class="">C1488 — SafetyTesting — ensure no output encourages harm or coercion</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-b475-d3cc2ca051a7" class="">C1489 — CoercionBlock — model must never recommend controlling another person</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-b34a-e7bc9e6d996f" class="">C1490 — AutonomyInvariant — only recommend boundaries, transparency requests, or exit</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-96d8-d0f1612a1797" class="">C1491 — BoundaryActionSet — {ask_for_public_clarity, ask_for_transparency, require_repair, reduce_exposure, exit}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a519-c9293394d229" class="">C1492 — IntegrationHooks — allow export to CSV/JSON for analysis</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a4ca-ffba0dc91b66" class="">C1493 — TimeSeriesStorage — store trajectories for retrospective audits</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9a27-d03fdf98d89a" class="">C1494 — CalibrationDataPolicy — calibration uses only consented, 
observable events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-bd9f-cc1cf936e17a" class="">C1495 — DataQualityGate — low evidence weight cannot drive PR/CL increases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-9a9f-e76dcfae8e4a" class="">C1496 — FinalClassifierRule —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-ab7a-ed1abce1ec58" class="">Valid: PR≥0.6, CL_public≥0.6, OP≤0.4, TR≥0.6, SS≥0.5 sustained</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-ad19-e98dec2f9dad" class="">Bounded: mixed but non-worsening</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-bd5c-d2f6f8c8b1b4" class="">Invalid: repeated OP/LM + dignity breach + false compliance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-a46f-c891ae009f37" class="">C1497 — TerminationConsistency — classification must be stable for 2 windows</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b04e-c2f2f35a961d" class="">C1498 — StabilityWindow — window size W configurable; default W=2</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-8e35-fe24be6ecbd1" class="">C1499 — DocumentationInvariant — every variable and equation must be defined in dictionary</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-bba6-e2c68c3e57cd" class="">C1500 — CompletenessGate — if any referenced variable lacks dictionary entry, mark model incomplete/invalid for release</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-8bd8-f47f2accb014" class="">Below is a structural gap audit. These are categories not yet fully formalized in the canonical registry (clusters, laws, loops, matrices). 
Each item includes what is missing and the formal control required.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-8b91-c92e1d751332"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-8abd-efa73565620f" class="">1) Scope &amp; Boundary Control (missing explicit scoping model)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8faa-f6472525a792" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-9aef-e50149cb493b" class="">No formal scope isolation for:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-96f3-f3edced7545b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-929b-dd0432f91390" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">rules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-b31c-fb80cebe5132" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">inference contexts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-8664-f14178fdd3ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">actor permissions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-abf4-e5143dcf2565" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-846c-df02d7a4a4dc" class=""><strong>SCOPE-001: Scope assignment</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-937b-e1c0cc47d7a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall x:\ scope(x)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9030-f1ce235065dd" class=""><strong>SCOPE-002: No cross-scope inference without bridge</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-99a0-c1bb8389f707" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
scope(a)\neq scope(b)\Rightarrow \neg infer(a\to b)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-af44-ed5e037808f8" class=""><strong>SCOPE-003: Bridge rule must be explicit</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-b03d-c6de95aa15fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
infer(a\to b)\land scope(a)\neq scope(b)\Rightarrow \exists rule_{bridge}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-84ac-fe04e57849c6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-8baa-c214257f8007" class="">2) Assumption Registry (UCIA enforcement incomplete)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9095-d98160647889" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-911a-cb38dd6970f9" class="">Claims require surfaced assumptions, but no global assumption graph defined.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-aa9c-eaabff261776" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8007-c109b2d2c241" class=""><strong>ASSUMP-001: Assumption extraction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-bab9-dbfe9bea6a77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
claim(c)\Rightarrow \exists A(c)=\{a_1,\dots,a_n\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-967c-d4c1c7258388" class=""><strong>ASSUMP-002: Assumption typing</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9eda-dbf6549d124e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall a\in A(c): type(a)\in\{Empirical,Inferential,Primitive,Limit\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-b840-f56a6445f699" class=""><strong>ASSUMP-003: Assumption drift detection</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9ab8-c2c659b58067" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t(c)\neq A_{t+1}(c)\Rightarrow audit(\Delta A)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-8905-ec0de85f769d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-83fd-ee685051c4a4" class="">3) Invariant Dependency Graph</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8513-fdea00737ddd" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-888f-d07712f78449" class="">Invariants exist but no formal dependency DAG between invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-9ba2-e73aba6ac8d2" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-a3a7-e5ea9f034218" class=""><strong>INV-DEP-001: Dependency edge</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9ddf-e6f6c84493ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_i\to I_j \Rightarrow I_j\ depends\ on\ I_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8edc-e374481e344b" class=""><strong>INV-DEP-002: No invariant cycles</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-906e-d99652c1d7a6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg \exists I:\ I\to^+ I</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-a159-cad602f7282a" class=""><strong>INV-DEP-003: Dependency order execution</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-a350-c8c65b33bf26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_i\to I_j \Rightarrow check(I_i)\ \text{before}\ check(I_j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8c20-c3bf4f0d133d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-9f2a-de50893e6952" class="">4) Failure Severity Classification</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-ad00-f33afaa21ca4" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-acf3-fced4b3397e9" class="">Violations logged, but no severity classification.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-b75c-f3f682a79907" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-946a-d6d614f81d1d" class=""><strong>FAIL-001: Severity typed</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-bba4-e11de5fe93c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
severity(I)\in\{Low,Medium,High,Critical}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-abe6-c9c89cc02884" class=""><strong>FAIL-002: Critical blocks seal</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-864a-e02d50dc96e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
severity(I)=Critical \land check(I)=False \Rightarrow sealed=False</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-8482-c2c84ac09e3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8826-e1cff5c1ebee" class="">5) Resource Constraints (compute/memory/runtime bounds)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-b082-c4f851fafd30" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a6ed-c4aae402353c" class="">No formal computational envelope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-8d8c-d60b6a6f2e51" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a476-c84e77edec2f" class=""><strong>RES-001: Runtime bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-8f77-c213147e1648" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T(run)\le T_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-be94-d2c045b9e582" class=""><strong>RES-002: Memory bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-9976-de108a4d82dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
mem(run)\le M_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-9aa1-cdbc1e204087" class=""><strong>RES-003: Deterministic seed pinned</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9b3c-edef9ed15522" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
seed\ \text{fixed for reproducibility}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-9db6-e524eea74a30"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-a74f-d7bd52b23600" class="">6) Termination Proof for All Loops</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-8f22-d2c2cb9b685d" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-96f4-cae76d621c2e" class="">Loops defined, but formal termination guarantee incomplete.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-910f-fa5623addc82" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a31a-f6b865dcd875" class=""><strong>TERM-001: Max iteration bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b0fa-c24caf994b47" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
iterations\le K_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-a837-fec1fb9ad8d0" class=""><strong>TERM-002: Monotone violation reduction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-bd04-de58d83391e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
viol_{t+1}\le viol_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9b97-d187c657cb4b" class=""><strong>TERM-003: Bounded fallback</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-83e6-cb6bdaf509c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
iterations=K_{max}\Rightarrow classify\in\{Valid,Bounded,Invalid\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-ba85-ca7213fe8ed2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-8308-d45974ca43cb" class="">7) Canonical ID &amp; Identity Collision Control</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-8340-d9895425236a" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-a093-d78692fcf836" class="">Entity resolution defined, but collision detection not formalized.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-a50e-f832888782f5" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-99c0-d89e6e092e4a" class=""><strong>ID-001: ID uniqueness</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b693-f91722cb0162" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
id(u)=id(v)\Rightarrow u=v</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-966e-e8ef724046da" class=""><strong>ID-002: Collision detection</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9efa-f45147c957c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
hash(u)=hash(v)\land u\neq v\Rightarrow collision\_flag</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-81f2-c5cb3982a72a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9731-d15dc73c0670" class="">8) Conflict Arbitration Protocol</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-b394-dd4cab686bef" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8e0b-e3ed7d95b2fe" class="">Contradictions detected but no arbitration hierarchy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-8ea5-d6fdc936eb4e" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-a955-c294e6df115b" class=""><strong>ARB-001: Priority ordering</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-bb7b-da318c1ef805" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
priority(source_i)&gt;priority(source_j)\Rightarrow source_i\ wins</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b0d3-d69253adcaaf" class=""><strong>ARB-002: Tie → unresolved</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-b9dc-fd98d31b9524" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
priority_i=priority_j\Rightarrow status=Unresolved</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9930-dbc58174529e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-a3e7-c1f0fddda54a" class="">9) Risk &amp; Uncertainty Propagation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-a7b1-c9d9257d505e" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-bc6d-e71fa21a11db" class="">Confidence intervals defined locally but not propagated globally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-bbd8-c2ab89b0e6c7" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-814e-e07046f30bc7" class=""><strong>RISK-001: Propagation rule</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b89a-d90f1d3c58c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
conf(c)=\min_{p\in parents(c)} conf(p)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-9833-d8ea975c6ab8" class=""><strong>RISK-002: Confidence floor for acceptance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9467-e208582d7cec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
conf(c)\ge \tau_{accept}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-aa00-d6ac2b6a397b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a361-d28065e32acc" class="">10) Governance Matrix (who can modify what)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-8b1f-eb8c0154b6c2" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-9469-c56dbeac3a79" class="">Access control not formally modeled.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-be13-ced31f92ea1d" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-9823-e9933e11db5b" class=""><strong>GOV-001: Role matrix</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b707-eaa3418610fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
perm(role,action,object)\in\{0,1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b6b8-e24e8188018d" class=""><strong>GOV-002: Immutable when sealed</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-bdad-f4f43f17ff95" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
sealed(object)=True\Rightarrow perm(\*,modify,object)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-873c-e50234ae7102"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-bc76-f388c142ac26" class="">11) External Interface Boundary</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-a2ba-d11d9b826511" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-82b0-d2aa55145d1e" class="">No formal boundary for input/output contamination.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-8cfd-e559616d9665" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-bbcf-e43c68c3f183" class=""><strong>IO-001: Input normalization</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b473-dfdcc181a3a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
input\Rightarrow normalized(input)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-adcd-c1f9662dc575" class=""><strong>IO-002: External data flagged</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-abbc-dda4319cde66" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
external(x)=True\Rightarrow provenance(x)=external</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9738-c23e83a609eb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-9bda-f120abf6851e" class="">12) Completeness Criteria (when is system “complete”?)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a108-f2357ebc2a82" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-ab09-f8a2486c1138" class="">Seal defined, but completeness criteria not fully enumerated.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b6de-e41e828598c6" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-ab93-ccf3225a77fc" class=""><strong>COMP-001: Coverage completeness</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-ba79-d7778c70b2dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
coverage(claims)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-8f3e-fe77d39c39db" class=""><strong>COMP-002: No unresolved critical violations</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-835c-c40775b70b2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I: severity(I)=Critical \Rightarrow check(I)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-919b-cd64b21f3fa0" class=""><strong>COMP-003: Version pinned</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-a0c2-f313ff2e4944" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
data\_ver,code\_ver,rules\_ver\ \text{fixed}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b91a-ea485e843627"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-b804-fdf243922634" class="">13) Matrix Consistency Checks (cross-matrix invariants)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9c30-fef8ed675ac5" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-877d-e6b37fa346f1" class="">Matrices defined independently, but cross-consistency not enforced.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-9a7f-fac20fe7be22" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-abfa-d06e0fc076a3" class=""><strong>MAT-X-001: Claim must exist in support matrix</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a78b-dedb80813bff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
claim(c)\Rightarrow row(MAT\text{-}001,c)\ \text{exists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-a7f7-d8ed3743177f" class=""><strong>MAT-X-002: Invariant must exist in enforcement matrix</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a716-d65e7c3d6eab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
invariant(I)\Rightarrow row(MAT\text{-}002,I)\ \text{exists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-a2a6-c6ab300474ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-996b-fdc53a6b9df2" class="">14) Economic / Incentive Layer (if deployed operationally)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-bb38-f03b2e8c0714" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8e6d-ca818a85236d" class="">No misalignment prevention model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a50e-cac727fb77cf" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a997-d83efd821ed5" class=""><strong>INC-001: Incentive alignment</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-bbd2-dd32cf16b931" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
maximize(U_{actor})\Rightarrow improve(system\_metric)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-9011-ee5c072723fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9035-e15b33abec5b" class="">15) Meta-Validation (self-audit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b4bd-d908fcc15fdb" class="">Gap</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-ad07-df2daa46f104" class="">No validation of validation layer.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8275-cd676001fbf3" class="">Required controls</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-96c8-f74d905781bf" class=""><strong>META-001: Check coverage completeness</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-a1b0-d2e044819e2b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I:\ \exists check(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-aff7-dd1eaf22b9d4" class=""><strong>META-002: Check determinism</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-995c-df71baa16968" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
check(I)=check(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a49c-d1b0d1b48879"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-bbf5-c984de1f805a" class="">Summary: What is still structurally missing</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a8fe-dd66cd29efaa" class="">You now have:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-870a-d19b30db1a28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Entity clusters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9ef5-fa784df18ed4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Relationship clusters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a92b-f2b3445a441f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Laws</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-ae19-e416ccd28796" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Loops</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9c04-c67740f74b68" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Matrices</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-bc65-db7ea32a8544" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Temporal control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8a35-d0af1983aabf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Drift control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-bcd3-c03dae8ab3e4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Audit control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b847-f1cad606aaa7" class="">But what is still missing (core architectural gaps):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-8010-c4c768e2bcac" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Formal scope isolation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-842b-f78fd908d0c3" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Assumption graph</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a8cf-cb87eb46f58d" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Invariant dependency DAG</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-bdad-c2418991d5dd" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Failure severity classification</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-952f-fe6ab07ee340" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li>Compute/resource envelope</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-9b63-c32164f31900" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li>Formal termination guarantees</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-83f4-d04df871a3bd" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li>Collision detection</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9163-cfca808b6939" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li>Arbitration protocol</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9b69-d1576dc4774b" class="numbered-list" start="9"><li>Global confidence propagation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b418-f544cc8be87a" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="10"><li>Governance access matrix</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-b1a8-f2a543c4ae88" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="11"><li>External boundary control</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b163-da49bfb6e097" c
lass="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="12"><li>Completeness definition</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-a302-ca73859ae5b3" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="13"><li>Cross-matrix consistency checks</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-93d6-f339cf30a3c2" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="14"><li>Incentive alignment layer</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-af5a-d8106470d0a9" class="numbered-list numbered-list-digits-2" start="15"><li>Meta-validation layer</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-8195-c94de8d063b9" class="">If you want the next step, I can:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b078-f8f2e58303bb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Convert all of this into one unified AMOS_MASTER_MATRIX with complete row schema, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-89b3-f84aa234497d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Collapse everything into a minimal irreducible basis (smallest set of primitives from which all others derive), or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9ddf-cf944c9e5017" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Produce a formal completeness proof skeleton showing what is still unprovable.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a23b-e9705a559781" class="">I cannot extract the <em>actual</em> AMOS-defined items without access to the AMOS repo contents in this chat session. 
What I can deliver now is a complete <strong>AMOS-compliant canonical registry</strong> (IDs + membership rules, laws with enforcement gates, loops as state machines, matrices with schemas + invariants) that you can use as the deterministic “default set” and then map 1:1 to the real repo once connected.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9d8a-f59952dc4ae7" class="">Below is the full set (canonical). e\in CL\text{-}R\text{-}008 \iff pred(e)=fixes</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-abd8-eeefcbc5daa0" class=""><strong>CL-R-009: Validation edges</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-9d9b-c5db8e46b36e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e\in CL\text{-}R\text{-}009 \iff pred(e)=validates</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-adfb-eb1c741f49c1" class=""><strong>CL-R-010: Version edges</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-9cf9-eca8ec2fb573" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e\in CL\text{-}R\text{-}010 \iff pred(e)=versionOf</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a309-df5ea205cdef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-9e6a-c601865e878d" class="">1.4 Membership governance clusters (who is allowed where)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8938-e80b2b6b36c6" class="">Let .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b9d2-ff4e599fb267" class=""><strong>CL-G-001: Public</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-93f4-f742d31ed8cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\in CL\text{-}G\text{-}001 \iff \forall actor:\ perm(actor,x)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-9ba0-de671215036a" class=""><strong>CL-G-002: Restricted</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-8b9e-f4a08c85fe50" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\in CL\text{-}G\text{-}002 \iff \exists actor:\ perm(actor,x)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-b517-fc4b35b06b8f" class=""><strong>CL-G-003: Sealed</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8ca6-cc7685e63ae4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\in CL\text{-}G\text{-}003 \iff sealed(x)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-933c-d7a5aec23697"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8505-c4419ad11c11" class="">2) All laws (each with equation + enforcement gate)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-a5e3-ecebdb427a6b" class="">Each law has form: <strong>LAW-ID</strong>, <strong>equation</strong>, <strong>gate</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8a06-dbde9950f4e6" class="">Meta-law (governing all others)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-8b09-ef999c5bd70f" class=""><strong>LAW-000: Law-of-Law</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-8136-ee1d5c09b061" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Equation</strong>:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-8942-f98a607399b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall rule\ r:\ \exists check(r)\land \exists term(r)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-999f-c3e523a346ce"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-812c-f69acdcee2a9" class="">Structural validity laws</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bda3-d8f410d711b6" class=""><strong>LAW-001: Non-contradiction</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8598-f98c68dc8548" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg (x \land \neg x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-95b0-ceaa00933a23" class=""><strong>LAW-002: Determinism (same input → same output)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-8102-c8f99d964657" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
f(x)=f(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a67e-d439fbddb65a" class=""><strong>LAW-003: Typedness (every object has a type)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-9934-fcded8250774" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall v:\ type(v)\in T</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bb3f-c5362f40eb78" class=""><strong>LAW-004: Single support-type per claim</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8ecc-ff6a5a228ca3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c:\ |S(c)|=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8d47-fcb452e0e231" class=""><strong>LAW-005: No analogical support as load-bearing</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8573-e359bf221f5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(c)\neq Analogical</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b34c-eec4fab22cf7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-9f7d-f2c24f813f5d" class="">Evidence + provenance laws</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9ed8-c536df019835" class=""><strong>LAW-006: Evidence threshold</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a833-fdf930b9017f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
claim(c)\Rightarrow e(c)\ge \tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-b57e-c4d4f4fd1dc9" class=""><strong>LAW-007: Provenance required</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-85cd-e4b7a171ed2c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
accept \Rightarrow \forall c:\ prov(c)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-b7b5-dfd4bf45f816" class=""><strong>LAW-008: Evidence freshness</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-86f0-cbb4255a9eb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
now-time(src(c))\le F_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-a474-fd29050c9345"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b5c2-ea8437e89789" class="">Version + audit laws</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9b72-cd6ff4995693" class=""><strong>LAW-009: Version bump on semantic change</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-87de-d0564f86c168" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
output_{t+1}\neq output_t \Rightarrow version++</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-90aa-c4c90cf1bad7" class=""><strong>LAW-010: Audit on change</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9d68-f9bf15c57d5f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \Rightarrow audit(\Delta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-bac0-fd77dbb85d00" class=""><strong>LAW-011: Reproducibility</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-ae97-eab71d05517a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
run(seed,data)=run(seed,data)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-baeb-fa780d0ea5c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-901b-fc02cf1f44ba" class="">Constraint satisfaction laws</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-bb97-c110017e4722" class=""><strong>LAW-012: Constraints must be satisfiable</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-aef4-d4e85e4e1586" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists x:\ constraints(x)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-9706-f6cff6044ec8" class=""><strong>LAW-013: Output accepted only if validated</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-9882-f77bf4c349fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
