accept \Rightarrow validate=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-884d-d98bd2d156d1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-bc41-c87443f49a65" class="">3) All loops (each as a state machine)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-afb6-c2dd84031c9c" class="">Notation: FSM</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-93ba-f697f19b0f7f" class="">LOOP-001: Enforcement Loop (core AMOS loop)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-b8f5-f16ce76f78b5" class=""><strong>States</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-957b-c0f1d677fff6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ingest</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-aead-c455c0ce1430" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Normalize</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-ba81-e20b9a958c94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ExtractClaims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-995b-fa7aa5388e25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">TypeSupport</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-9dba-e3fac5566785" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BuildGraph</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-84ae-cf08a6a78f79" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CheckInvariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-9ae2-db1feafc6307" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-bfc6-cc8e8fd77d8e" class="bulleted-list"><li s
tyle="list-style-type:disc">Recheck</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8d54-e1af6c8f6b9f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Seal</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8464-cfbe87ca60d1" class=""><strong>Transitions</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-b84e-e57d150693b9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>ingest_ok</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-a767-d411fae080f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>normalized</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8a80-dabbdcf49739" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>claims_extracted</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-a0bb-d8aba1191fbc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>typed</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-bb7f-efd650f589c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>graph_built</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-a909-d555341d7ef6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>all_checks_pass</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a50f-e450f48709a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>any_check_fail</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a41b-eea367cb11b5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>repair_applied</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a9bd-d346bb3c200b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <
code>recheck_pass</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-b7c2-dac090a729b2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">on <code>recheck_fail</code> (bounded by max steps)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-866c-fe5ad7e00617" class=""><strong>Start</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-87dd-f09981f2d0fd" class=""><strong>Accept</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-a20e-e8b1ecb21405" class=""><strong>Termination condition</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-a7a9-fa97e735ff46" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
steps\le S_{max} \ \land\ (\text{pass}\ \lor\ \text{declare bounded invalid})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-81ff-d2a400d84d4d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8216-c65003c85f1a" class="">LOOP-002: Drift-Closure Loop (ΔInternal / ΔFeedback)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-8505-ca1b1a98a07b" class=""><strong>States</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a8ad-d0277372cb53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ObserveDelta</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-87f7-cfa461cb9b45" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">AttributeCause</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-84e2-fd6a4de89f28" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SelectRepair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-b44c-fe9c4eaa0a50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ApplyRepair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-9fb1-e926736fae54" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">VerifyStability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b067-f0bcc99b3a78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">CommitVersion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8bc8-dbbdb84e38be" class=""><strong>Key variables</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9b9e-c9262143061f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{internal},\ \Delta_{feedback}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9828-c7cd4c6b836c" class=""><strong>Acceptance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-bc72-d698146fd748" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\Delta_{internal}|\le \epsilon \land |\Delta_{feedback}|\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-b0ee-f0f1c6fad8d8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-9a46-d43e17dd3508" class="">LOOP-003: Temporal Snapshot Loop</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a3fb-cc114c827a84" class=""><strong>States</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-8f92-d5148d3ad521" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">BuildSlice</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8e77-eb42f38575d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ValidateSlice</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8050-fb4c56f152ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">HashSlice</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b2d2-f072972f4054" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">StoreSnapshot</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-9fdf-f3452d94caa8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ReplayVerify</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-92bf-fabc0c00f129" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-8142-e8167912a611" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
verify(snapshot(t),hash)=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9bc3-f5fe93671507" class="">replay(t)=replay(t)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b5a9-c205da42e3f7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-9f45-e3d282b3add8" class="">LOOP-004: Release Loop (policy-governed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-abb3-f69604aa17bc" class=""><strong>States</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-b94a-d49f180cda5f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-bc91-d74f0e952e4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">RunChecks</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-828b-f1f9ff3703a9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Approve</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a497-c968858da0c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">SealRelease</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-89cf-cb7671aa885b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Publish</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b3d4-f9e841b4b8e6" class=""><strong>Acceptance</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-a305-e01ce39aa8b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
validate=True \land audit\_complete=True \land version\_pinned=True</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-8f10-c2e1affa59af"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8093-e398c442854a" class="">4) All matrices (schema + invariants)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-814b-c466a9c97312" class="">MAT-001: Claim → Support-Type Matrix (UCIA core)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b474-fde7f7249cf6" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8ac5-fc30790c68cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rows: claims</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8729-c4aef833fc3e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-8143-e53da04467ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cell: one-hot assignment</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-8bf1-d06223eb3cff" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-8dfe-d574e014b8cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall c:\ \sum_{t} M[c,t]=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-ad30-feca8612a352" class="">M[c,Analogical]=0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-8f03-dbaf5ce5aa02"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9a1b-d581b2e4d71e" class="">MAT-002: Invariant → Enforcement → Telemetry → Trigger → FailMode → Fix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bac5-f846d185c6f5" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-ad6e-cd57705c5b5a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Row: invariant</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-b3c6-ece19ff399db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-85cb-f84fbc6d648c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>check(I)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b50e-d5b7df7fc2cd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>telemetry(I)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8406-f75058d5431f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>trigger(I)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-888e-ff8d768cfa1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>failmode(I)</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b7fd-f666216a76a5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle"><code>fix(I)</code></li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b1d8-d716ae226eee" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-aaed-e0c99e04f081" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall I:\ check(I)\ \text{defined}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-925c-eea95c38dfe4" class="">check(I)=False \Rightarrow trigger(I)\Rightarrow fix(I)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-964d-e77fdd8d0e58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
fix(I)\Rightarrow audit(\Delta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-b006-ed3ebd5faacf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-89cb-d3fdaf31cf23" class="">MAT-003: Predicate → Constraint Matrix (relationship semantics)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-b2e6-c1d78b825f9e" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a594-f2051a98d503" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rows: predicates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-91f2-ec4b3c0961d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns: symmetry, antisymmetry, transitivity, domain, range, cardinality, disjointness</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-a3e1-d595b1bae983" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong><br/>If symmetry required:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-927d-ddc32b9dd53f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
sym(p)=1 \Rightarrow p(u,v)\Leftrightarrow p(v,u)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-aef2-f53da1967ac8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
trans(p)=1 \Rightarrow p(u,v)\land p(v,w)\Rightarrow p(u,w)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-b1d4-fa87e3add50d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(u,v)\Rightarrow type(u)\in Dom(p)\land type(v)\in Ran(p)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b248-e759add05798"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-88e2-f384d77f774e" class="">MAT-004: Module → Tests Matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-bd8a-ed674b38e909" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8b6c-ca7b94fbc84f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rows: modules</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9d2b-d51f7ae9a3fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns: tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-a54a-c9ab6b2392d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cell: pass/fail</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b82a-cb4cda8761f6" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-bd18-c6d3e2cb07bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
release(m)\Rightarrow \forall t\in Tests(m): pass(m,t)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-bac8-faffc2cf1825" class="">fail(m,t)=1 \Rightarrow block_release(m)=True<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9483-c02150af3d34"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-8138-fbf3b6dbbbb6" class="">MAT-005: Data → Version → Audit Matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-b8d6-e4540035138f" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-a4a2-c1089556005b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rows: datasets/artifacts</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-b65a-ee736e481ad5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns: data_ver, code_ver, rules_ver, hash, timestamp, actor, change_reason</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-ad44-d14761744238" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-86af-c5b7a8341c1c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
hash_{t+1}\neq hash_t \Rightarrow version++</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-b410-cba9392cd292" class="">change \Rightarrow audit(change)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-96ac-e6a6311dd535" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
replay \Rightarrow pinned(data\_ver,code\_ver,rules\_ver)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-a64a-cae8ea03bf39"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-b667-d6627994b9f5" class="">MAT-006: Cluster → Metrics Matrix (density/cut/purity/stability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bd9b-ea6bfc18ae5a" class=""><strong>Schema</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9044-c649bf83935d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Rows: clusters</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8f0c-e12b1716e807" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Columns: dens, cut, purity, stability, churn, anomaly_score</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-89b2-ef49343be616" class=""><strong>Invariants</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a84e-f2b318e82b35" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
0\le dens\le 1,\quad 0\le purity\le 1,\quad 0\le stability\le 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-b801-c1f46bd428b1" class="">anomaly\ge \tau \Rightarrow flag<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8de4-c66898eb4791"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-9b42-e34c1ecc98fb" class="">What is missing to make this “ALL from AMOS” (actual, not canonical)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-833a-c627eb38dd8f" class="">To enumerate the real AMOS items, I need the repo contents available in-chat (GitHub selection is currently not enabled here). Once you connect the repo or upload a zip, I will replace this canonical registry with the <strong>exact</strong> AMOS-defined list (names, IDs, membership rules, gates, FSMs, matrix schemas) extracted from source-of-truth files.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a001-d78e8186b350" class="">C1101–C1300 — Next 200 (engineering + reasoning focus: verification, threat models, parsers, and runtime systems)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b475-dadde3da4ccc" class="">All variables in . Discrete time . 
 squashes to .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-9507-ca8f52f40bfa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-af67-dea5823afe65" class="">Group K1 — Threat models &amp; 
adversarial behavior in “human systems” (C1101–C1140)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9064-d52bdbd928bb" class="">C1101 — AdversaryModel — define adversary class</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a6a1-eeb82ec4b4d7" class="">C1102 — FaceOptimizer_Objective — maximize  while minimizing social cost and accountability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-90da-e525a30c722a" class="">C1103 — Extractor_Objective — maximize  while keeping  just above detection threshold</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-ab36-d773f4f17633" class="">C1104 — AmbiguityAttack — increase  while decreasing :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-8cdb-d285346d6456" class="">C1105 — AmbiguityAttack_Effect —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-9d37-d8a0ef97cd81" class="">C1106 — DignityStarvationAttack — maintain proximity/gifts while keeping</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8046-e694cb33059b" class="">C1107 — DignityStarvation_Effect —  appears high but  persists:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-b24d-ebc6eaccd91a" class="">C1108 — GaslightVector — raise  then deny:  without</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-8ff3-f2dac8df3c47" class="">C1109 — GaslightDetection — if  deception risk</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-ad30-c63cc28b7d2b" class="">C1110 — ImageShieldingPattern — ex protection to preserve IM:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b1ab-c1a46a574f38" class="">C1111 — ImageShieldingImpact —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bf58-c3bdd29c86b9" class="">C1112 — TriangulationVector — maintain ties with ex t
o preserve audience credibility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-ac55-cba07b1eaf1b" class="">C1113 — TriangulationDetection — if  and  and  triangulation risk</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-9208-db89655be2cd" class="">C1114 — SlowRollCommitment — keep  fluctuating around 0.5 to avoid decision cost</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-8aa7-f94a7e875f4a" class="">C1115 — SlowRollDetection — high variance:  slow-roll</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-87f4-d59b1b3d284c" class="">C1116 — ExtractionMask — provide small care to keep  from collapsing while extracting large</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-98d9-dc47462b8d6f" class="">C1117 — ExtractionMaskDetection — if  masked extraction</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-9d59-d822ba05c290" class="">C1118 — AvoidantSafetyClaim — “I’m not ready” + continued access:  but</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-9fff-de0e0477d149" class="">C1119 — AvoidantDetection — if  persists  avoidant access pattern</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-a638-e48f73a501b7" class="">C1120 — AudienceSplitAttack — behave differently across contexts to keep optionality</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b475-ec34aa59efeb" class="">C1121 — AudienceSplitMetric — ; 
low  split</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-81de-ddf37fb2d8bb" class="">C1122 — ReputationFirewall — use third-party norms to justify OP: “culture/family”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-966e-d392bc654cae" class="">C1123 — FirewallDetection — OP attributed to norms, but no compensating PR/CL actions ⇒ firewall</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-bbe6-fd5569bca8d6" class="">C1124 — ControlRefusalByDeflection — shift issue from PR/CL to CO (gifts/attention)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-9841-c57b5c654e7a" class="">C1125 — DeflectionDetection —  while  despite dignity alerts</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-899e-cdeb6bb6fb0f" class="">C1126 — TimeDiscountAttack — “later” promises reduce immediate cost; 
no state change</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b4ab-fdf13808eea1" class="">C1127 — TimeDiscountDetection — promises without verified deltas in  steps ⇒ false compliance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-9d94-db8f08c061f3" class="">C1128 — ShameTransfer — make victim feel “asking too much” to suppress DT</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-8cde-ebc0b4cb541f" class="">C1129 — ShameTransferDetection — if  after boundary request and  transfer</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-99c4-ddc9ca664cda" class="">C1130 — LocalMaxTrap — keep system in “bounded” zone  to avoid rupture or commitment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-ad50-fda3935cf853" class="">C1131 — TrapDetection — if S oscillates in band for &gt;N windows with no trend ⇒ trapped state</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a4dd-c5b846260369" class="">C1132 — HonestInability_SafeExit — admitting inability reduces harm vs hiding</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-b61b-d06182aa779f" class="">C1133 — SafeExitRule — if cannot meet PR/CL thresholds, 
require explicit exit statement</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-a046-eb4cface4039" class="">C1134 — ThreatModelInvariant — persistent OP implies low PR regardless of tenderness</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a68d-c54c37cff638" class="">C1135 — ThreatModelInvariant2 — repeated LM implies strategic ambiguity or fear of cost</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-b69f-dd833512eaa6" class="">C1136 — AdversaryLikelihood —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a8fe-f1e020621577" class="">C1137 — ExtractorLikelihood —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9294-cb95d88e1153" class="">C1138 — RiskScore —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8d4f-d428e4597c74" class="">C1139 — RiskThreshold —  treat as structurally unsafe</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a886-fcfbb54213df" class="">C1140 — RiskRecoveryGate —  sustained 3 steps needed to re-open softness</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-8f23-e7b28a3b59a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-86e8-d9a7352bdde3" class="">Group K2 — Parsing “signals” into state updates (NLP-to-state) (C1141–C1180)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-819e-cba90514af7d" class="">C1141 — EventSchema — define event</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-9340-f453e60765aa" class="">C1142 — EventToState —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-9f70-d5039253498b" class="">C1143 — PublicNamingEvent — if type=NAME_PUBLIC and label correct ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9eb9-d0d1c0375e92" class="">C1144 — MislabelEvent — type=MISLABEL ⇒
</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b142-dfbb32195dee" class="">C1145 — TransparencyEvent — type=DISCLOSE_SENSITIVE ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-b22c-d55152787f9c" class="">C1146 — HiddenContactEvent — type=HIDDEN_CONTACT ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-b1e7-f556987fdb11" class="">C1147 — SocialCostEvent — type=PAY_COST ⇒  (requires verifiable cost)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-8578-e916948a0035" class="">C1148 — CostlessGiftEvent — type=GIFT_ONLY ⇒  small;  unchanged</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-8320-eb6f11bae159" class="">C1149 — RepairEvent — type=REPAIR_WITH_CHANGE ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-9a8a-c98c10324e19" class="">C1150 — ApologyOnlyEvent — type=APOLOGY_ONLY ⇒  small; if repeated ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-98b2-f9cdae80fcf4" class="">C1151 — ConsistencyWindow — compute  over contexts/events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-89a2-c13ef652ead4" class="">C1152 — CC_Update —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-957e-edb39334aab8" class="">C1153 — EvidenceWeight — update magnitude scales with verifiability :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-b26c-f27e64705067" class="">C1154 — VerifiabilityRule — words-only ; 
third-party observable</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-9f95-e39310b07f0c" class="">C1155 — ContradictionPenalty — if event contradicts prior claim ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b3c1-c8234d260f58" class="">C1156 — ContradictionMetric —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-bd9a-ceedcdacc25e" class="">C1157 — AudienceContextTagging — contexts: friends/family/work/public</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bc97-f63a84e8cd9f" class="">C1158 — ContextPriority — for dignity: family/public contexts weighted higher</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-a969-fd15d6db210f" class="">C1159 — TemporalDecay — event effects decay:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-b54f-d2a736fa9b65" class="">C1160 — DecayException — violations (OP, mislabel) decay slower than positives</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-b504-d56dc496274b" class="">C1161 — ShockAmplifier — if , multiply violation deltas by</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a333-e89eeeb4a743" class="">C1162 — NR_EstimationFromText —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-836d-dfd129664d62" class="">C1163 — ShameLexiconSignal — shame phrases increase SH estimate</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-a42a-ca3adb44cb31" class="">C1164 — SafetyLexiconSignal — “safe/held” phrases increase SS estimate only if PR/CL conditions met</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-bc7c-e8bdf1a7e3fe" class="">C1165 — ActionLexiconRule — “I will” is not action; 
“I did” needs evidence tag</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-9d4c-dba37ce16485" class="">C1166 — EvidenceTagging — attach evidence: screenshot/public post/witness/introduction</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-b6a3-cd167bddf75a" class="">C1167 — PassiveAggressiveDetector — detect indirect hostility ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-913e-c48318e01c28" class="">C1168 — AvoidanceDetector — long response latency ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-9290-e1f69df61215" class="">C1169 — ConsistencyDetector — match naming across audiences; mismatch ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-94e8-c9a80b0a4a84" class="">C1170 — LM_Update —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-964f-d2ac13a248c4" class="">C1171 — NarrativeToEventCompiler — convert paragraph → ordered event list</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-9b7b-cbff232a8a29" class="">C1172 — CompilerInvariant — every claim must map to an event or be marked “unsupported”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-a14b-e8b065bb6fe5" class="">C1173 — UnsupportedClaimPenalty — unsupported love claim does not increase TR; 
may decrease TR if repeated</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8d55-d5a5bb8377e1" class="">C1174 — MinimalClaimTypes — {action, intent, status, boundary, disclosure, repair, refusal}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b74a-c8547c8aa914" class="">C1175 — StatusClaimCheck — “girlfriend” status requires NAME_PUBLIC or INTEGRATION evidence</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9ae5-db0941ef1ed7" class="">C1176 — IntegrationEvidence — family intro / friend circle intro / public acknowledgement</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9843-c8c333ab5754" class="">C1177 — LatentOPInference — if repeated avoidance + inconsistent labels ⇒  even without explicit hiding</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-be8f-cd708bdf7f74" class="">C1178 — LatentEXInference — if repeated requests + asymmetry ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-b64a-cc1aa49a8a06" class="">C1179 — ParserConfidence —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-bd7b-e5fbc696df7e" class="">C1180 — LowConfPolicy — if Conf&lt;0.4, freeze updates; 
request more observable events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-b591-e2f5c6164cf0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-ac5c-e8f3eadcb426" class="">Group K3 — Runtime system design (state machine + modes) (C1181–C1220)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a52e-f37150cd0bbe" class="">C1181 — Modes — {Observe, Stabilize, Dignity-Enforce, Repair, Decide, Exit}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-89f9-dc727ae81644" class="">C1182 — ModeTransition — determined by thresholds on D_t, S_t, Risk</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-899a-e5e557c2d374" class="">C1183 — Observe→Stabilize —  Stabilize</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9a83-f1d28f229a42" class="">C1184 — Stabilize→DignityEnforce —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-84ed-c3f997606243" class="">C1185 — DignityEnforce→Repair — rupture detected and partner attempts verified change</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-a438-ca69d1b394bf" class="">C1186 — Repair→Decide — after 2 windows of verification outcomes</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-83be-fc20caaafe04" class="">C1187 — Decide→Exit — if constraints unmet after N cycles or false compliance detected</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-96be-ca94402911f4" class="">C1188 — Decide→Observe — if stable + verified; 
return to monitoring</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-86a1-cc2b492331a1" class="">C1189 — ExitInvariant — do not remain in system requiring SE (self-erasure) to maintain access</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-96b7-d8ab9f8c0e85" class="">C1190 — SE_Definition —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b4e0-c356172b36cd" class="">C1191 — SE_Threshold —  Exit recommended (structural harm)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-8b38-ed048360e3b1" class="">C1192 — SE_SensitiveAmplifier — if NR&gt;0.7, threshold drops to 0.6</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-8a55-c207a8bd0985" class="">C1193 — StabilizeActions — only CO + exposure reduction; do not negotiate status while SS low</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a643-f8bc3f79dba8" class="">C1194 — DignityActions — require explicit PR/CL public evidence; 
no substitution allowed</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-a3bc-e4f717029f9e" class="">C1195 — RepairActions — require apology specificity + cost + non-repeat guarantee + transparency</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-8b6f-d12ef8043d5b" class="">C1196 — DecideRule — decision is function of verified deltas, not promises</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-b323-c204ae9ece10" class="">C1197 — FailureMode_Limbo — repeated bounded regime with no PR/CL improvement ⇒ limbo harm</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-887a-d131dd3af9df" class="">C1198 — LimboDetector —  for &gt;N windows and  flat ⇒ limbo</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-9b62-e1459e98e82a" class="">C1199 — LimboPolicy — escalate to “Decide” with hard deadline in model time steps</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-a8d1-eaec2d4aa473" class="">C1200 — DeadlineEquation — if no improvement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-bbad-ecda9434e206" class="">C1201 — IntegrityAuditGate — every mode switch logs reason + violated invariants</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9e1f-d1b97064e784" class="">C1202 — AuditFields — {mode, trigger, violated_constraints, evidence}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-bad3-f8845974efb7" class="">C1203 — RollbackRule — if evidence falsified, 
rollback positive deltas and decrease TR</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-88c8-ecfae3eed67c" class="">C1204 — EvidenceFalsificationPenalty — if falsified ⇒ TR→0.2 floor + Exit recommended</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-ba60-c4aa7acec9c4" class="">C1205 — MultiContextRequirement — PR must hold in ≥2 contexts (public + private)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-8881-dbfbba1bb24c" class="">C1206 — MultiContextCheck — if PR only in private ⇒ PR capped at 0.5</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-a6c6-fe95dc9d212e" class="">C1207 — ExTiePolicy — ex-related interactions require transparency to keep OP low</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-8974-c4a5ebe4593e" class="">C1208 — ExTieViolation — hidden ex contact ⇒ OP spike + TR drop</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a48f-fb58cd1fa9a2" class="">C1209 — CostAccounting — define cumulative cost paid  vs avoided</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8944-d61bc8131709" class="">C1210 — CostRatio — ; low CR implies image optimization</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-b16d-d54118e39c48" class="">C1211 — CR_Threshold —  face-optimizer likelihood increases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-891e-ff933347ccef" class="">C1212 — CR_Impact —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-86fa-dfd25c986562" class="">C1213 — SoftnessLock — once COLD&gt;0.8, softness cannot be forced by CO alone</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-bc78-f15bee5b79f0" class="">C1214 — UnlockRule — require sustained PR &amp; 
CL &gt;0.7 for 3 steps + OP&lt;0.3</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-88a5-ef9bd1adb082" class="">C1215 — StateResetRule — after exit, reset CO dependency; 
keep lessons as constraints</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-bde7-fd47119330aa" class="">C1216 — PostExitRecovery — SS increases by removing OP exposure:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-b34a-ea156a8deebb" class="">C1217 — SystemInvariant — never trade dignity for access (PR/CL not negotiable)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-afb5-ecdb8061399e" class="">C1218 — SubstituteInvalidation — gifts/time/sex cannot substitute for PR/CL deficits</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-b44a-ef4a63dcbb8d" class="">C1219 — TerminationCondition — model terminates when Valid/Bounded/Invalid classification stable 2 windows</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a623-f0262ab6cf20" class="">C1220 — TerminalLabels — Valid (stable + verified), Bounded (mixed), Invalid (harm basin)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-b7c3-f3e179277a21"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-9392-f3fa8ce1c85b" class="">Group K4 — Formal reasoning layer (claim typing + UCIA-style gates) (C1221–C1260)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8ed5-e8c5ad8cd064" class="">C1221 — ClaimExtraction — parse narrative into claims</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-9e47-e6c4f96cac36" class="">C1222 — SupportTyping — each  labeled {Empirical, Inferential, Definitional, Limit}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a9a1-d135108183a7" class="">C1223 — AnalogicalNonLoadBearing — analogies cannot support PR/CL assertions</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-95cd-e80e2f776a5e" class="">C1224 — MechanismCheck — any inference must specify mechanism: (IM, PD, OP, 
cost)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8d6a-fd32f9264a8f" class="">C1225 — TemporalCheck — claims must specify window; otherwise bounded</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-b2d1-cdbb8059ef6b" class="">C1226 — InvariantCheck — compare against invariants: PR/CL/OP thresholds</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-aada-c9c68ddcde42" class="">C1227 — LoveClaimGate — “love” claim requires</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-981a-eb97ba3dd378" class="">C1228 — RespectClaimGate — “respect” requires correct public naming + no downgrade events</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-97f8-cf30247ddb77" class="">C1229 — SafetyClaimGate — “safe” requires SS stable + no repeated OP spikes</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-baca-ec97f4e875ab" class="">C1230 — RepairClaimGate — “changed” requires verified deltas and non-repeat across 2 windows</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-bd51-f59ac1b3b6c1" class="">C1231 — InvalidityCondition — if claim contradicts invariants, mark “structurally invalid”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-80cf-c3a2d8f08dfe" class="">C1232 — BoundedCondition — if insufficient evidence, mark “structurally bounded”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-bf45-ddc545b0d6e1" class="">C1233 — DecisionFromClaims — decision uses only Empirical+Verified claims</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-90bb-e61c18ebad44" class="">C1234 — WeightingRule — verified public events weighted &gt; 
private words</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8671-c18b706724b8" class="">C1235 — CounterfactualTest — if removing relationship decreases OP and increases SS, 
system was harmful</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-8831-fce805eee50d" class="">C1236 — CounterfactualEquation —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-8303-e97633eda727" class="">C1237 — HarmDominance — if  and  harm-dominant system</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-af3e-c49ed56356dd" class="">C1238 — BenefitDominance — if  and PR/CL constraints met ⇒ benefit-dominant</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-beef-e22c0d3aee65" class="">C1239 — CostOfPretending — define</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-8cd3-e25ff983427d" class="">C1240 — CP_Threshold —  self-erasure tax high</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-b3ca-f6dc088e5e0d" class="">C1241 — SelfRespectConstraint — require  to continue</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b7b1-cc03f827a8db" class="">C1242 — SR_Violation — if SE≥0.5 for 2 windows ⇒ exit triggered</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-a816-ef0bd52eca39" class="">C1243 — DignityDebtUpdate —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-9cd9-ce07f78b0ee1" class="">C1244 — DD_Exit — if DD&gt;0.7 ⇒ exit recommended</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9218-f1b13fa9f953" class="">C1245 — TrustErosionUpdate —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-ac1b-ed4b28bbc6e7" class="">C1246 — TrustFloor — repeated false compliance ⇒</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a20b-dac7d581e0a6" class="">C1247 — ReconciliationBound — reconciliation only possible if TR rises above 0.6</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9de4-ed3b9944da71" class="">C1248 — R
econciliationImpossibility — if OP remains &gt;0.6, TR cannot exceed 0.5</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-b8b5-c651b0b910e3" class="">C1249 — PriorityProof — priority is measured by cost paid under conflict, 
not by affection</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-bd83-dababca74721" class="">C1250 — PriorityEquation —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8f1b-d04767bd0110" class="">C1251 — PriorityThreshold — Priority&lt;0.5 ⇒ not “chosen” structurally</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-811b-f1b17a8d0a34" class="">C1252 — “Chosen”Gate — chosen requires Priority&gt;0.6 + CL public &gt;0.6</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-b1d0-daa75e3c5036" class="">C1253 — AmbiguityPenalty —  priority score capped</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-b0c2-c0c9eb70e94d" class="">C1254 — CapRule — if LM&gt;0.3 then Priority≤0.5</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-9624-ee08683f1fb5" class="">C1255 — EmotionalRiskIndex —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-95dc-ed489c406b1b" class="">C1256 — ERI_Threshold — ERI&gt;0.7 ⇒ system unsafe for sensitive profile</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-95df-dfd740faee16" class="">C1257 — NeutralLanguageGate — do not attribute motive unless inferentially supported</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-a524-d0e37d67aae9" class="">C1258 — MotiveInferenceRule — motive inference allowed only if pattern persists across contexts/time</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-848e-d664e1cef518" class="">C1259 — TerminationGate — stop analysis when classification stable and next action determined</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b511-ce51d418b65d" class="">C1260 — NextActionSet — {enforce PR/CL, demand transparency, repair protocol, 
exit}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-bd94-e7e875029304"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-810d-d3b14fcf19ac" class="">Group K5 — Systems loops for Vietnam-style hiddenness (macro cultural channel) (C1261–C1300)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bf80-c23ea3bc616f" class="">C1261 — CultureChannel — define  (hiddenness climate) as</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-90e4-e9b9112dfd43" class="">C1262 — HC_Raises_OP_Micro —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b345-dbd4de2092bc" class="">C1263 — HC_Lowers_PR —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-ba6e-c7593639edaa" class="">C1264 — HC_Lowers_CLpublic —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a784-e318be33a8d2" class="">C1265 — HC_Raises_LM —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-8f2e-d9f068d7dccf" class="">C1266 — LM_Raises_SH —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-8cb9-f39fcb6d9902" class="">C1267 — HC_Raises_ImageDefenseOfEx —  to preserve narrative</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b967-dd06c31128d8" class="">C1268 — HC_Raises_NewPartnerDowngrade —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-a632-c29604b2d9e0" class="">C1269 — FamilyAudienceWeight — in high HC, 
family context weight dominates public actions</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8dcc-d92a71617e7c" class="">C1270 — FamilyCostAvoidance —  cost avoided in family context</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-837d-e2c3ecfd27fb" class="">C1271 — CostAvoidance_Raises_OP — avoided cost implies hiddenness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-b0fa-e37fd36ab720" class="">C1272 — CostAvoidance_Raises_PD —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-9fa6-d8ae46bba862" class="">C1273 — “NoDrama”Norm — define</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a750-c1aac3fb9e3a" class="">C1274 — ND_Suppresses_DT —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-ad40-c46488902568" class="">C1275 — ND_Raises_SE —  (self-silencing to keep peace)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-b273-e6e0721fdbdc" class="">C1276 — SE_Raises_ERl —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a669-ca0d68386ca2" class="">C1277 — ClassSignalMismatch — difference in LI expectations increases rupture sensitivity</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-8fb3-dbb225e01951" class="">C1278 — MismatchEquation —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-987c-f4f146c792be" class="">C1279 — MM_Raises_SH —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-aa41-fa9ff901ddcf" class="">C1280 — MM_Raises_COLD —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-a51c-c1a86090e45c" class="">C1281 — HonorAsStructure — in low HC, honor aligns with PR/CL; 
in high HC, honor aligns with IM</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b5ed-eedb449a957d" class="">C1282 — HonorSplitRule —  else</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-b1c3-ff367acf59e0" class="">C1283 — HonorSplitImpact — if Honor≈IM then PR to new partner decreases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-808d-cc877f781fdc" class="">C1284 — LI_BufferRule — high LI reduces HC sensitivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-aeee-d3ad3dfb466e" class="">C1285 — RegionalVariance — treat HC as environment parameter per context (hometown vs city)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8e48-d2291ac66caa" class="">C1286 — ContextShiftShock — moving into high-HC context causes OP spike:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-91b4-e6ef5cdd084e" class="">C1287 — TetShockModel — family aggregation increases HC_ctx temporarily</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b89e-c0d0ac0cdf55" class="">C1288 — TetShockEquation —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-adce-e4a96a434668" class="">C1289 — SensitiveAmplifier — if NR&gt;0.7, Tet shock multiplies SH delta by</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-abf1-d3a97b126a75" class="">C1290 — TetMitigation — mitigation requires pre-commitment: PR/CL public steps before context entry</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-828f-ca797599bf43" class="">C1291 — PrecommitmentGate — if entering high-HC context, 
require  first</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-ac22-f78442420435" class="">C1292 — NoPrecommitmentCost — without precommitment ⇒ SE tax increases</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-884b-c21b6dfbb027" class="">C1293 — HiddennessAttractor — high HC + low DT yields stable OP-high basin</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b406-e574bbaaab9d" class="">C1294 — ExitHiddennessAttractor — requires DT increase + paid social cost + consistent naming</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-ba82-d31b872aa1c2" class="">C1295 — HiddennessStabilityCriterion — if</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-bee9-c4ecfc5f2859" class="">C1296 — HiddennessRecoveryCriterion — if</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-92d5-f9478d55fd04" class="">C1297 — StructuralCompatibilityScore —</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-a156-c3fa57f8d219" class="">C1298 — SC_Threshold —  chronic mismatch risk high</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-baec-ede0fffd9ce0" class="">C1299 — CompatibilityInvariant — long-term stability requires  or sustained compensating PR/CL</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-98a2-f3b547b1fdf2" class="">C1300 — TerminalCulturalGate — if environment forces OP and suppresses PR/CL, 
classify system “Invalid for sensitive profile”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-883b-da832b00d97c" class="">Below is a <strong>Loop–Stage Detection Rubric</strong> designed for deterministic classification.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b4f2-c127099ca756" class="">This converts qualitative loop observation into measurable stage assignment (S1–S4).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b403-e2f3721333c9" class="">No ideology.<br/>No narrative bias.<br/>Signal-based only.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-969d-e09c15ae856b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-b11d-e2086d768b38" class="">I. 
UNIVERSAL LOOP-STAGE SCORING FRAMEWORK</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b6ae-dc2e3d81954d" class="">For any loop L, 
measure six core variables:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-a9ba-f054207d0022" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-adeb-f1ea54739c4f"><th id="qp]}" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Variable</th><th id="iIbc" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Meaning</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a814-c79a832231d9"><td id="qp]}" class="">g</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Growth rate of core state variable</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-a0e3-c1dfb6f17a08"><td id="qp]}" class="">F</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Feedback strength (self-reinforcement intensity)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-8e9b-cad5cac40870"><td id="qp]}" class="">X</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Cross-domain propagation breadth</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-81fe-d0e21abad166"><td id="qp]}" class="">H</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Concentration / lock-in index</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9842-c9b12e4aef5b"><td id="qp]}" class="">R</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Resilience (buffering capacity)</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a042-cf2edf5a8e2a"><td id="qp]}" class="">S</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Shock sensitivity</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-b6b7-f9b431b47335"><td id="qp]}" class="">Δ</td><td id="iIbc" class="">Marginal returns trend</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-afd1-c8a153c9a5d3" class="">All normalized to 0–1.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-bf8c-f2ea4ba675cf"/></div><div s
tyle="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b8e2-d57b918286f5" class="">II. METRIC DEFINITIONS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-94aa-e70c37011dbc" class="">1. 
Growth Rate (g)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-988b-eb15f1403b72" class="">g = (Current Level − Prior Level) / Prior Level</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-bbc9-d31c60a700bc" class="">Interpretation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-8bbd-c30565199152" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">&lt;0 → contraction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b266-fcfccf1208be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">0–0.1 → slow growth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-8d95-f2a4f6a309d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">0.1–0.3 → moderate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a1bd-fddee0af8b1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">0.3 → rapid</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a03b-cec1ce500720" class="">Examples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b9f0-c281f9785700" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">User growth %</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-8cde-dc11b42b8435" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Capital growth %</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a54f-fe60ca22d89e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy expansion count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9ae6-d19f887912eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Asset price velocity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9423-c684d7fe271d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-964e-eb9eeabd847b" c
lass="">2. Feedback Strength (F)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-98c9-c922987af023" class="">Measure elasticity of input to output.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9073-da11e724d550" class="">F ≈ d(Output)/d(Input)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-a5b4-fe9e1f3fdcf6" class="">Proxy:<br/>Correlation between reinvestment and next-period expansion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-868c-e5453f2ab531" class="">High if:<br/>Output growth directly increases future input capacity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-a81d-c329b9eb4946"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a615-cfb93b04f850" class="">3. Cross-Domain Propagation (X)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8d70-fe68a94fb3d0" class="">Number of domains materially affected / total domains.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-b890-de065e7271d8" class="">Domains:<br/>Capital<br/>Policy<br/>Technology<br/>Infrastructure<br/>Narrative<br/>Security<br/>Labor<br/>Culture</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-a27f-f771781e867e" class="">X = impacted_domains / 8</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-8949-d8cba90fbb5b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-8f73-c0820768b415" class="">4. 
Lock-In / Concentration (H)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-9fdb-fe39204a7b95" class="">H = concentration_index × switching_cost_index</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-ac20-dd53b1f0892d" class="">Use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b327-ff61c31714dc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Herfindahl index</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-a0cc-f4fe24902e7a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Top-3 share</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b375-c9238a68d20b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Regulatory barrier count</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-80e0-e4c489dfaa6b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Contract length</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9bca-ede2642692ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dependency depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8a44-cb1745a3361b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-8cf1-c2b852e91bde" class="">5. 
Resilience (R)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a917-ebcdde1b0492" class="">R = buffer_capacity / volatility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-833e-f6a749ad05ed" class="">High if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b2c9-d7ee72d9221a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Cash reserves high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-b06e-fa55fe8da6c2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Redundant infrastructure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-ab3b-f57f8d190806" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Diversified input sources</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-ae71-f67646e8eac2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Institutional trust high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-83a3-d8b419fe3fc8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a2a7-e07f35eace60" class="">6. 
Shock Sensitivity (S)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-8341-ce87ce68fd62" class="">S = volatility_amplitude × leverage</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-9453-f9de4e8cc804" class="">High if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b75f-e5ddc5c6221f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High debt</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8fc3-e6fec70895be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">High margin compression</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-95ba-f11e2dbc5b8a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Thin buffers</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8d11-cf1bfc370d10" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy dependence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-b469-c8ac65cbbcc9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-8455-cec9f16a5d0f" class="">7. Marginal Return Trend (Δ)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-88d9-fac0c3e7d483" class="">Δ = d(growth rate)/dt</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-bcf8-cc8fff10d5de" class="">Positive → accelerating<br/>Flat → plateau<br/>Negative → decelerating</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-aece-efe40beebb95"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-be05-d297b9f94190" class="">III. 
STAGE CLASSIFICATION LOGIC</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-abd8-f41039bc924f" class="">Now we classify.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a9c7-c54d51de9545"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b444-cc5b799c519e" class="">S1 — Birth</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-870b-c409538e2d56" class="">Conditions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-b228-e82fd10b2ce6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">g: low to moderate (&lt;0.2)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-bf25-f82e77a48714" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F: emerging (&lt;0.4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8756-c48a4354fd51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">X: low (&lt;0.3)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-9909-e307a8f4ab05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">H: low (&lt;0.3)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8629-c4de3ea88485" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Δ: positive</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-addb-c3dcd75ff0e7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">S: moderate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-ac26-f4a3d889aa3e" class="">Interpretation:<br/>Loop exists but not entrenched.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-85d5-ee84b9c817a3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-a542-c9c07c262dff" class="">S2 — Expansion</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b871-f22c08bbec62" c
lass="">Conditions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a19c-e0fd61191a53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">g: high (&gt;0.2)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-bc6e-e53b3f04ef35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F: strong (&gt;0.5)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-938b-e8da620eeb07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">X: rising (&gt;0.4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-b983-e0c41cbfee3a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">H: moderate (0.4–0.7)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-a2bf-c7fdd37683f4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Δ: positive or stable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-b013-f402b3532cc0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">S: rising</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-af43-f013b90ebbfc" class="">Interpretation:<br/>Positive feedback dominant.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9c41-e6a42aea29c6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-b9c0-e9bbc8f357c3" class="">S3 — Dominance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b5d8-ee1a78dd8bc2" class="">Conditions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-b6a3-d66937bab6b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">g: moderate (0.05–0.2)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-b28b-ef96742961be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F: stable (&gt;0.4)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul i
d="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-971b-ea675d4baf83" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">X: high (&gt;0.6)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-bcd5-e1f706683a53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">H: high (&gt;0.7)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b6dc-d3f619717423" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Δ: flattening</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-b3e4-d04fa5dd2924" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R: high (appears stable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9e63-e20ffd74b218" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">S: moderate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-bb7c-e749199a9b05" class="">Interpretation:<br/>Lock-in achieved.<br/>Growth stabilizes.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-baf4-e8135e3d032f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-84f4-c29293c0087d" class="">S4 — Decay</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-9f39-eee436798b65" class="">Conditions:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9ba3-c15d129b2cf3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">g: low or negative</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-a54a-c53a626c9748" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">F: weakening or brittle</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-9503-d0f962b06feb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">X: high but unstable</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9e6d-eb5f95c02d4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">H: s
till high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-9cad-f7e789c94eb3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Δ: negative</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-92fc-daa8df4cdfe8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">R: declining</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-83d2-c3ecc17e4e96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">S: high (&gt;0.6)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b092-f61ebcdfe215" class="">Interpretation:<br/>Structure still large but fragile.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-bf4b-d2b9bac5be2f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-adbc-e0d0aca839f6" class="">IV. NUMERICAL SCORING METHOD</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-82a6-ec02f43747ce" class="">Compute Stage Score:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9811-faba9989d2f4" class="">S1_score = (1−g) + (1−F) + (1−X) + (1−H) + positive_Δ<br/>S2_score = g + F + X + moderate_H + positive_Δ<br/>S3_score = moderate_g + F + X + H + flat_Δ + R<br/>S4_score = negative_g + declining_R + high_S + negative_Δ</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-a44e-c081760d083d" class="">Pick max score.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8ad8-d499699e51ba" class="">Tie-breaker:<br/>Choose stage with strongest Δ trend alignment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-83f3-e516fa9a010c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-9ed7-fe1d1356e912" class="">V. 
STAGE TRANSITION TRIGGERS</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-bdff-f64c3a4ad21c" class="">Birth → Expansion:<br/>F &gt; 0.5 AND g &gt; 0.2</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-a479-d4f937c6e87c" class="">Expansion → Dominance:<br/>H &gt; 0.7 AND Δ ≤ 0</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-af47-cdfdf166716b" class="">Dominance → Decay:<br/>Δ &lt; 0 AND S &gt; 0.6</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-953b-ff9165af1a86" class="">Decay → Reset:<br/>System collapse or restructuring event</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b92d-e5df19cf94f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-a26c-d45eaba4b1c4" class="">VI. LOOP INSTABILITY TEST</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-9135-d048b57f1c6a" class="">Compute feedback matrix M for loop cluster.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-98d1-dd03dcb63717" class="">If spectral_radius(M) &gt; 1.2<br/>→ runaway expansion or crash likely</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-a14f-e9dd512fca8e" class="">If &lt; 0.8<br/>→ damping / decay</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-856e-d97b262da379" class="">Between 0.8–1.2<br/>→ plateau</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-bb98-ede02e49f963"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-a34f-c9588143aa71" class="">VII. 
EXAMPLE (ABSTRACT)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8a6d-dc0edc7cc5a9" class="">Platform loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a4ec-c5b4a3aa9c5b" class="">g = 0.25</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-bba9-fbb0fabe7a41" class="">F = 0.7</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-980a-c87d6c6184d3" class="">X = 0.6</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-9b99-f662c5a1274e" class="">H = 0.5</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8987-d84bacb3d2f4" class="">Δ positive</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-854b-c03081a3a3cc" class="">→ S2 Expansion</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9898-f5e14a3d3d56" class="">If later:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-9a60-faedbac87d21" class="">g = 0.08</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9355-ccc0d8596a40" class="">H = 0.85</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-97e1-c4348034951c" class="">Δ ~ 0</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-984b-fb621c8eb835" class="">→ S3 Dominance</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-bef8-ea2499d1b348" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-bee7-e5c679492c95" class="">g = −0.05</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-989f-e693ee858678" class="">R falling</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-b686-c22e942ae9fc" class="">S = 0.7</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8d2c-d817a4f89d66" class="">Δ negative</p></div><div style="display:contents" d
ir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-82e0-ff337ea16b20" class="">→ S4 Decay</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8aa0-d5d22cbe5706"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a000-c00c872b1b23" class="">VIII.
