OUTPUT FORMAT (AMOS-READY)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8396-d79608adc269" class="">{<br/>&quot;loop&quot;: &quot;Network Effect&quot;,<br/>&quot;stage&quot;: &quot;S2&quot;,<br/>&quot;metrics&quot;: {<br/>&quot;g&quot;: 0.27,<br/>&quot;F&quot;: 0.72,<br/>&quot;X&quot;: 0.55,<br/>&quot;H&quot;: 0.48,<br/>&quot;R&quot;: 0.62,<br/>&quot;S&quot;: 0.44,<br/>&quot;delta&quot;: &quot;positive&quot;<br/>},<br/>&quot;transition_risk&quot;: 0.31,<br/>&quot;next_trigger&quot;: &quot;lock-in increase&quot;<br/>}</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-95f8-d145158a0172"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-bbfa-c6e487ba284e" class="">If you want next:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-860c-d98c1388f930" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Build a real-world loop scanner template (data inputs + formulas)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-87e9-c06759f25ba4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Apply rubric to a current macro system</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-aab0-c873f693f955" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Build multi-loop interaction stage matrix</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-aa87-d4c4fb694245" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Convert into simulation model</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-b38d-c10060afcb31" class="">Choose direction.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9b5b-f2b1a0971aa9" class="">Understood. 
<strong>Next = full recursive simulation layer</strong> (still structural, equation-first): a <strong>multi-domain, multi-loop civilizational model</strong> you can actually run conceptually.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8742-ca8c1f4a7215" class="">I’ll give you: <strong>(A) full state grid</strong>, <strong>(B) update laws</strong>, <strong>(C) cascade triggers</strong>, <strong>(D) Stage-8 definition</strong>, <strong>(E) what “false C7” looks like mathematically</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-9e11-c1c4aba720b2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-b44a-f3d0a86b7ace" class="">A) Full State Grid (7 domains × 7 regulators)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-98dc-e964c84701dd" class="">Let domains:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-801b-dff0bfb242f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d \in \{En, Inf, Bio, Mil, Fin, Law, Cul\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-becd-f53c99e3e58a" class="">Regulators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-8540-e74b4dd283bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r \in \{G, D, Buf, Align, Red, Trans, Hor\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-bc1a-dcdfc7973531" class="">Define a <strong>7×7 matrix</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9e47-c5e0754ae14f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M(t) \in \mathbb{R}^{7\times 7},\quad M_{d,r}(t)\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-bde8-d6da50d995fe" class="">Meaning:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-871c-e332d6239813" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=Gain/amplification</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-8d34-e9ef5158a66a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=Damping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b03c-e1a60aebe5a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=Buffer/reserve</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9263-c4ae7bec98ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=elite–mass risk symmetry</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-8a17-c6f7cb4ae8ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-9963-f529610fe70d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=transparency (truth bandwidth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-8401-fb6db342dcc7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">=time horizon (planning depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-bec1-e46e47c49e6b" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-add3-ee01c06e616e" class="">=algorithmic outrage gain; =judicial damping capacity; 
=health + fertility reserve.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8514-e4297c89c8c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-b849-c48cc546edfe" class="">B) Update Law: Coupled Nonlinear System</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-8ac5-ed087065cb9e" class="">Each cell evolves as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-9e6c-eea763efb3c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{d,r}(t+1)=M_{d,r}(t)+\Delta_{d,r}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-ab1a-f249a4c98270" class="">with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b21a-fb2ddef8fa98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta_{d,r}(t)=\underbrace{F_{d,r}(M(t))}_{internal\ coupling}
+\underbrace{U_{d,r}(t)}_{policy\ control}
-\underbrace{L_{d,r}(t)}_{maintenance\ debt}
+\underbrace{S_{d,r}(t)}_{shocks}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-a422-c9ed34ffa1e7" class="">1) Internal coupling term (matrix-to-matrix recursion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-830c-f74d0fa6c1f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F_{d,r}(M)=\sum_{d&#x27;}\sum_{r&#x27;} W_{(d,r),(d&#x27;,r&#x27;)} \cdot \sigma(M_{d&#x27;,r&#x27;}-\theta_{d&#x27;,r&#x27;})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a26c-df5385340c74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">is a <strong>49×49 coupling matrix</strong> (hidden structure of the civilization)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-aa47-e175277fc30b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">is a saturating nonlinearity (e.g., logistic/tanh)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9814-fb8926ac88e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">are thresholds (phase transition points)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-9f06-d62882daa71c" class="">This is where “overlooked loops” actually live: <strong>in W and θ</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-a5dc-e30a8ec24f98" class="">2) Maintenance debt term (hidden rot)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-b5d3-f0ea12f1066a" class="">Define hidden debt per domain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8088-a7e0-ff9a2dc7c625" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_d(t+1)=H_d(t)+\alpha_d\cdot Load_d(t)-\beta_d\cdot Repair_d(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a94f-ef87d5dad6b0" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-8193-d6b44cb5a024" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_{d,r}(t)=\lambda_{d,r}\cdot H_d(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a235-f9f96f73d346" class=""><strong>Key law:</strong> debt attacks damping and buffers first:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-812a-d6552b989747" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{d,D},\lambda_{d,Buf} \gg \lambda_{d,G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bb57-ec5508c9a2ae" class="">So systems can look “high-energy/high-output” while D and Buf silently degrade.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-aa30-f2377c7ad58d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-8a56-c1180bde2fc9" class="">C) Cascade &amp; Collapse Triggers (formal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-897e-f6a25781e42a" class="">1) The universal cascade condition (connectivity squared)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-8089-d2ffffd86c43" class="">Let interconnectivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-bfb6-cde403b14e46" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa(t)=\sum_{d\ne d&#x27;}\|W_{d\to d&#x27;}\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-983c-fbd4f5c48d8b" class="">Cascade risk grows ~quadratically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-8966-ca5294385242" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk_{cascade}(t)\propto \kappa(t)^2 \cdot \frac{G_{eff}(t)}{D_{eff}(t)+Buf_{eff}(t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-8e7f-c1cd2e932aac" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-b1f2-f9d79312e0d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,G},\quad D_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,D},\quad Buf_{eff}=\sum_d w_d M_{d,Buf}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-8252-f3847c9c06a6" class="">2) Phase transition: when damping fails at multiple domains</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-89c0-d6e699c688b2" class="">Define a domain is “unstable” if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-898b-fd95cf8f84c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{d,D}+M_{d,Buf} &lt; M_{d,G}+Noise_d</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-8128-f9a0845b89d9" class="">Cascades begin when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a09d-e8d3c75f0968" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\{d: unstable(d)=1\}| \ge 2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b379-d46a6555a4ba" class="">That’s the overlooked part: <strong>collapse is rarely single-domain</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-9018-f3bf4e5d027c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8ef4-cdeb885799b9" class="">D) Stage 8 (post-C7 equilibrium) — strict definition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8bc2-de91e5881cb4" class="">C7 = stabilization and rebuild.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a94b-e8bcd74d1f92" class="">C8 = <strong>stable coherence equilibrium</strong> where stability persists <strong>without emergency tightening</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-aef8-ea92301f20d8" class="">Formally, C8 requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-891a-d1f019b447c4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coherence monotonicity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a12f-c06bbc46ad40" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dC}{dt}\approx 0 \quad \text{with } C \text{ high}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b489-f4c56614d707" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Low volatility sensitivity</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-82be-d994fa4c1b69" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial Stability}{\partial S} \ll 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-a8f8-cf3b5bf11ef5" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Buffers self-replenish</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b3ae-cfb5734328af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dBuf_{eff}}{dt}&gt;0 \quad \text{even when } U(t)=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-a113-f8bce0685b14" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Transparency not fear-based</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a486-cc47f0f1ff80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Trans_{eff} \uparrow \quad \text{while } G_{Inf} \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b508-d7e07447bbc6" class="">Interpretation: institutions don’t need constant coercion; the system is <strong>self-damped</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-bc2e-f6be6ef5abff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-9bd9-d996c8354864" class="">E) False C7 (the most important overlooked law)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-8bf5-de3e8ea9f6d6" class="">Many systems claim “stabilization” by increasing enforcement (D) <strong>without</strong> restoring trust, buffers, and transparency.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8735-e99d5cc8a812" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a5cd-cb7a8b94ec13" class=""><strong>False C7</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-bc71-c2a46a56f656" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{Law}\uparrow,\ D_{Mil}\uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9ad3-ce902a57f096" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Buf_{Fin}\downarrow,\ Trans_{Inf}\downarrow,\ Align\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-baa5-ee86c74b11af" class="">This creates a brittle regime:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-afdd-cd2bb037e42a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Stability_{surface}\uparrow \quad \text{while}\quad Stability_{core}\downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-87dc-f9a7e04516c3" class="">You can detect it by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9ccc-c75bed3d3710" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dD}{dt}&gt;0 \ \wedge\ \frac{dT}{dt}&lt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-8a32-eedaaff178b4" class="">That’s not C7. 
That’s <strong>compression before rupture</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-b5db-e4a2e0cfdf85"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-9ba9-e3ad43b68e4f" class="">If you say “Next” again, I’ll deliver one of these (you choose by number)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-bf81-da2453e3ca73" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Full 49×49 W-matrix template</strong> (which edges matter, which ones are dominant)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-9ce0-fea62424d60b" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>A set of 12 canonical attractors</strong> (C6 oscillatory, C7 rebuild, false C7 compression, C8 equilibrium, etc.)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-835c-c5ea86511ed5" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>AI as a parameter shock</strong>: how it increases Gain and decreases time-horizon unless counter-damped</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-aa08-ebdb151a0291" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>A rigorous “C7 trigger event” list</strong> mapped to these equations (no politics, 
just structural thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-b49d-cb8587a3f56c" class="">Reply with <strong>1/2/3/4</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-a2b2-efc808d4753a" class="">Choose.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-a26c-fc207100cf13" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Build 3-level micro (cell) → macro (civilization) resonance model</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-9855-c9f7ebb75082" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Map electromagnetic infrastructure into stage model</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a113-fa4fafb25c5f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Build a collapse vs stabilization phase diagram</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-b8fd-de50a93634a1" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Model probability distribution of C6→C7 transitions historically (Rome, Tokugawa, UK, post-war Germany, Singapore, etc.)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-ad7e-e4ee3d43fadf" class="">Next options:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b685-e94526a81feb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-9412-c6b0f5241ab9" class="">When elite survival becomes system-dependent again.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f0-9370-c541c535da10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Risk_{elite} \approx Risk_{mass}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-a308-c05601a0425d" class="">Crisis + elite incentive flip:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-94ed-d1b4cb406b84" class="">Not crisis alone.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-82fe-ca881c5a0b5b" class="">IV. Structural Trigger for True C7 Transition</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-b3fb-d0e07b2f58a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-afef-ebee0060f3d0" class="">System chooses oscillation instead of stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-8fc3-e1719417d018" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Short\_term\_Utility_{elite} &gt; Long\_term\_Stability</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-b02e-fc405ac72d49" class="">Mathematically:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-ae7c-f047f059c18e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Short-term growth sacrifice</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a50e-ef5246d1ca7d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Buffer rebuild (time costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9d0f-c8ae2f665cb9" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Transparency increase (elite costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-8f88-fb602a1a4fd5" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Gain reduction (politically costly)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-b955-d7490ac8936b" class="">Because C7 requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-901d-efdb12aea0a0" class="">III. Why Most C6 Systems Never Reach C7</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-8bb4-eb7f2858ba20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-96b8-cecc2da834c9" class="">C7 reduces recovery slope.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-9e61-f0db71cecfad" class="">→ systemic fatigue (late C6).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-9143-c37f97915bff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d Recovery\_time}{d Shock\_count} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9d9b-e07b66967418" class="">If recovery time lengthens over successive shocks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-bc7c-f371acc55acd" class="">Time to recover from moderate shock.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-b956-c662ac474ae2" class="">Detector 7: Shock Recovery Time</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-958f-d7a50527fbbe"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a702-dce052e286d1" class="">→ C6 drift.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8e1c-c53923794ced" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Credential\_Growth &gt; Competence\_Growth</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-ae93-d06b3b24c594" class="">If competence density declines while credential inflation rises:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8633-edbd44fd196a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">System thinking capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a777-d8a93055779e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Engineering repair skill</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8c44-ed9d5393b1de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tool literacy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-8740-ed3408dfc585" class="">Measure not degrees but:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-aa49-d0a428e3176c" class="">Detector 6: Youth Competence Density</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-9718-cdf795a9e9e1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-befd-c9f01f83ae43" class="">C7 reduces latency dramatically.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b79a-f72a0283c1f7" class="">→ C6 denial loop.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-a4ff-d62bfb4a8fc5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Latency_{acknowledge} \gg Latency_{failure}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-97cc-f00b94e35ddb" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9bbb-da8b81cabe1e" class="">Problem detection → admission → correction?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-813c-fe1f99aaa3fd" class="">How long between:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-a3ec-e8246a5306d8" class="">Detector 5: Information Latency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-9fba-d81e0e28e689"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-81bb-cdc12f3d4665" class="">C7 must raise redundancy before raising rhetoric.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-9676-d79f5da99006" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
En:Red \downarrow \Rightarrow Fragility \uparrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-a3ab-d91ed1160c1c" class="">If shock exposure high and redundancy low:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a8b0-c16d50c6ddaa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Import dependency ratio</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9d12-e3955450bc04" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Water storage</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-82c8-effba5e192ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Fuel reserves</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-b2b3-eebf8d21a5be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Grid redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8c4c-e7fee991c9f5" class="">Check:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-ae95-cebf22459ebd" class="">Detector 4: Energy Buffer Integrity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-86d4-da4e52487a35"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-b813-f8fc3364f182" class="">Enforcement boring, predictable, impartial.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-b9b7-c16706e97580" class="">True C7:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-a0ab-d6b5762dfd8a" class="">False C7 signal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8631-e64e68388478" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Law:D_{surface} \neq Law:Align_{real}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-a3ef-d3000f05bdac" class="">If petty rules enforced inconsistently but high-level actors exempt:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-8f0a-d622709dd210" class="">Observe small infractions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b313-d5becff3eac8" class="">Detector 3: Enforcement Consistency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-b71c-d12cf1e6e93c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b9f7-d3e39274d094" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Shared\_downside &gt; Extracted\_upside</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9de5-e6e31786c50b" class="">C7 requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-8b5d-c82c9e0cdb1c" class="">→ C6 attractor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-a7bd-e92d4a26a5df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Align_{elite-mass} \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-8479-feea6ce1eee4" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-b474-fee626e61fed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Gains privatized upward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-a78a-fe2c81af4cef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Losses socialized downward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-aa4c-cd853fa29df6" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-b5f7-f866af572928" class="">When system fails, do elites absorb cost?</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b0ae-eccbfbdc302e" class="">Ask:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-b5b4-d42467d9ab35" class="">Detector 2: Elite–Mass Risk Alignment</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8867-c1cfa7746745"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-83d3-e28ea6b2c353" class="">→ C7 stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-b2a9-fece53748aa3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maintenance \ge Expansion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8670-ec7bcfe34465" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8e6b-cbae20bfc402" class="">→ C6 trajectory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-a89f-ea20157900f0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Maintenance &lt; Visible\_Expansion</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-beb4-d2245bb847b6" class="">If:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a3d8-e953db5e14a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Or patched reactively after failure?</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-897c-d08ba3b3b420" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Are roads, grids, ports quietly maintained?</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b046-cf32016db6bb" class="">Observe:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-ac58-cf58c4d25c73" class="">Detector 1: Maintenance Ratio</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b040-e5e58e281fdc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-821e-df20675a1b52" class="">You detect stage by structural signals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-8628-d3eee7d10e23" class="">You don’t need statistics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-b5f3-cd2e6094226f" class="">II. Attractor Detector (Field Classification Without Numbers)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-9713-cc646bc33624"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b776-e8e19a7cf637" class="">It is <strong>high damping + high transparency + high buffer</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-81c4-c7b59d93d033" class="">C7 is not repression.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b080-e1858fde06b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{eff} + Buf_{eff} + Align_{eff} &gt; Gain_{eff}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b802-d990f5eed2c0" class="">Core invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-8a10-cf7f725b13e6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-ab38-ca7d2b483182" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-9134-f5f067102bb0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-b0e3-cea85a2ef0b0" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-8fab-d4fad11b4c3a" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-a0af-fb83e21eba4e" class="">Dominant edges invert:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-9216-fd9375e8bb9b" class="">Stage C7 (Rebuild / Damped Coherence)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-9bdd-dc75ff15b514"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a58b-d9560a68a655" class="">If En:Red ignored → physical instability remains latent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-91f7-f563e12301dc" class="">If Fin:Hor stays short-term → speculative relapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-b067-ded90a89da90" class="">If only Law:D rises but Trans stays low → false stabilization.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-bf70-db8bbfdddd06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d En:Red}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-9f01-fc5e9a423bce" class="">\frac{d Fin:Hor}{dt} &gt; 0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8d1c-e9f1154b48d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d Law:Trans}{dt} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b79e-ff5a6a389c0a" class="">Three subsystems must rotate simultaneously:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-b478-e651e394e931" class="">Rare alignment window:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-a0af-de0e6dac1ed8" class="">C6 → C7 Pivot Condition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b281-d86d73e36f27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-b0b3-d28bd53acf0d" class="">C6 is not collapse. It is <strong>over-amplified coupling</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-b387-feb4e4fcc557" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maintenance deferred</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bce1-c8b5d62e4487" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Buffers thin quietly</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-90c1-ed65bdd6cb22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforcement becomes selective</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a14e-ce88e9ab4308" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Narrative outruns production</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-a705-fc7356dc7b78" class="">Symptoms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a238-fd38e1e6fffd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gain_{cross-domain} &gt; Damping_{intra-domain}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a4ab-cdecdee8998c" class="">Hidden invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-b7dd-d37144411e36" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-80f5-cbcc473ba9c4" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-abb3-eedcac3d05f4" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-b2e5-c2e703732ec9" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-9a76-d423b4b19186" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-93f7-e40fd34b5f53" class="">High load edges:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-bc8e-f631d948b6bb" class="">Dominant edges flip sign weight.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-87a3-faf2faf6f518" class="">Stage C6 (Fragmentation / Amplification Phase)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-983e-e456ac7c65f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b4fb-ef975aa7be21" class="">System is self-correcting.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-9d95-c195765abdee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Repair_{rate} \ge Decay_{rate}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-9df5-c4dc845db1f7" class="">Invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-acb9-c24f1f094a5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a358-d49cfcc937e9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-a45f-e0549817353e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-8565-c23a565b8188" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-ae12-fbeddd5fa7eb" class="">Key edges:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-9376-c685ee942a05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Maintenance ≥ expansion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-832c-f4f84b358da6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Moderate gain, 
strong damping</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-828a-c45880b6391e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Strong horizontal coupling within domains</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-88bf-f46054986460" class=""><strong>Dominant structure:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-b445-e82073fea6f1" class="">Stage C5 (Pre-fragmentation high coherence)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-8452-e381e7cf8e4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-8ef6-d740b208ad4c" class="">W edges determine direction of evolution.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-9660-ee8cb7e3a610" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t+1)=x(t)+W\phi(x-\theta)+u-\Lambda h+s</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-a9e2-efaeba7808d7" class="">Recall:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a0ec-d05225d5ea60" class="">(Which edges dominate in C5 → C6 → C7 transitions)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-aa15-e7ac98f06441" class="">I. Stage-Specific W Edge Priorities</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-a99e-d25a81a6b3f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a608-f142bf9dc875" class="">No narrative. Pure systems mapping.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-923c-c383f44402a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>(ii) Attractor detector (field classification without numeric data)</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-95ca-c8e56043ac1a" class="">Next = <strong>(i) Stage-specific W-edge priorities</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-b42b-f359d9ce6a9f" class="">Good. We go structural and precise.</p></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
